# Ethiopian Synaxarium: Tahschasch I (Les mois de Taḥschasch, Ṭer et Yakâtit : IV. Mois de Taḥschasch, I)

**Author**: Anonymous (Ethiopian Synaxarion)  
**Series**: PO Tome 15  
**Columns**: pp. 548–799  

[p. 548] {#po.15.p548}
አመ ፣ ጸለታኅሣሥ ።
THE BOOK OF THE SYNAXARY THE 1st TAHSCHASCH
፪. -፦ ፻0606.0810. ህጀ:በ1(1ኛ6. |
|. - Primitive recension. 1. Death of Peter of Edessa, bishop of Gaza.
"በስመ ፡ አብ ፣ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ፡፡ ወርኃ ፡ ታኅሣሥ ፡ ቡሩክ ፣ መዓልቱ ፡ ፪ሰዓት ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ይትዌሰክ ።
* In the name of the Father, of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. Blessed month of Tâhschâsch, whose first day has nine hours. Then the days increase.


[p. 550] {#po.15.p550}
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አብ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፣ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፣ ጋዛ። ፦ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ሮሓ ፡ እምዘመደ ፣፤ ክቡራን ። ወወሀ ብዖ ፡ አበዊሁ ፣፤ ለቴዎዶስዮስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚሞ ፡ መኩ›ንነ ፡፣ ወሜሞ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መኩንንነ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይሜንን ፡ ሚመተ ፡ ወክብረ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይትጋደል ፣ ወይጸመድ ፡ በአ ምልኮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በውስተ ፡ ጽርሐ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሀሎ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ እምነ፣ ሥጋ ሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ እለ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ፋርስ ፣ ወኮነ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ በውእ ቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፳8ዓመት ።
1. - On this day died the holy Father Peter (Pêtros), bishop of the city of Gaza (Gâzà). 2. - This saint was originally from the city of Edessa (Rohä), from an illustrious family. His parents gave him to King Theodosius (Téwodosyos), in order that he might establish him as judge. He himself established him as judge. He despised dignities and honors. He showed himself pious and devout in the worship of the Lord, in the king's palace. There was with him one of the bodies of the holy martyrs who had been brought from the land of Persia (Fârès). He was, at that time, twenty years old.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምነ ፡ አድባራት ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ ዜና ፡ ቅድስናሁ ፡ ነሥእዎ ፡ ዘእንበ ለ፣ ፈቃዱ፣ ወሜምዎ ፡ ኤዲስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ጋዛ ፣ ወበኩሉ ፡ ደወላ ፡ ወተ
3. - Then he left and became a monk in one of the monasteries. He practiced great asceticism. When the fame of his holiness was learned, he was taken against his will and he was established as bishop over the city of Gaza (Gâzä) and


[p. 551] {#po.15.p551}
ብህለ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሠርዓ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቀሩርባን ፣ ውሕዘ፣ ደም ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ አምጐፉርባን ፡ እስከ ፡ መልአ ፡ ጻሕለ ፡=፡ ወሶበ ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ያፅቆብ ፡ ዘግሙድ ፡ነሥኦ ፡በኀቤሁ፣ ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እምነ ፡ አድባራት ፡፣ ዘሀገረ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡-መዋዕለ ፡= -፦ ወእንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይሠርዕ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ፉርባን ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሕዝ ብ፣-እለ።፣ ይቀውሙ ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ " በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ፣ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ይጌሥጾሙ ።፡፡ ወርእዮ ፣ ለመልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አሐዞ ፡ እማእከሉሙ ፡ ወፈቆቅደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውግሮ ፡ እስከ ፡ መትሕት ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አክበሮሙ ፡ ወኃፈ
over all its surroundings. It is said about him that, when he himself celebrated mass formerly, abundant blood flowed from the Eucharist, to the point of filling the paten. 4. - When the body of Saint James (Yä'qob), the Intercis, had been brought, he took it with him. He remained in one of the monasteries of the city of Jerusalem (lyarousälêm), for a few days. 5. - While he himself was celebrating mass one day, there were assistants who were talking * among themselves at the moment of the mass, without Saint Peter reprimanding them. He saw an angel of the Lord who seized him in their midst and wanted to throw him down, because he himself was honoring them by his silence and was ashamed to reprimand them.
ረ ፡ከመ ፡ ይገሥጾሙ ፡፡ -፦ ወዕበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለመርቅያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡
6. - When the days of Marcian (Marqyânos), the impious, were finished, Saint Peter returned to the land of Palestine (Felest'êm) and strengthened


[p. 552] {#po.15.p552}
ጴጥሮስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምድረ ፣ ፍልስጥኤም ፡ ወአጽንዓ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡፡ ወተጋብአ ፡ ም ስለ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ግብጻዊ ፡ ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ቬኖን ፣ ንጉ ሥ ፡ ወፈተወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአዮ ፡ ወኢፈቀደ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ ኮነ ፡ ይጐ ይይ ፡ እምክብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኀላፊ ።፡ ወእምድኅረ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጽንፈ ፣
the church. He made company with Saint Abba Isaiah ('Isäyyäs), the Egyptian. His fame was learned by Emperor Zeno (Zênon). The emperor desired to see him, but Saint Peter did not want that. Indeed, he himself was fleeing the honors of this passing world. Then he went to the border of the land of Gâwr and remained there.
ብሔረ ፡ጋውር ፡ወነበረ ፡በህየ = ፦ ወበጽሐ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድር ያ ፡= ወሠርዐ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፣ ዮርባን ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ጴጥ ሮስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡፣ ጸውዐከ ፣፤ ከመ ፡ ትምጻእ ፡ ኀ ቤነ = ወእምይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አእመረ ፡፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ሞቱ ፣ ወጸውዖሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጽንዑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ቅድስት =፡ወ እምዝ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወመጠወ ፡ነፍሶ ፡፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡=፡
7. - The feast of Saint Peter, patriarch of the city of Alexandria (‘Eskendryâ), arrived. He celebrated mass on the day of his feast. Saint Peter appeared to him and said to him: "Behold, Our Lord Christ has called you, in order to come toward us." From that day on Saint Peter knew the time of his death. He called the people and ordered them to be firm in the right and holy faith. Then he stretched out his hands and rendered his soul. May his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen.


[p. 553] {#po.15.p553}
-፦ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ክዕበ ፣ አዕረፈ ፣ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሊቀ፣ ጳጳሳት ፣ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወውእቱ ፣ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ግዓ ፡=፡ -፦ ዝንቱ ፡ አ”ብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፣ እምሰብአ ፣፡ ሀገረ ፣ ገምኑዲ ፣ ወበመዋዕ ሊሁ ፡ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ተሐንጸት ፣ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኖሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ሰርጊዮስ ፡ ወ ባኮስ ፡ ወቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኖሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፣ አባ፣ ቂሪ፣ ወዮሐንስ። በቅጽረ ፡ ጉባኤ ፤ በምስር ፍ ሐነጾሙ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ብእሲ ፣ ፈራሄ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ግብጻዊ ፡ያዕቆባዊ ፡ ዘስሙ ፣ እንድርያስ ፡ወኮነ፣ ጸሓፌ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ አብድል ፡ አዚዝ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ምርዋን ፡=
2. Death of Abba John, patriarch of Alexandria. 1. - On this day also died the holy Father Abba John (Yohannès), patriarch of the city of Alexandria ('Eskendryä). He is the fortieth in the number of the patriarchs. 2. - This Father * was a priest. He was originally from the people of the city of Gamnoudi. In the days of this Father were built the church of the saints Sergius (Sargiyos) and Bacchus (Bâkos) and the church of the saints Abba Cyrus (Qiri) and John (Yohannès) at Qesra-Goubä'ë, in Egypt. They were built by a man fearing the Lord, an Egyptian, a Jacobite, called Andrew ('Endryâs). He was the secretary of King 'Abdel-'Aziz, son of Merwân. It is this Father Abba John who


[p. 554] {#po.15.p554}
ወዝንቱ ፦ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ዘቅዱስ ፡ ማር ቆስ ፣ ወንጌላዊ ፡፣ በሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ፣ እንተ ፣ ዕውቅት ፡ በቃምስ ፡ በመጠነ ፡ ይዓ መት ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ አብድል ።፡
built the church of Saint Mark (Mârqos), the evangelist, in the city of Alexandria, which was famous for the archpriest who administered it for three years, in the days of King 'Abdel.
ወኮነ ፤፣ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ረኃብ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ መጠነ ፡ የዓመት ፡=፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይሔሊ ፡ ለነዳያን ፣ ወለምስኪናን ፣፤ ወለፅኑሳን ፣ ወይሁቦሙ ፡ ብሩረ ፤ ወኅብስ ተ ፡ ፪ጊዜ ፡ ለለ ፡ ሰሙን ፡፡ ወኮነ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ምሕረት ፡ ወምጽዋት ፡ ወገቢረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡=፡ ፦፦ ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ይዚድ ፡ ወነግሠ ፡፣ ህየንቴሁ ፡ መርዋን ፡= ወነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፣ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፣ ዓመተ ፡ወአዕረፈ፣ በሰላም ፡= እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ፡፡
3. - There was in the days of this Father a great famine for three years. He thought of the poor, the unfortunate, and the destitute. He gave them money and bread, twice each week. He was great in mercy, almsgiving, and beneficence. 4. - In his days died King Yezid. Marwân reigned in his place. This Father sat on the seat of Mark, the evangelist, for nine years. He died in peace. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer. Amen.


[p. 555] {#po.15.p555}

3. Death of Abba Athanasius, patriarch of Alexandria. On this day also died the holy Father Abba Athanasius ('Atnäsyos), patriarch of the city of Alexandria ('Eskendryä), known by the name of Paul (Päwlos), son of the priest Makârem, son of Kalil. He is the seventy-sixth in the number of the patriarchs. May his prayer and his blessing be with us. * Amen. II. - Revision or Vulgate. 4. Salâm to Peter of Edessa, bishop of Gaza. Greeting to Peter (Pêtros), who grew in the enclosure of the priesthood And was planted in the house of God. While he was celebrating mass, it is said about him

That at the moment of the breaking sprang from the source of the bread The blood of the divinity, abundant to the point of filling the paten. 5. Salâm to John, patriarch of Alexandria. Greeting to John (Yohannès), patriarch in his office And righteous in his faith. Under the name of Mark (Märgos), he built a church during his administration. The day he emigrated from this world, His death coincided with that of the patriarch Athanasius ('Atnätêwos). 6. Memory of Peter, of James, the knight, of Elijah, the monk, and of Bathsheba, mother of Solomon.

2. - This Elijah, the zealot, was of the stock of Levi (Lëwi). The name of his father was '"yâschënyou; the name of his mother was Tonâ. It is said about him that, the day he was born, his father saw two luminous men. They prostrated themselves before him and wrapped him in swaddling clothes of fire, in place of the swaddling clothes with which small children are wrapped. When he was born, his father came to Jerusalem (lyarousâlêm). He told this to the priests. The priests said this to each other about him: "His dwelling will be in the light. He will be sharp by his word. He will torment Israel by the sword and by fire." 3. - The account of the miracles that he performed in the days of Ahab ('Ak 'âb), king of Israel, and of his wife Jezebel ('Élzâbël), and the account of his ascent to heaven are found written on the 7th of Ter.

John 'Abougalamsis called Elijah and Enoch the two olive trees and the two lamps that are before the Lord. When the Beast will have conquered them, will have killed them, and

will have thrown their corpse, which will remain lying for three days, then the Spirit of the Lord will come and enter into them. They will resurrect alive, * so that fear will come upon every being who will see them. When the voice of God will call them, they will ascend to heaven on a cloud. Then there will be a great earthquake and seven thousand souls will die. May their blessing be with our king 'lyâsou and with his dear Za-Manfas-Qedous for the ages of ages.

Greeting to you, Elijah, who ascended to the heavens to rest, By shaking far from you the life of this world. Because you admonished Ahab in his palace regarding his great sin, And because you demolished the temples and destroyed the statues of the gods, How much in your glory, Elijah, you are glorified!

8. Death of Naboth, the Jezreelite. 1. - On this day also died Naboth, the Jezreelite (Näboutê 'Eyzrä'éläwi). 2. - This Naboth had a vineyard near the barn of Ahab ('Ak'ab), king of Samaria (Samäryä). Ahab said to Naboth: "Give me your vineyard, so that it may become a garden for me, for it is near my house. I will give you the gold that will represent the price of your vineyard." Naboth said to Ahab: "The Lord keep me from giving you the inheritance of my fathers!" Ahab was sad. He went to lie down on his bed. He veiled his face and did not eat food.
-፦- ወቦአት ፡ ኤልዛቤል ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፣ ምንተ ፡ ያቴክዘከ ፡ዘኢት በልዕ ፡ እክለ ፡ ወይቤላ ፣ እስመ ፡ ተናገርክዎ ፡ ለናቡቴ ፡ ወእቤሎ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ ዓፀደ ፣ ወ ይንከ ፡ በሜጥ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ኢይሁበከ ፡ ርስተ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ኤልዛቤል ፡ አንተ ኑ ከመዝ ፡ ትሬሲ ፡ ንጉሦሙ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ወብ”ላፅ ፡ ወአነ ፣ እሁበከ ፣ ዓፀደ ፡ ወይኑ” ለናቡቴ ፡ ኢይዝራኤላዊ፣ ወጸሐፈት ፡ በስመ ፡ አክአብ ፡ ወሐተመት ፣
3. - Jezebel ('Élzäbël), his wife, entered to him. She said to him: "What matter saddens you, you who do not eat food?" He said to her: "It is that I spoke to Naboth and said to him: 'Give me your vineyard at the price it is worth.' He said to me: 'I will not give you the inheritance of my father.'" Jezebel said to him: "Will you yourself do thus, king of Israel (‘Esrä'êl)? Arise and * eat. I myself will give you the vineyard of Naboth, the Jezreelite." She wrote a letter in the name of Ahab and sealed it with the seal of Ahab. She sent this


[p. 560] {#po.15.p560}
በማኅተሙ ፡ ወፈነወታ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሊቃናተ ፡ህሀገር ፡ አለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ናቡቴ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ጹሙ ፡ ጸመ ፡ ወአንብርዎ ፡ ለናቡቴ ፡ ቅድ መ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡ ወዓቅሙ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ፪ዕደወ ፣ ውሎደ ፡ ዓመፃ ፡ እለ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ባረክሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ወለንጉሥ' ፡ ወአውፅእዖ ፡ አፍኣ ፡ ወውግርዎ ፡ ይሙት ፡፡ ፦ ወገብሩ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ለአከት ፣ ኤልዛቤል ፡ ወሰበኩ፣0፣ ጾመ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ለናቡቴ ፡ ቅድመ ፡፣ ሕዝብ ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ፪ዕደው፣ ውሎደ ፡ ዓመፃ፣ወ ኮኑ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ላፅሌሁ ፡ ወይቤልዎዖቻ ፡ ባረካሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወለንጉሥ ፡ ወእምዝ ፣ አውፅእዎ ፡ አፍኣ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወወገርዎ ፣ ወሞተ ፡=፡ በረከቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
letter to the elders of the city who dwelt with Naboth. Here is what the terms of the letter said: "Proclaim a fast. Place Naboth before the people. Set against him two men, sons of iniquity, who will say: 'You have cursed, Naboth, the Lord and the king.' Take him out of the city and stone him, (so) that he may die." 4. - The men of the city did as Jezebel had commanded. They proclaimed a fast. They placed Naboth before the people. Two men, sons of iniquity, entered. They testified against him and said to him: "You have cursed the Lord and the king." Then they took him out of the city and stoned him. He died. May his blessing be with our king Iyasu for ever and ever.
ሰላም ፡ ለናቡቴ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ዓፀዱ ። ለንጉሥ ፡ አክአብ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ተወርወት ፣ ፈቃዱ ። እስከ ፡ አቅተሎ ፡ በግፍዕ ፡ እስመ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ልማዱ ። አጽርዓት ፡ ግፍዓ ፣ ደሙ ፡ በፍትሐ ፡ፈጣሪ ፡ ። መንግሥተ ፡ ወምልክና ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ውሉዱ ፡፡
Salutation to Naboth, who had love for his only enclosure. When the will of King Ahab rose up against him, to the point of having him killed unjustly, for such was the custom of Ahab, the injustice of the shedding of Naboth's blood put an end, by the decision of the unique Creator, to the kingship and dominion of Ahab over all the sons of Israel.


[p. 561] {#po.15.p561}
ሰላም ፡ ለኤልያስ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ዘለጐመ ፳፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየሀብ ፡ ዝናመ ።፡=፡ በሠረገላ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ንዒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትባርኪ ፡ ዓለመ ፡=፡ ሽ ማርያም ፡ ዘኮንኪ ፡ ለብኡላን ፡ ሰላመ ፡= አለ ፡ ክሀላ ፡ ከናፍርኪ ፡ አፈ ፡ ነደ ፡ እሳት ፡ ስዒመ።
9. Salam to the prophet Elijah. Salutation to Elijah (Elyas), who restrained the heaven, so that it would not give rain. Come on a chariot of light, in order to bless the world, Mary (Maryam), (you) who are peace for the generous. Your lips, which were able to kiss the mouth of the Flame of fire, tell me that your canticles are sweeter than honey.

### THE 2 TAHSAS
ይቤላኒ ፣ ማኅሌትኪ ፡ እመዓር ፡ ጥፅመ ፡=፡ አመ ፡፪ለታኅሣሥ ። ፪. -፦፦ ፲፻6666፲0810፲3. ዐሀ፲1:011176.
I. - Primitive recension. 1. Commemoration of the Seven thousand thirty-three martyrs of Antioch, servants
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
of Basilides. In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.


[p. 562] {#po.15.p562}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፣ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ድ፻ወየ፻ነፍስ =፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ አግብርቲሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ፋሲለደስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ወአግብርተ፣፡አዝማዲሁ ፡=፡ በእዴሁ ፣ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ።፡ ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከተ ፡ ደሞሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡= -፦ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሆር ፡ መነኮስ ፡=፡ -፦ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ብህጋውራ ፡ አምላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ 5፡ ወኮነ ፡ መነኮሰ ፡ ኅሩየ ፣ ወተለዓለ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እምነ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ በአምልኮቱ ፡ ወተጋድሎቱ ፡
On this day are the holy martyrs: the seven thousand thirty-three persons. These are the servants of Saint Basilides (Fasiladas), martyr, and the servants of his parents. They suffered martyrdom by order of Diocletian (Diyogletyanos), the impious, in the city of Antioch (Ansokiya). May their prayer and the blessing of their blood be with us. Amen. 2. Death of Abba Hor, the anchorite. 1. - On this day also died the holy Father Abba Hor, monk. 2. - This saint was descended from people of the city of Behgawra, in Upper Egypt (La'lay-Gebs). He was an elite monk. He rose above many saints by his piety and his asceticism.


[p. 563] {#po.15.p563}
-፦ ወኮነ ፡ ያፈቅር ፡ ይንበር ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ፣ ይጸመድ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ወቀንአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰይጣን ፣ ጸላኢ ፡ ወአስተርአዮ፣ ሎተ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ገዳምሰ ፣ አንተ ፣ ትመውአኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፣ በኀቤሁ ፣ ሰብእ ፡ አላ ፡ ለአመ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ጽኑዓ ፡ ወኃያለ ፡ ነዓ ፣ ኀበ ፣ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድ ርያ 5፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ሆር ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይቀድኅ ፡ ማየ ፡ ለሙቁሓን ፡ ወለነዳያን ፡፡
3. - He loved to dwell alone. He departed to the desert. He continued to be devout for many years. Satan (Saytan), the enemy, was jealous of him. He appeared to him openly and said to him: "In the desert you yourself defeat me, for there are no people near you. But if you are strong and valiant, come to the city of Alexandria (Eskendrya)." When Saint Abba Hor had heard this, he arose and went to the city of Alexandria. He drew water for the prisoners and for the poor.
ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ የኃልፉ ፡ ወይረውፁ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወ፳እምኔሆሙ ፡ ረገጾ ፡ ለ8ሕፃን ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡፡ ወቦአ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰብእ ፤ ወኮኑ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘቀተሎ ፣ ለዝንቱ ፡ ሕየን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፣ መነ ኮ"ስ = ወመጽአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሆር ፣ ወሐቀፎ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ጸለየ ፡በልቡ ፥፡ ወአስተብቀሩዓ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወዓተበ ፣ ዲቤሁ ፣ በትእምርተ ፡
4. - At that moment, horses passed by and ran in the city. One of them kicked a child. This child died immediately. Satan entered into some people. They said: "No one other than this old monk killed this child." * Saint Abba Hor came. He embraced this child, prayed in his heart, besought Our Lord Christ, and made over him the sign


[p. 564] {#po.15.p564}
መስቀል ፡ ወገብአት ፡፣ ነፍሱ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፣ ወተንሥአ ፡ ሕያወ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡=
of the cross. The soul returned to this child. He rose up alive. Abba Hor gave him to his father.
ወጐየ፡አፍኣ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወኀሠሥዎ ፣ ወኢረከብቻ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብሩ፣፡ወ ነበረ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣፡ ዓመታተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ይትጋደል ፣፡ ወይጸመድ ፡ በገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡፡ -፦ ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ለብዙኃን ፡ቅዱሳን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዴውዕዎ፣ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡፡ ወአስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለውሉዱ ፣ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፣፡ ይጽንፁ፣ው ስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወበገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይፈልስ ፣ ኀበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣፡ ወሐዘኑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ሐመ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወመጠወ፣0፣ ነፍሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡፡ ፡፡
5. - He fled out of the city. They searched for him, but did not find him. He went to his monastery. He dwelt there for many years, practicing asceticism and being devout in the accomplishment of holiness. 6. - When his death approached, he saw many saints who were calling him. He rejoiced extremely. He gathered his spiritual sons. He commanded them to be firm in the monastic way and in the accomplishment of holiness. He explained to them that he himself was going to emigrate to Our Lord Christ. They were extremely sad. Then he was sick a little. He rendered his soul into the hand of the Lord. May his prayer and his blessing be with us forever. Amen.


[p. 565] {#po.15.p565}

II. - Revision or Vulgate. 3. Salam to Abba Hor, the anchorite. Salutation to Hor, tested in the forge of pain, to the point of rising strong above many. When they had risen against him, on the counsel of Satan (Saytan), by the power of his prayer, he resurrected the dead child, so that the senseless recognized that he had not killed him. 4. The Three Children in the furnace. 1. - On this day the Lord performed a wonder for the Three Children. 2. - These are Ananias (Ananya), Azarias (Azarya), and Misael (Misa'el), sons of Joachim (Iyo'aqem), king of Judah (Yehuda), whom Nebuchadnezzar (Nabukadanador) had taken into captivity with their father, had raised in his palace, and had set over the provinces of Babylonia (Babilon).


[p. 566] {#po.15.p566}
፦ ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ናቡከደነጾር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ መኳን ንቲሁ ፡ ወሰብአ ፣ መንግሥቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስግዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወለአሉሰ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሶበ ፡ አ ስተዋደይዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፣ አበዩ ፡ ሰጊደ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይደይዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፣ እቶነ ፡ እሳት ፣ ዘአንደድዎ ፡ ምስብዒተ ።
3. - Then when Nebuchadnezzar had made a golden statue, he ordered his prefects and the people of his kingdom to worship it. When these saints were accused of refusing to worship the statue, he ordered them to be thrown into a furnace of fire, which they would light sevenfold.
፦ ወጸለዩ ፡ ነዋኃ ፣ ማእከለ ፡ እቶን ፡ ሰፊሖሙ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወረ ደ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወረሰዮ ፡ ለእሳት ፣ ከመ ፡ ነፋስ ፣፤ ቄሪር ፣ ወአውፅኦ ሙ ፡እማእአከለ ፡እቶን ፣ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘለከፎሙ ፣፤ ወኢያውዓየ ፡ ስዕርተ ፡ ርአሶዕሙ ፣ ወሶ በ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ናቡከደነጾር ፡ ሰገደ ፡ ወተጋነየ ፡፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሎሙኒ ፡ አክበሮሙ ፡ ፈድፋደ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀሮ ፣፤ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም =
4. - They prayed at length in the midst of the furnace, stretching out their hands. Then an angel of the Lord descended. He rendered the fire like a cool wind. He brought them out from the midst of the furnace. Nothing had touched them, nor had it burned the hair of their head. When Nebuchadnezzar saw this, he worshipped and humbly besought the Lord. Them too, he honored extremely. May the Lord have mercy, through their prayer, on our king Iyasu for ever and ever.
ሰላም ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ኃሣሥያነ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ በኅዳጥ ። ሲድራቅ ፡ ወሚሳቅ ፣ ወአብ"”ድናጎ ፡ ሰየጥ ።

In the heat of the furnace, in the pitch and in the asphalt, the powerful Lord consumed those who were outside, while you he protected inside. 5. Commemoration of 'Absetfen, of 'Anbas, and of Nathanael, the wonder-worker. On this day also is the death of 'Absetfen, martyr, of 'Anbas, who was a native of Arabia (Arab), and of Nathanael (Natna'el), monk, author of wonders. May the Lord have mercy, through their prayer, on our king Iyasu for ever and ever. 6. Salam to 'Awkateyos

heard him say thus: "I give thanks to the Lord, for from my members has been cut that which was useless." 7. Commemoration of 'Awkyanos. On this day also is Saint 'Awkyanos, who wrote his course and kept his faith. They cut off his testicles. He gave thanks to the Lord. May the Lord have mercy, through his prayer, on his dear Se'la-Syon for ever and ever.

### THE 3 TAHSAS

I. - Primitive recension. 1. Presentation of the Holy Virgin. In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. 1. - On this day took place the Presentation of Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary (Maryam), generator of God, in the temple, in Jerusalem (Iyarusalim), when she was three years old.


[p. 570] {#po.15.p570}

2. - Indeed, she herself had been vowed to the Lord. Her mother Anne (Hanna), as she found herself without child and found herself far from the women who were in the house of the Lord, had become extremely sad, as well as the illustrious old man * Joachim (Iyo'aqem), her husband. The Lord heard their groans. The mother of the Holy Virgin, Anne, made the following vow to the Lord: she said that the fruit that he would give her she would consecrate to the Lord. When she had given birth to Our Lady, the pure one, she raised her for three years in her house. Then she led her, so that she might dwell, with the virgins, in the temple of the Lord. Mary dwelt in the temple for twelve years, while she received her food from the hand of the angels, until the time when Our Lord Christ came into the world. He became incarnate from her. It is she whom he chose from among all women.

3. - When twelve years were completed for her in the temple, the priests took counsel among themselves, in order to give her into the care of someone who would protect her, for she had been vowed to the Lord. It did not seem fitting to them to leave her in the temple, so that there might not happen to her what happens to women. Then protectors were appointed over her, while waiting for her to be betrothed to him who would be worthy to see her.

4. - Zacharias (Zakaryas), the prince of the priests, answered and said: "Bring her, so that we may be certain of what is in her heart." She was called. She arrived and stood before the priests. Zacharias said to her: "Know, O Mary, that you yourself have grown and have become accomplished as a woman. Do you wish to marry? We must pronounce upon you the curse that is written in the Law, so that you yourself do not approach the door of the temple during the days when there happens to you what happens to women." Mary answered and said to them: "Behold, I am the servant of the Lord before you. I have no longer a father, nor a mother. You replace for me my father and my mother with the Lord, whose name is holy and blessed. According as you yourselves know the order of the Lord, accomplish it upon me." 5. - The priests and the whole assembly of the Jews said to the priest Zacharias: "Enter into the temple, pray to the Lord, and what the Lord will reveal to you, do it upon Mary." The angel of the Lord appeared to him and said to him: "O Zacharias, go out and gather all the widowed men, whose wives are dead, of the house of David (Dawit), old men and young men. Take their staffs; write their names upon their staffs, by designating each by his name; gather all the staffs and introduce them into the temple. Pray to the Lord. Then go out and give to each his staff. He upon whose staff the Lord, our God, will manifest for you a sign, he will be worthy to receive * Mary and to protect her."

He found many men gathered there. 7. - The priest Zacharias took all their staffs and wrote their names upon them. The number of the staffs was 1,985. He prayed in the temple to the Lord. Likewise all the men who were standing outside the temple prayed also. When Zacharias had finished his prayer, he brought out from the temple the staffs and gave to each one of them his staff. When Joseph, the carpenter, had approached and had taken his staff, there appeared upon the staff a kind of white dove. It flew and stood at the top of his staff. When all the priests saw this, they were extremely astonished. They glorified the Lord much. Zacharias said to Joseph: "O

Joseph, take the Virgin of the Lord and keep her in your house, as the angel of the Lord has said." Joseph received * Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary. She dwelt at his house. The angel Gabriel (Gabr'el) announced to Mary that the incarnation of the Son of the Lord would be from her. May her great intercession, her prayer, and her blessing be with us. Amen. In order to turn away sadness for my soul. 3. Feast of the archangel Phanuel. On this day also is the feast of the glorious angel Phanuel (Fanu'el) archangel. May his intercession and the power of his help be with our king Iyasu for ever and ever. Amen. Salutation to your function, at the door of the Lord, in order that you may be the ascent of prayer and the expeller of the demon of slander. Phanuel, take me with the modulations of my canticle, when they will receive the gain of their talents, as remuneration, those who have not lent their gold with usury.

2. - The lot of this saint fell to preach in the city of Lydda (Leddä) and in the land of the Kurds ('Akrâd). When he entered the city of Lydda, many people of this city believed through the mediation of Peter, his brother. His disciple Philemon (Filmonä)


[p. 578] {#po.15.p578}
ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ፊልሞና ፡ ጥዑመ ፣ ቃል ፡ ወጠቢብ ፡ ወሠናይ ፡ አምልኮቱ ፡ ወአን ብቦቱ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕርግ ፣ መልዕልተ ፡ መዓርግ ፡ ወያንብ ብ ፡ በጥዑም ፡ ዜማ ፡ ወዐርገ ፣ ወአንበበ ፡ በቃል ፡ ጥዑም ፡፡ -፦ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ገነውተ ፡፤ ጣዖታት ፡ በምጽአቱ ፡ ለሐዋርያ ፡ እንድርያ ስ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፣ ወነሥኡ ፡ ንዋየ ፣ ሐቅሎሙ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ከ መ፣ ይስምዕዎሙ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይረግሙ ፡ ለአማልክቲሆሙ = ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዕዎ ፡ ለፊልሞና ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያነብብ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አማልክቲሆሙ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፣ ወብሩር ፣ ግብረ ፡ እደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመ ፡ ሕያው ፡ አፈ ፡ ቦሙ ፣ ወኢይነቡ ።፡ አይነ፣፡ ቦሙ፡ ወ ኢይሬእዩ ።፡ እዝነ ፡ ቦሙ ፡፣ ወኢይሰምዑ ፡ አንፈ ፡፤ ቦሙ ፣፤ ወኢያጹንዉ ። እ ደ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢይገሱ ።፡ እግረ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢየሐውሩ ። ወኢይነቡ ፡ በጐራዒቶሙ ፣
was with him. This Philemon had a sweet voice. He was wise. His piety and his diction were beautiful. The apostle Andrew ordered him to go up to the ambo and read with a sweet harmony. He went up and read with a sweet voice. 3. - When the priests of the idols learned of the arrival of the apostle Andrew and Philemon, they rose up, took their weapons, and went to the church, in order to listen to them, desiring to know if they would curse their gods. When they heard Philemon speak and say: The gods of the peoples are of gold and silver, the work of the hands of men. They have a mouth, but they do not speak; they have eyes, but they do not see; they have ears, but they do not hear; they have a nose, but they do not smell; they have hands, but they do not feel; they have feet, but they do not walk. They do not speak with their throat, and there is no breath in their mouth.


[p. 579] {#po.15.p579}
ወአልቦ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ውስተ ፣ አፉሆሙ ፣ ከማሆሙ ፥ ለይኩኑ ፣ ኩሎ”ሙ ፦ እለ ፥፦ገ ብርዎሙ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ አለ ፡ ይትአመኑ ፡ ቦሙ ፡=፡ ወበእንተ ፦ሠናይ ፡ ቃሉ -፡'ወጥ ዕመቱ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ አልባቢሆሙ ፡ ወበኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ታ ሕተ ፦ እገሪሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡፡ ወ መሀሮሙ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ትምህርተ ፣ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡፣ ክርስ
May * all those who made them and all those who trust in them become like them. Because of his beautiful voice and the sweetness of his language, their hearts rejoiced. They entered the church, prostrated themselves at the feet of the holy apostle Andrew, and believed in Our Lord Christ. The apostle taught them the doctrine of the Church and baptized them with Christian baptism. Many people who worshipped idols believed with them.
ትና ፣ ወአምኑ ፡ ብዙኃን ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ እምእለ ፣ ያመልኩ ፡ ጣዖታተ = ፦ ወአምዝ ፣፡ ወፅአ ፤ እምኅቤሆሙ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አክራድ ፡ ወሀገ ረ ፤ አክሲስ ፣ ወሀገረ ፣ ሶንፋሪስ ፡ እምድኅረዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ምስለ ፣ በርተሎሜዎስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ጋዝሪኖስ ፡ ወዘኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ከልብ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ኀ በ ፣፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ወሶበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ዛቲ፣ሀ
4. - Then he left them. He went to the land of the Kurds, to the land of Scythia ('Aksis), and to the land of Sonfäâris. Then he went with Bartholomew (Bartalomëwos) to the land of Gâzrinos. Incidents occurred to them with the Cynocephali, until they converted to the knowledge of the Lord. When Saint Andrew entered this city (Patras) and


[p. 580] {#po.15.p580}
ገር ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ሎሙ ፣ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እኩያን ፡ ፈድ ፋደ ፡ ወኢይትኤዘዙ ፣ ወኢይሰምዑ ወቦ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ‹፡ እለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በእንተ ፡ተ ኣምራት ፣ ወመንክራት ፡፣ ዘርእዩ ፡ እምነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ እንድርያስ ፡=፡ ወእለሰ ፣ ኢአምኑ ፣ ተማከሩ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ በምክር ፣ እኩይ ፡ ወፈነዉ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ በጽልሑ ት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኅቤሆሙ ፡ ወተንሥኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወይቅትልዎ ፡፡ ወላእካኒሆሙ ሰ ፡ ሶበ ፣፡ በጽሑ-፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንድርያስ ፣ ወሰምዑ ፣ ትምህርቶ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወርእዩ ፣ ገጾ ፡ ብሩሃ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወኢተመይጡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እለ ፡ ፈነውዎሙ ፡፡ ፦ ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፣፤ እምእሉ ፡፣ እኩያን ፡ ተማከሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፣ ወ ይቤሉ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንንሣእ ፣ ወናውዕፅዮ ፡ በእሳት ፡= ወተጋብኡ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፥ብዙኃ ን ፡ እኩያን ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ።፡ ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ሰአሎ ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትረድ ፡፣ እሳት ፣ እምሰማይ ፡ ከመ ፣ ታውዕዮ
preached the holy gospel to them, the people were extremely wicked; they did not obey and did not listen. Some of them believed because of the miracles and wonders they saw from Saint Andrew. But those who did not believe plotted an evil design against Saint Andrew. They sent to him by ruse, so that he would come to them: then they would rise up against him, to kill him. But when their envoys reached Saint Andrew, and had heard his good teaching and had seen his luminous face, they believed in Our Lord Christ and did not return to those who had sent them. 5. - Others of these wicked men took counsel among themselves and said: "* Let us ourselves take him and burn him in the fire." Many wicked men gathered with them. They came to Saint Andrew, in order to kill him. But the saint asked Our Lord Christ that fire might descend from heaven, in order


[p. 581] {#po.15.p581}
ሙ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ወረደ ፡ እሳት ፣ እምሰማይ ፡ ወአውዓየቶሙ ፡ ወእለሰ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ ፈርሁ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወተሰምዓ ፣ ዜናሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ እሎ ንቱ ፡ በሓውርት ፡ ወብዙኃን ፣ አሕዛብ ፡ እለ ፣ አምኑ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡=፡
to burn them. Immediately fire descended from heaven and burned them. Those who remained were extremely afraid. The fame of the holy apostle Andrew was learned in all these lands. There were many people who believed in the Lord.
-፦ ወገነውተ ፣፤ ጣዖታትሰ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ወኢአምኑ ፡ አላ ፡ ኮኑ ፣ የኅሥሥዎ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተ ጋብኡ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፣ ወመጽኡ ፡ ወነሥአዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ እንድርያስ ፣ ሐዋርያ ፣ ወቀሠ ፍዎ ፤ ቅሥፈተ ፣ ዓቢየ ። ወአዖድዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩላ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እንዘ ፣ ዕራቁ፣ ውእቱ ፡፣ ወወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡፣ ሞቅሕ ፡ ከመ ፡ በሳኒታ ፡ ይስቅልዎ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ል ማዶሙ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ይፈቅዱ ፡ ይቅትሉ ፡ አሐደ ፣ ይነሥእዎ ፣ ወይሰቅልዖ ፡ ዲበ ፡፣ዕፅ፣ወ ይወግርዎ ፡ በአዕባን ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ይመውት
6. - As for the priests of the idols, they saw all these miracles and did not believe. But they sought Saint Andrew, in order to kill him. Then they all gathered and came to take the holy apostle Andrew. They scourged him with a great scourging, paraded him naked through the whole city, and threw him into prison, in order to crucify him the next day. It was their custom, when they wanted to kill someone, to take him, to crucify him on the wood, and to stone him with stones, until he died.


[p. 582] {#po.15.p582}
፦ ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ሰአሎ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ትረድ ፡ እሳት ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወታውፅዮሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ = ወአስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢትደንግጽ ፡ ወኢትሕዝን ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ፍ ልሰትከ ፣ እምዝንቱ ፡፣ ዓለም ፣ ወወሀቦ ፣ ሰላመ ፣ ወተሰወረ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡= ወተፈ ሥሐት ፣፡ ወተሐሥየት ፦ነፍሱ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ።፡ ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ጽባሐ ፣ ነሥእዎ ፡ ወሰቀልዖ ፡ ዲበ ፡፣ ዕፅ ፡ ወወገርዎ ፡ በአዕባን ፡ እስከ ፣ አዕረፈ ።፡ ወመጽኡ ፣ ሰብእ'፣ መሃይምናን ፣ ወነሥኡ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፣ መቃብር ፡ ወተከሥተ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ዓበይት ፡=፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡፡
7. - That night, the apostle Andrew asked Our Lord Christ that fire might descend from heaven and burn them as before. Our Lord Christ appeared to him and said to him: "Do not be afraid and do not be sad, for your departure from this world is near." He gave him the salâm and hid himself * from him. The soul of the holy apostle Andrew rejoiced and exulted. When it was morning, they took him, crucified him on the wood, and stoned him with stones, until he died. Faithful people came to take the body of the saint and placed it in a tomb. Numerous miracles and great wonders were manifested there. May his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen.

II. - Revision or Vulgate. 2. Complementary narrative and Salâm. 8. - Then Satan (Saytän) came into a city. He found two children playing. One of them was the son of John (Yohannès), a priest of the city; the other was the son of one of the notables of the city. While playing with one another, one struck the other; the latter died. His father accused John and said to him: "Deliver your son to me, so that I may kill him in place of my son whom he killed." Having heard this, John became sad. He said to the people of the city: "Promise me to wait, until I go to my father Andrew, so that he may come and resurrect for me the son who has died." As for them, they promised him. 9. - He arrived at Saint Andrew's. He found him baptizing people. He explained to him everything that had taken place. Andrew answered and said to him: "* It is not possible for me to go with you at this hour, because of these people

who are being baptized, but take Philemon with you, so that he may resurrect for you the one who has died." Philemon and this man went away from Andrew. When Philemon approached the city, behold, the judge Rogos was searching for the murderer, for Satan, having taken the appearance of an old man, had explained to him everything that had taken place. The people said to Philemon: "Do not enter the city, so that the judge does not kill you." Philemon said: "It is not possible for me to transgress the order of my master, but I will go, in order to resurrect the dead. If he kills me, my master will come to resurrect me, as well as the one who has died."

10. - As for Philemon, he arrived in the city and met the judge Rogos. The judge ordered the military guards to crucify him. The judge said: "Perhaps this man killed the one who has died." The guards went to arrest him and crucified him. Philemon answered the judge Rogos and said to him: "Why do you scourge me? As for me, I am young and I have not sinned. Condemnation has never been found upon me. Where is my master Andrew, to look at what is being done against his disciple?" He turned his face toward the guards and said to them: "Is there not among you a merciful one, capable of being lenient toward me, of going to my master Andrew and explaining to him

how I have found myself * to be crucified and to be scourged?" When the guards heard this, they wept because of the sweetness of Philemon's speech. 11. - Immediately birds came. They spoke to him as to Noah (Noh), in the days of the flood, and they said to him: "Behold, we ourselves have come. Take whichever one you wish from among us and send him to Andrew." A small bird approached him and said to him: "I myself am lighter of body than these birds here. I myself will go to your master, in order to bring him to you." Philemon said to him: "As for you, you are subject to dying. You will not hasten to return to me. Every time you meet one of your kind, you will stay with him and you will not return in haste." The raven approached him and said to him: "I myself will go to Andrew." Philemon said to him: "In the ancient days, when you were sent, you did not bring back the news to Noah, who had sent you. As for me, I will not send you." Philemon called the dove and said to her: "O chosen traveler, you whom the Lord named the sweet one among all the birds, you who brought the news

of the Lord to Noah, while he was in the ark, in the days of the flood, you whom our father the righteous Noah blessed, go to the city of Lydda to my master Andrew and tell him to come and see his disciple Philemon, for they have crucified him to be scourged." Having gone, she explained this to Andrew. The dove returned and answered Philemon, saying to him: "Be strong and do not fear: behold, Andrew has arrived; he himself has heard your words."

13. - As for Satan (Saytân), he was jealous. He entered the heart of the judge's wife. She killed her children. Her servants gathered and arrested her. They told this to the judge and to Philemon. Philemon called the dove and sent her to the judge's house. She uttered many words. When the people heard the dove speak, they were astonished. They all gathered where Philemon was.

14. - But behold, Andrew arrived. He ordered his disciple Philemon to resurrect the one who had died. Philemon prayed to Our Lord Christ and resurrected the adolescent from death. Furthermore, Andrew and Philemon went to the judge's house. Saint Andrew prayed, healed the judge's wife, and resurrected

15. - Behold, the dove was flying in front of Andrew's head. Andrew said to her: "What is your age?" She said to him: "My age is eight years." Andrew said to her: "You are blessed to have heard the word of my disciple Philemon. Go to the countryside. You will be freed from the servitude of the people of the world. Let no man have access to you." As for her, she went to the countryside, as Andrew had ordered. When the people who were gathered saw this, they all believed and were baptized in the name of the Father, * the Son, and the Holy Spirit.
ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፣ ረድአ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ኬንያ ። አንተ ፡ ሰበከ ፣ ወንጌሎ ፡ ወመሐርከ ፡ በኒቆምድያ ። እዜምር ፡ ለከ ፣ ወእየብብ ፡ በሃሌሉያ ። አንቅሀኒ ፡ እምሐኬትየ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ። ከመ ፡ በእዴከ ፡ ነቅሀት ፣፡ እሙታን ፡ ልድያ =
Hail to you, disciple of Jesus, the Artisan. You yourself preached his Gospel and you taught in Nicomedia (Niqomedyä). I will praise you with songs and I will celebrate you with jubilations with alleluias. Awaken me from my laziness, Andrew, apostle, As by your hand Lydda (Ledyä) was awakened from the dead.

3. Memory of Abba 'Od, of virgins, of James, of Zechariah, of Simeon, of Theodora, and of Täwfinä. On this day also is the memory of Abba 'Od, of virgins, of James (Yä'qob), of Zechariah (Zakäryâs), of Simeon (Sem'on), of Theodora (Tä'oudrä), and of Täwfinä. May their prayer and their blessing be with our king 'Iyâsou for ages of ages. Amen.

### THE 5TH OF TAHSCHASCH
አመ፣፳ለታኅሣሥ። ፪. -፦- ፻0666:0610ኳ. ፔ:ቴህ196. በስመ ፡ አብ ፣ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ፡፡
I. - Primitive recension. 1. Death of the prophet Nahum. In the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, one God.

1. - On this day died the holy and righteous Nahum (Nâhom), the prophet. 2. - He was of the tribe of Simeon (Sem'on). He was in prophecy the sixteenth since the prophet Moses (Mousë). This righteous man and prophet prophesied in the days of Amos, son of Yonädäb, who is also called Joas (Iyo'as), and in the days of Osias ('Ozyân), his son. He rebuked the children of Israel regarding their infidelity and their worship of idols. In his prophecy he revealed that, if the Most High Lord is merciful, lenient, and full of mercy, on the other hand, he himself must take vengeance on his enemies and those who fight against him, and keep punishment for them. He prophesied regarding the preaching of the holy Gospel


[p. 590] {#po.15.p590}

and regarding the apostles who would preach it. He named them: announcers of good things and preachers of peace. 3. - He prophesied regarding the city of Nineveh (Nanawë), declaring that waters and fire would destroy it and do it harm. It happened as he had prophesied. Indeed, the Lord produced in it a great earthquake. Fire ignited * in it and burned half the city: these were those who had turned away from the path of justice and had committed sin. As for those who remained firm in their repentance toward the Lord, nothing bad happened to them at all. 4. - When this prophet had finished his prophecy and had pleased the Lord by his ascetic life, he died in peace. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer. Amen.


[p. 591] {#po.15.p591}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ኢያስድሮስ ፣ ሰማዕት = እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡፡ ፪፪. ፪06918103 “ዣ:ሀ105169. ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ሰባኬ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ እምሲና ። ዘበድልቅልቅ ፣፡ ፍና ። ለዝ ፣ አምላክ ፣ አንተ ፣ ርኢኮ ፡ በዓይነ ፡ ሕሊና ። አስተብቀሦዖ ፡ ናሆም ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ እምፀበለ ፡ እግሩ ፡ ደመና ። ዲበ ፡ ሕማምየ ፡ ይነፍንፍ ፡ ጥዒና ፡፡
2. Martyrdom of Saint Isidore. On this day also is Saint Isidore ('Iyâsderos), martyr. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer forever. Amen. II. - Revision or Vulgate. 3. Salâm to Nahum. Hail to you, preacher of the coming of Him who on Sinai (Sinä) Descended in the midst of an earthquake. This God, you yourself saw him with the eye of the mind. Supplicate him, Nahum, and ask him, from the dust of his feet that rest on the cloud, To make health fall like dew upon my suffering.


[p. 592] {#po.15.p592}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነት ፣ ብፅዕት ፡ አውጋንያ ፡=፡ -፦ ወዛቲሰ ፣ ቅድስት ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ይእቲ ፣ ወስሙ ፣ ለአቡሃ ፣ፊ ልፅስ ። ወኮነ ፡ንጉሥ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ መምድያኖስ ፡ ዘይሰግድ ፡ ለ ጣዖት ፡ ወአቡሃኒ ፡ መምለኬ ፡ ጣዖት ። ወተወልደት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክን ድርያ ፡ ” ወአእማሰ ፡፣ ክርስቲያናዊት ፣ ይእቲ ፡ ወመሀረታ ፡ በኅቡእ ፣ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስ ቲያን ፡ ወሶዕበ ፡ ልህቀት ፡ ፈኃርዋ ፡ መኳንንት ፡ ዐበይት ።፡ ፦፦ ወዕበ ፡ ነገራ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ትቤሎ ፡ አብሐኒ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እፃእ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወእፍታሕ ፡ አዕይንትየ ፡ ወእትፌሣሕ ፡ በርእየተ ፡ አድባራት ። ወሶበ ፡
4. Martyrdom of Saint Eugenia. 1. - On this day also is the martyrdom of the blessed Eugenia ('Awgânyâ). 2. - This saint was from the city of Rome (Romê). The name of her father was Philip (Filpos). There was then the impious king of the city of Rome, called Commodus (Mamdyânos), who worshipped idols. The father of Eugenia was also a worshipper of idols. This saint was born in the city of Alexandria ('Eskendryâ). * Her mother was a Christian. She taught her, in secret, the faith of the Christians. When she had grown up, she was betrothed to one of the great magistrates. 3. - When her father had explained this to her, she said to him: "Allow me first to go to the desert of Alexandria, to open my eyes and to rejoice in the sight of the convents." When her father had heard these words,


[p. 593] {#po.15.p593}
ሰምዓ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ደመረ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ፪ሕፅዋነ ፡ ወአብሓ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትግበር ፡ ዘፈቀደት ፡ ወወ ፅአት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወኦደት ፣ አድባረ ፣ መነኮሳት ፣ ወበጽሐት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክር ስቲያን ፡ እንተ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ላቲ ፡ ኤዲስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ጻድቅ ፣፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ። ወበጺሐ ፡ ነገረቶ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ልባ ፣ ወተጠምቀት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሕፅዋኒሃ ፡ ወመነ ኮሳት ፡ በህየ ፡ ወተሰምየት ፡ ስማ ፣ አውጋንዮስ ፣ ወኢያእመሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብእሲት ፣ ይእቲ ፡፡
he assigned two eunuchs to her and allowed her to do what she would like. She went to the desert, traveled through the convents of the monks, and arrived at a church, at the head of which was a righteous and holy bishop, called Theodore (Tëwodros). Having arrived there, she explained to him everything that was in her heart. She was baptized with her eunuchs. They became monks there. She was called by the name of Eugene ('Awgânyos). It was not known that she was a woman.

4. - When she had not returned to him, her father searched for her in every place. When he had not found her, he made an idol in her image and continued to worship it, evening and morning.

5. - After he had remained a year in office, the abbot of the monastery, that is to say of the sanctuary, died. The monks elected Saint Eugene and instituted him abbot in his place.

7. - Then Satan (Saytân) put an evil desire in the heart * of a woman, to the point that she told Saint Eugene to abandon his convent and his monasticism and to marry her. When Saint Eugene had heard the words of this woman, he said to her: "Go away from me, O my sister, for Satan has tormented you." When he had confounded her, this woman went to the judge of Alexandria and said to him: "Behold, I myself, when I went to such a sanctuary, a young monk came to me in the night and wanted to dishonor me. When I cried out to my menservants and my maidservants, he went away from me." When the judge, who was the father of Eugenia, had heard this, he ordered the monks to be brought, after having chained them. When they had been brought to him, he gave them to another judge, so that he would torture them in his palace. Some of them died.

10. - When the patriarch of Rome had learned the story of Saint Eugenia, he received her and instituted her abbess over the very convent she had built. The nuns of this convent were about three thousand virgins and three hundred nuns. The two eunuchs who had remained with her, he instituted as bishops over two cities. 11. - Another judge arrested Saint Eugenia and tortured her with various tortures, to the point that she gave up her soul and became a martyr for the name of Our Lord Christ. May his blessing be with our father Za-Manfas-Qedous for ever and ever. Hail to Eugenia, who made herself like a eunuch


[p. 597] {#po.15.p597}
በእንተ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ታንዝሕ ፡ መነኮሳተ ፡ አኃወ ። ወጽድቀ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ፊልይስ ፡ እዜኑ ፡ መትልወ ። እመንግሥተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ለመንግሥተ ፡ሰማይ ፡ አድለወ ። ' እስከ ፡ በትረ ፡ ያርክ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይጌሥጽ ፡ አበወ ፡= ወካዕበ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅ”ዱስ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፣ ዘሀገረ ፣ ሻው ፡ ዘአምአድያመ ፡ አስዩጥ ።
For Christ, so that she sprinkled the monk brothers. I then proclaim the justice of her father Philip, Who cared more for the kingdom of heaven than for the kingdom of earth, To the point that, having become patriarch, he rebuked the bishops. 5. Martyrdom of Saint Victor. 1. - On this day also was martyred Saint * Victor (Figtor), from the city of Schâw, in the territory of Asyout.
-፦ ወለዝንቱሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስመ ፣ አቡሁ ፡ መርመር ፡ ወስማኒ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ማ ርታ ። ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ጻድቃነ ፡፣ ወያመልክዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍር ሃት ፣ ወኢኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወልድ ፡፣ ወነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይስእሉ ፡ ወይሁቡ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡
2. - The name of this saint's father was Marmar; the name of his mother was Martha (Märtä). As for them, they were righteous. They worshipped the Lord without fear. They had no child. They continued to


[p. 598] {#po.15.p598}
ብዙኃ፡ ለነዳያን ።ወእምዝ ፣፡ ተወክፈ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስአለቶሙ ፡ወበይእቲ ፡ ዓመት፣ ፀንሰቶ ፣ ቡርክት ፡ ማርታ ፣ ለዝንቱ ፡ ማር ፣ ፊቅጦር ፡ ወወለይዶቶ › አመ ፣ ፪ለግንቦት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሐፀነቶ ፡ በተግሣጽ ፡ ወበፈሪሃ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ፦ ወሶበ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ ፳አመተ ፡ ወሰዶ ፣ አቡሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሜሞ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መስፍነ ፡ ሀየንተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረስዓ ።
pray and to give many alms to the poor. Then the Lord accepted their prayer. That year, Martha, the blessed one, conceived this Mar Victor. She gave birth to him on the 9th of Genbot. Then she raised him in discipline and in the fear of the Lord. 3. - When Victor was twenty years old, his father took him to the king. The king instituted him prefect in his father's place, for the latter had grown old.
፦ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ በጽሐ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ንጉ ሥ ፣ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ኀበ ፣ መኩንነ ፡ እንጽናዕ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይቅትሎሙ ፡ ለክርስቲ ያን ፡ እለ ፣ ኢያመልኩ ፤ አማልክተ ። ወውእቱሰ ፣ መኩንን ፡ መጽአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሽው፣ እንዘ ፡ የኃሥሦሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ። ወአሜሃ ፣ አስተዋደይዎ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እኩያን ፣ ለቅዱ ስ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ያመልክ ፡ እግዚአብሔርሃ ፡ በንጽሕ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዘ ! ዘ፣ ያምጽእዎ ፣ ወአቀምዎ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ አገበሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሠዕ ፡ ለአማል ክት ። ወሶበ ፡ ዓበዮ ፣ አዘዘ ፡ ይዕሥርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወእንዘ ፣ ሀሎ ፡ይ
4. - In those days or a few days later, the letter of the Emperor Diocletian (Diyogletyânos) arrived to the judge of Ensnâ', enjoining him to kill the Christians who did not worship the gods. The judge himself came to the city of Schâw to seek out the Christians. Then wicked people accused Saint Victor, saying that he himself worshipped the Lord purely. Immediately he ordered him to be brought. He was made to appear before him. He himself compelled him to sacrifice to the gods. When he had refused him, he ordered him to be chained in prison. While he was praying


[p. 599] {#po.15.p599}
ጴሊ ፡ በህየ ፣ ወረደ ፣ ኀቤሁ ፣ መልአከ ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፣ ሚካኤል ፡ ወአዕረ ጎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወዓቀብተ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ሞቅሕሰ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ኃጥእዎ ፡ ተሀውኩ ፡ ፈድፋ ደ ። ወአምድኅረ ፡ ኒዕለት ፡ ረከብዎ ፣፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንን ።፡ -፦- ወውእቱኒ ፡ ሰደደ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ተራኅር ኃ ፣፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወተየውሆ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚጦ ፡ እምአምልኮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበዝ ፡ሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡፣ ወስእነ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ አብሎቶ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ወሜጦ ፡ ኀበ ፣ አውጣኮያኖስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ እ ንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ናሁ ፣ ፈነውክቻ ፡ ለከ ። ወለእመ ፡ ሰምዓ ፣ እምኔከ ፡ ይሁዕ ፡ ለአማልክ ት ፡ ወለእመ ፡ አበየከ ፣ ኢትምሀክ ፣ ቀቲሎቶ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አሰሩ ፡ እደዊሁ ፣ ወእገ ሪሁ ፡ ወወደዩ ፡ ልጓመ ፡ ሐዊን ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉሁ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብእዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሠ ፣ ሐመር ፡ ወይሰድዎ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በሀየ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግ ቪአብሔር ፡ ወፈትሖ ፡ እማዕሠሪሁ ።
there, the angel of the Lord, Saint Michael (Mikä'êl), descended to him and made him ascend * to heaven. When the prison guards did not find him, they were extremely troubled. Seven days later, they found him again. They led him to the judge. 5. - He himself sent him to the Emperor Diocletian. The latter showed himself tender and showed himself gentle toward him, in order to turn him away from the worship of the Lord. When he was tired of this attempt and when he could not persuade him, he sent him and directed him to the judge Eustace ('Awtäkoyânos), saying: "Behold, I send you Victor. If he listens to you, let him sacrifice to the gods. If he refuses you, do not hesitate to kill him." Then they tied his hands and feet and put an iron bit in his mouth. The judge ordered him to be taken into the hold of a ship and to be taken away. While he was there, an angel of the Lord descended to him and delivered him from his bonds.


[p. 600] {#po.15.p600}
-፦ ወአምዝ ፡ አብጽሕዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወቅዯስሰ ፡ አኃዘ ፣ ይርግሞ ፡ ለመ ኩንን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ዓላዊ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ተቄጥዓ ፣ መኩንን ፣ ወኩ?ነኖ ፡ጽኑዓ ፡ ኩ ነኔ ፡ ወእምዝ ፣ አሰሮ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በሀየ ፡ አስ ተርአዮ ፣ መድኃኒነ ፡ በሰረገላ ፡ ብርሃን ፣ ወአወፈዮ ፡ ኪዳነ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይገበ ር ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ተኣምራተ ፣ ወመንክራተ ፣ ወይፌውስ ፡ ኩሎ፣ ድ
6. - Then they brought him to the judge. The saint began to curse the judge with his infidel emperor. Immediately the judge became indignant and tortured him with a violent torture. Then he chained him in prison. While Victor was there, Our Savior appeared to him on a chariot of light and gave him the kidân. As for the saint, he performed, from that day on, many miracles and wonders and he healed all the sick
ውያነ። -፦ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ተምዕዓ ፣ ወአዘዘ ፡ ያምጽእዎ ፡ ወዓ ዲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይየውሆ ፡ ወይጊጦ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ፀዐሎ ፡ ወረገመ ፡ አማልክቲሁ ፡ ር” ኩሳነ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይዕሥርዎ ፣ ላዕለ ፡ ዘነበ ፣ ፈረስ ፣ ወይስሐብዎ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ምህ ዋረ ፡፤ አሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምንዳደ ፡ አሳት ፡ ዘቤተ ፡ ብለኔ ፡ ወከመዝ ፣ ፈጸመ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ በሠናይ =፡
7. - When the judge had learned this, he became angry. He ordered him to be brought. Again, he tried to persuade and seduce him. But the saint insulted him and cursed his impure gods. * Immediately the judge ordered him to be tied to the tail of a horse and to be dragged for a day's journey. Then they threw him into the furnace of a bathhouse. Thus he finished his combat in a beautiful way.
-፦ ወሥጋሁሰ ፣ ኢያውፅኡ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ብለኔ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ወረዱ ፡ በሰ
8. - As for his body, they did not pull it from this bathhouse,


[p. 601] {#po.15.p601}
ዋስው ፣ ወገነዝዎ ፡ በአልባስ ፣ ሠናያን ፣ ወአምዓዝዎ ፣ በአፈው ፡ ወሐነጹ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ቤተ ፡ክርስቲያነ። በረከቱ ፣ ለዝንቱ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
but they went down with a ladder, buried him in beautiful clothes, and embalmed him with perfumes. They built a church over him. May the blessing of the combatant Victor be with his dear Za-Manfas-Qedous for ever and ever.
ሰላም ፡ ለዝክረ ፡ ስምከ ፣ ምዑዝ ፡ እምአፈው ። ወጥዑም ፡፣ ዓዲ ፡እምነ ፡ መዓር ፡ ወፄው ፡=፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ በግፅ ፣ ዘአርአያከ ፡ ቅድው ። ይኩነኒ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ አመ ፡ ጠብሑከ ፡ በሸው ። ደመ ፡ ስምዕፅከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማይ ፡ ክዕው ፡=፡
Hail to the memory of your name more fragrant than perfumes And sweeter even than honey and salt, Victor, you who are a sheep whose appearance is pure. May it become my redemption, since you were immolated at Schâw, The blood of your martyrdom which was shed like water.


[p. 602] {#po.15.p602}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለፊልንጦስ ፡ ወሐናንያ ፡ ወበርክዮስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወፊልፅክስ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ለአውጋንያ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወተጋዳሊት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፣ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፣ ንጉሥነ ፣ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡=፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ተዝክሩ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፣ ኤላውትሮስ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፣ አምላ ክ ፡ ዘፈጸመ ፡ ስምዖ =፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለፍቁሩ ፡ አቅበ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፣ ዓለም ፡=፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ኤላውትሮስ ፣ ብፁዕ ። ሰማዕተ ፡ እግዚአ ፡= በእንተ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አለ ፡ ተቀትሉ ፡ ሰብእ ። አሀሉ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀሎከ ፡ በታሕተ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ። ፡ አስተዳልወኒ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ሥሩዕ ፡፡
6. Memory of Filentos, of Hanänyâ, of Barachie, of John and of Philip, father of Eugenia. On this day also is the memory of Filentos, of Hanänyâ, of Barachie (Barekyos), of John (Yohannès) and of Philip (Filpos), father of Eugenia ('Awgânyä) martyr and combatant. May their prayer and their blessing be with our king 'Iyäsou for ever and ever. Amen. 7. Memory and salâm of Saint Eleutherius. On this day also is the memory of Saint Eleutherius ('Éläwteros), martyr of God, who completed his martyrdom. May the Lord have mercy, through his prayer, on his dear 'Aqba-'Iyäsous for ever and ever. Hail to you, blessed Eleutherius, Martyr of the Lord, You who were the companion of people who were killed for Jesus Christ. May I be found where you are under the great altar. Make me, through your prayer, a predestined man.


[p. 603] {#po.15.p603}
፲፲፪. ጅ፲ጀዐህ።6'.
III. - Proper. 8. Life of the holy Father Abba Gabra-Näzräwi.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ናዝ ራዊ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወንጹሕ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ ወቀናዒ ፡ ለሕገ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ።
1. - On this day also is the memory of the holy Father Abba Gabra-Näzräwi, virgin, pure, ascetic and zealous for the law of the Lord.
-፦ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፣፡ ክቡራን ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ኮኑ ፡ ፈራህያነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሕገ ፡ ቤተ ፡፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወእሙንቱኒ ፡፣ መሃይምናን ፣ ጌራ”ን ፣ ወመፍቀርያተ፤ እግዚአብሔር ።፡ ወሶበ ፣ ተወልደ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ልህቀ ፡ በጥበብ ፡ ወበትምህርት ፡ ወበፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተሰይመ ፡ ዲያቆነ ፡ ኃሠሦ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ነቢያት ፣ ወሐዋ ርያት ። ወረከበ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ወዘሰ ፣ ሠዓረ ፡፣ ወአብደረ ፡ ባቲ፣ በዕለተ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ግብረ ፡ ሞተ ፣ ለይሙት ።፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ረከበ ፡ አባ ፣ ሲኖዳስ ፣ አክብ ሩ ፡ ሰንበታትየ ፡ በተጠናቅቆ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ይብል ፡ ወንጌልየ ፡ ለበወ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ለየውጣ ፡ አሐቲ፡ ኅርመት ፡ ቅርጸታ፡ ኢትኅልፍ ፡ እምኦሪት ፡ ወእምነቢያት =፡ ወዘሰ ፡ ያሰትት ፡ ወያአትት ፡ አሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ወአሐተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እምፅለተ ፡ ትእዛዝ ፣ ሕ
2. - His parents were noble. They themselves feared the Lord and observed the law of the Church. They were also excellent believers * and loving the Lord. When this saint was born and had grown in wisdom, in doctrine and in the fear of the Lord, he was ordained deacon. He scrutinized the books of the prophets and the apostles. He found in the Mosaic Law the passage which says: Whoever has violated the Sabbath and has preferred, on the day of the Sabbath, to do a work, let him die the death. Furthermore, he found the commentary of Abba Sinoda (Sinodäs) on the text: Honor my Sabbaths with diligence, where Abba Sinoda speaks of the two Sabbaths. He understood my Gospel which says: Let not a single iota, a single stroke and a single petiole pass from the Law and the prophets. As for him who neglects and omits a single day of


[p. 604] {#po.15.p604}
ፁፅ ፡ ይከውን ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ለበወ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ ዘኢኃ ደገኒ ፤ አቡሁ ፣ ወእሙ ፡ ወዓዲ ፣ ነፍስ ፣፡ ወኢይክል ፡ ይጸመደኒ ፡ ወከዊነ ፡ ረድእየ ፡=፡
precept and a single word relative to the day of precept, he will become small in the kingdom of heaven. Furthermore, he understood what Our Savior says: Whoever will not have abandoned his father, his mother and even himself will not be able to

serve me and become my disciple. 3. - When he went to our Father Gregory (Gorgoryos), spiritual son of our Father Honorius ('Anorëwos), of Dabra-Segâdjà, he became a monk under him. Then he persevered in great labor, in many vigils, in fasting and in prayer. Then he went to the desert. Then he was ordained priest. He desired to go to the land of Tigray (Tegrây) with Abba Jacques (Yä'qob). They explained to the monks the Scriptures of the Lord, of the Old and New Testament. Abba Gabra-Näzräwi revealed his thought to Abba Jacques and said to him: "The Lord will help us." They worked together. But Abba Jacques acted with cunning. Abba Gabra-Näzräwi said to him: "Do not work with me anymore." They separated from each other * on the will of the Lord, saying: "We make ourselves free from each other." Abba Gabra-Näzräwi made a monastery there. Many monks gathered to him. He made canons there. He observed my commandments, the glory of my two Sabbaths, the glory of the days of obligation and the monastic rules from morning until evening. He observed the hours, each in their time, without ceasing. He forbade taking the property of others. He forbade priests from receiving bribes. He forbade cursing, in the name of the


[p. 605] {#po.15.p605}
ሙ ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ከዊኖ ፡ ወዝንቱ ፣ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ። ወለሊ ሁሰ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዓቅም ፡ ለጸሎት ፡ ተዘኪሮ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ እባርኮ ፣ ለአግዚአብሔ ር ፡ በዙሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡፡ ወዘልፈ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉየ = ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ወእዛዋዕ፤ በትእዛዝከ ፣ ወኢያረስዕ ፣ ቃለከ ፣ ወዓዲ ፣ ኢይትረከብ ፣ ይትዔረም ፡ እምነ ፡ ስብ ሐት ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ደም ። ወበመዓልትሂ ፡ ኢይበልዕ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ይዕለት ፡ ወቁስጠንጢኖስ ሂ ፣ ኢይበልዕ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወረከበ ፡ ከዊኖ ፣ በሕገ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያዜክ ራ ፤ ለነፍሱ ፤ ሕማማተ ፡ እግዚኡ ፣ ወይውኅዝ ፡ አንብፁዑ ፡ እስከ ፣ ይርኅስ ፣ መከየደ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ረሲኖ ፡፣ ተዘኪርሮ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ብፁዓን ፡፣ እለ ፡ ይላህዉ ፡ይ እዜ ፣ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፣ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይሰግድ ፡ ተፅዒኖ ፣፡ ፍትወተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወኢሞኦ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ እንግዳ ፣ ጻሊቃናተ ፣ ተዘኪሮ ፡ ይሰፍሕ ፡ ለውሂብ ፣ የማኖ ብፁዕ ፡ ዘርአ ፡ ጽዮን ፣ ወይቤ ፡ ተዘኪሮ ። ወያጠውቃ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ በሩር ፡ ወበዕርቃን ፣ ወበድካም ፣ ወሐፍ ፡ ተዘኪሮ ፣ ዘይ"ቤሎ ፣ ለቀሌምንጦስ ፡ ወግፋዕ ፡ ሥ ልጣኖ ፡ ለርጉም ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፣ ወበብዙኅ ፡ ትጋህ ፣ ሌሊተ ፣ ወበእንተ ፣ ኢ
Lord, whoever it may be. He instructed his spiritual children. He himself had no measure for prayer, remembering what David (Dâwit) says: I will bless the Lord at all times. Always his glorification will be in my mouth. Furthermore, (David) says: I will meditate on your commandments and I will not forget your words. David says again: He will not be found with those who praise and he will be removed from the glorification, he who sheds blood. During the day, Abba Gabra-Näzräwi did not eat, except three days per week. Constantine (Quastantinos) also only ate bread. Abba Gabra-Näzräwi applied himself to meditating on the law of the Lord. He reminded himself of the Passion of his Master. His tears flowed to the point that the footstool of his feet was wet. He was inflamed with the Holy Spirit and remembered what Our Lord says: Blessed are those who mourn now, for they themselves will rejoice. He was pious to the point that he worshipped the Lord, even while riding a horse. The desire of the world did not overcome him. He was the friend of strangers, remembering the seven deacons. He extended his right hand to give. He remembered what the blessed Zar'a-Syon had said. He tormented himself by cold, by nakedness, by fatigue and by sweat, remembering what Peter said * to Clement (Qalêmentos): "Repress the power of the Cursed one by fasting, by prayer and by many vigils during the night." Following the example of Abba Isaiah, he did not take for himself any advantage over


[p. 606] {#po.15.p606}
ሳይያስሂ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘአዕበየ ፡ ለርእሱ ፡ እምነ ፣ ደቂቁ ፣ እመሂ ፣ በመብልዕ ፣ ወእመሂ ፣ በመስቴ ፡ ወአመሂ ፡ በጸብሕ ፡ ወገቢረ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ፳8ወዓቀበ ፡ ዘንተ እስከ ፣ ተፍጻሜ ቱ ፡ፍልሰቱ
his spiritual children, whether in food, or in drink, or in the seasoning of dishes. He observed, in practice, each of the canons until the end of his life and until his departure from this world.

4. - To our Father Gabra-Näzräwi were given science, intelligence and numerous charisms: expulsion of demons, healing of the sick, accomplishment of wonders and explanation of things hidden until now. By the Holy Spirit he knew everything. Even when his spiritual children consoled him, he reminded them of their secret sins. He perceived what was happening in their heart, before it took place. Furthermore, let us mention some of his wonders. First miracle. He once found the great gate of the monastery locked. He turned his face toward his God. The Father Abba Gabra-Näzräwi, after having offered the holy sacrifice and sprinkled the gate with holy water, saw it open. As for him, on the will of the Lord, he passed with his spiritual children. Second miracle. Being young, he was restoring a church. He found a column fallen toward the south. He called his own disciples. An hour later, there were about seventeen monks who were there. Others also came by necessity. He said to them: "Go, lift this column and do not hesitate. To preserve it, lift it on the will of the Lord." They could not lift it at all because of their wickedness.

Then they cut it. It had a thickness of three cubits and, from its top, a length of three measures. Many people struggled to lift this column, * monks from another convent: from the sanctuary of our Father John (Yohannès) and people of the world. They could not lift it. They said they were abandoning it. Abba Gabra-Näzräwi prayed over the column. Having finished his prayer with tears, he then said to them: "Lift it." They said yes to him and they raised it on the will of the Lord. Third miracle. He sent his disciples to a distant land: to Hadyâ. His disciple Isaac died. Our Father Abba Gabra-Näzräwi knew it by the Holy Spirit. He said to the priest, at the altar: "Remember Isaac (Yeshaq): it is today the day he died." Six months later, he returned to his country. He announced the day of Isaac's death. It turned out to be so. We have mentioned some of his miracles, but we have omitted the most numerous

ሪረ ፡ ሞት ፣፡ወሰዓት ፡ለዕለት ፡ ወያስተፌሥሖሙ ፡ በሕንጻ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ለጻ ድቃን ፡ዕሜተ ፡ ኖላዊ ፡ ከዊኖ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ሐኬት ፡ ወዓቀበ ፡ መርዔቶ ፡እምነ ፡ ተዙላት ፡ ወአዝዕብት ፣ ወእምነ ፡ አክይስት ፣ መሣጥያነ ፡ ነፍሳት ። ወአኮ ፣ ለባሕቲቶሙ ፡ ዘኮነ ፣ ዓላዊ ፣፤ አላ ፣ ለመኳንንት ፡ ወለዓበይትሂ ፡ ወለአብዕልትሂ ፡ ለእድ ፡ ወለሕፃናት ፡ ይስ ሕብዎሙ ፡ ወይጌሥጽዎሙ ፡ በእንተ ፤ አምልኮቶ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ።፡ እስመ ፣ ይብ ል ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ አፍቅር ፡ ቢጸከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነፍስከ ። ወብፁዕሰ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ናዝራዊ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይበውዕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፉርባን ፡ ያልዕል ፡ ሕሊና ፣ ምድራዊ ፡ ወያውኅዝ ፡ አንብዓ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ ነቅዓ ፣ ማ"”ይ ፣ መልዕልተ ፡ ርእስከ ፣ ወለሊሁ ፡ ይቤ ፣ መጽአኒ ፡ ሕ ሊና ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ አሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፣ በውስተ ፣፡ ምሥዋዕ ። ውእቱሰ ፡፣ ዘኮነ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አኃው ፡ ፅፁብኬ ። በእንተዝ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ናስተበፅዖ ፣፡ ወ ኦኮ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ አላ ፡ ለነቢይ ፡ ንሰምዖ ፣ ወይቤ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ብእሲ ፣ ዘይፈርሆ ፡ ለእግዚ አብሔር ፣ ወዘይፈቅድ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡=፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ እለ ፡ ንጹሓን ፡ በፍኖቶሙ ፡ ወእለ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በፍናዊሁ ፡= ስብሐት ፣ ለአብ ፣ ዘኃረዮ፣ ስብሐት ፡ ለወ
reminded them of the bitter death and the hour of the last day. He rejoiced them by the edifice of the kingdom of heaven, reward for the righteous. Being a pastor without laziness, he guarded his flock from wolves, hyenas and serpents, ravishers of souls. Not only any infidel, but the magistrates, the great, the rich, the men, the women and the children, he attracted them and rebuked them regarding the worship to be rendered to the Lord. Indeed, the Scripture says: Love your neighbor as yourself. When the blessed Gabra-Näzräwi, our Father, entered the church for the Eucharistic sacrifice, he raised his thought from earthly things and made his tears flow, as if he were going to die and as if a fountain * of water was on his head. He himself said: "The thought of heavenly things comes to me inevitably every day, while I am at the altar." As for him who was at the head of his brothers, he acted only for them. It is therefore admirable. That is why we declare this holy man blessed. It is not we who declare him blessed, but we hear the prophet who says: Blessed is the man who fears the Lord and who wants to accomplish his commandments exactly. Furthermore, he says: Blessed are those who are pure in their way and who go in the ways of the Lord. Glory to the Father who chose him. Glory to the Son who loved him. Glory to the Holy Spirit who

was pleased in him and established his dwelling in him. Glory to the Lord in a single divinity and in a single dominion, without separation, now, forever and for ever and ever. Amen.

6. - When the departure of his soul from this passing world arrived for him, he gathered his spiritual children. He declared to them, saying: "The Holy Spirit is my witness that I have taught you nothing other than the eighty-one Scriptures, which I have gathered for you. Never free yourselves from them, neither to the right, nor to the left." Regarding the faith in the Trinity he said to them: "Strengthen what I have taught you. One will not understand this teaching after you. May the torrents and the winds not shake you. May your edifices not be found on the sand. Do not unite yourselves, in your faith, with those who are heretical and do not revere the Lord, whose existence was, before the world, with the Father, the Son and the Holy Spirit Paraclete, but not * generating. One is the nature of the divine persons; one is their royalty; one is their sovereignty; one is their clemency; one is their mercy. No person is of a different essence, nor made, nor created, in the holy Trinity. The Father is Lord; the Son also is Lord; the Holy Spirit also


[p. 610] {#po.15.p610}
ብዑድ ፡፤ ወግቡር ፡ ወፍጡር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ አብኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወ ወልድኒ ፤ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስኒ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ውእቱ ፡በ ዝንቱ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ቅንዓት ፡ አሐቲ ፣ ልብክሙ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ፣ ወአልቦ ፣ ዘየሐ ፅፅ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡፣ አላ ፡ ፳ኅቡረ ፣ ህላዌሆሙ ፡ አልቦ ፣ ዘይትዔረዮሙ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይ ትሜሰሎሙ ፡ ለቅድስት ፣ ሥላሴ ፣ በሰማይ ፡ በላዕሉ ፣ ወበምድር ፡፣ በታሕቱ ፡፣ ከመ ፣ ቀዳሚ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ምሥጢር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይትሜሰሎ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ እስመ ፣ መልዕል ተ ፡ አምሳል ፡፤ ንሕነ ፡ ወጥቅመ ፡ ርአስክሙ ፡ በአሚነ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወእመቦ ፡ ዘወሰከ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ አኃው ፡ ዘአሕፀፀ ፡ ውጉዝ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወእ ምዝ ፣ ዘንተ ፡ ብሒሎ ፡ ወአለቢዎ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፣ ናዝራዊ ፡ አዕረፈ፣0በ ሰላም ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ለታኅሣሥ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ እጉድ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሞርባን ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሃሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ስእለ ፡ ጽዮን ፣ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
is Lord. May your zeal be unanimous for the profession of this faith. No person is greater than the other; no one is smaller than the other, but the three persons are, each, consubstantial. Nothing is equal and nothing is similar to the holy Trinity, in heaven above and on earth below. As was said formerly, it is a mystery to which nothing resembles, for it is above all symbols. May the principal foundation of your piety be in the faith in the Father, in the Son and in the Holy Spirit. If someone adds to the Trinity, O my brothers, or diminishes anything from it, he is excommunicated." Then having said this and having taught it to his spiritual children, our Father Gabra-Näzräwi died in peace on the 5th of Tâhschâsch, on Sunday, after the Eucharistic sacrifice. May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear Se'la-Syon for ever and ever.


[p. 611] {#po.15.p611}

### THE 6TH OF TAHSCHASCH
ሽ አመ ፡ ጳለታኅሣሥ ። ፪. ፲8666.0810. ህ9፲1:0111ሆ6. በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ = በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካረ ፣ በዓሉ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፣ ሰማዕት ፡ አንጦሎስ ፡ ቀሲስ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ፡፡
I. - Primitive recension. 1. - Memory of Saint Anatole, priest. In the name of the Father, of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day is the memory of the feast of the holy martyr Anatole ('Antolos), priest. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer. Amen.


[p. 612] {#po.15.p612}
-፦ ወበዛቲ፣ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ፣ አዕረፈ ፡አብ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ሶርያዊ ፡ ሊ ቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወውእቱ ፣ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፳ወ፪ ።
2. Death of Abba Abraham, the Syrian. 1. - On this day also died the holy Father Abba Abraham ('Abrehâm), the Syrian, patriarch of the city of Alexandria ('Eskendryâ). He is of the number of the patriarchs the sixty-second.
ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምነ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘብሔረ ፣ ምሥራቅ ። ወኮነ ፡ ነጋዴ ፡ ወብዙኅ ፡ ንዋዩ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምስር ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይነግድ ፡ ወ ኅደረ ፣ ውስቴታ ፣ ድኅረ ፣ ወአስተርአዩ ፣ እምኔሁ ፣ ትሩፋት ፣ ብዙኃት ፣ ወምሕረ ት ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለጊሩት ፣ ወለአእምሮ ።፡
2. - This Father was from the Christians of the land of the East. He was a merchant; his riches were numerous. He came to Egypt (Misr) many times while trading. He finally remained there. Many virtues appeared in him, as well as his mercy toward the poor. The fame of his goodness and his knowledge spread.


[p. 613] {#po.15.p613}
ወተሰናአዉ ፡ አበው ፡፣ ኤኢስ ፣ ቆኦሳት ፣ ወልሂቃን ፣ ወማእምራን ፡ ላዕ ሴሁ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይሚምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ እስክንድርያ ፡፡ ወሶበ ፡ ተሠ ይመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ በፈቃደ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሀበ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ለነዳያን፣፡ወ” ለምስኪናን ፡= ወመተረ ፡፤ ኩሎ ፡ ልማደ ፣ እኩየ ፡ እምሚመቱ ፣ ወአውዝዞሙ ፣ ለኩ ሎሙ ፣ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፣ ከመ ፡ ኢይንሥኡ ፡ ሕልያነ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ሚመተ ፡ ክህነት ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ወአውገዞሙ ፡፣ ለእለ ፣ ይነሥኡ ፡ ዕቁባተ ፡=፡ ወተምዕዓ ፡ ወተቄጥዓ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እለ ፣ ቦሙ ፡ በኅቤሆሙ ፡ ዕቁባት ፡፡
3. - The Father bishops, the notables, and the scholars agreed to appoint him patriarch of the city of Alexandria. When he was appointed patriarch by the will of the Lord, he gave all his riches to the poor and * to the unfortunate. He suppressed every evil custom within the jurisdiction of his authority. He forbade, under pain of excommunication, all bishops from ever receiving bribes on the occasion of priestly ordination. He excommunicated those who took concubines. He became angry and indignant against those who had concubines in their homes.
-፦ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ዕቁባተ ፣ ግዘተ ፣ ዘአውገዞሙ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳ ሳት ፡ ፈርሁ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወእምግዘቱ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ ወፈርሀዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ፡
4. - When those who had concubines learned of the excommunication with which the patriarch had struck them, they were extremely afraid of


[p. 614] {#po.15.p614}
ወአውጽኡ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡፤ ዕቁባቲሆሙ ፡፣ እምውስተ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ።፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ኅበ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡ አብርሃም ፣ ወሰገዱ ፣ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወነስሑ ፡ ወተወክፈ፣ ንስሓሆሙ ፡ ወኅደገ ፡ ሎሙ ፣፡ ኩሎ ፡ አበሳሆሙ ፡፡ ወኢደፈረ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ፳ብእሲ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ጸሓፊ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ ምስር ፣ ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ምስኪን ፣ ኢፈርሆ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ፡ ወኢ ፈርሃ ፡ እምግዘቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ። ወገሠጾ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ብዙኃ፣ ጊዜ፡ወ ተዓገሠ ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፣ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወኢተገሠ ጸ፣፤፡ወኢተመይጠ ፡ እምእከዩ ፡ ወኢፈርሃ ፡ ክመ ፡ ያጥፍኦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ፡፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሬኢ ፡ ይአተ ፡ ሲበተ ፡ ቅድስተ ፡ ወንጽሕተ ፣ ውግርተ ፡ ዲበ ፣ ምድር ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ዘይደልዎሙ ፡ መቲረ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይስእሎ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይትመየጥ ፡
him and his excommunication. They feared the Most High Lord. They drove all their concubines from their houses. They came to the patriarch Abba Abraham, prostrated themselves at his feet, and repented. He accepted their repentance and forgave them all their fault. 5. - No one dared to revolt against him, except for one man, who was the great secretary of the king of Egypt. This wretch did not fear the Most High Lord, nor did he fear the excommunication of this Father. This Father admonished him many times; he was patient with him for many days and prostrated himself before him many times. But the latter did not correct himself and did not turn from his malice. He did not fear that the Most High Lord would annihilate him. He himself remained obstinate, even though he saw this holy and pure old man, stretched out on the ground at his feet, which deserved to be cut off, asking him to turn from his malice


[p. 615] {#po.15.p615}
እምእከዩ ፣ ወያወጽኣ ፡ ለይእቲ ፣ ዕቅብት ፣ እምቤቱ ፡ ወኢሰምዓ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፣ ወኢተ መይጠ ፡ እምእከዩ ፡=፡
and to drive the concubine from his house. He did not listen to the patriarch's order and did not turn from his malice.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ ኢኃደገ ፡ ዝ”ንቱ ፣ አብ ፣ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ትምሀርቶ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወኢተግግጾ ፡ አላ ፡ ተተሐተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቤቱ ፣ ለውእቱ ፦ ብእሲ ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ከሓዲ ፡ ምጽአቶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ፣ ኅበ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ አፅጸወ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኖኅተ ፣ ቤቱ ። ወነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፪ሰዓ ት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፕኅተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ወይጐደጐ ድ ፡ዲበ ፡ ፕኅት ፡ ወኢያርኃወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወኢተናገሮ ፡ 8ቃለ ።
6. - Then * this Father did not in any way abandon his teaching for him, nor his admonition, but he humbled himself like Christ, his creator. He went to the house of this man. When this impious man learned of the coming of this Father to his house, he himself had the door of his house closed. This Father remained for two hours, standing at the door of the house of this wicked man. He knocked on the door, but the latter did not open for him and did not address a single word to him.
-፦ ወሶበ ፡ ጠየቀ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ምስኪን ፡ ፈለጠ ር እሶ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወተሐጉለ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡፣ እመርዔተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ኩለንታሁ ፣ መ
7. - When this Father was certain that this unfortunate man was separating himself on his own initiative, that he was losing himself by his own will, by


[p. 616] {#po.15.p616}
ለያልየ ፡ ሙሱነ ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አበሳ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ በምክ ር ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሮ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢይማስን ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መለያልያት ። ወረሰየ ፡ ደሞ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ርእሱ ፣ ወአውገዞ ፡ ወአንገፈ ፡ ጸበለ ፡ አሣእኒሁ ፡ ዲበ ፣ ኖኅ ተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ርኩስ ፡፡ ፦ ወከሠተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ትእምርተ ፡፤ በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፣ በቅድመ ፡ ሰብ እ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፣ በህየ ፣ ወተሠጥቀት ፣ መድረከ ፡ ጥኅቱ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወኮ ነት ፣ ፪ክፍለ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እብን ፣ ጽኑዕ ።፡ ኦዝመንክር ፡ ወዕፀብ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ እስ መ ፡ እብን ፡ ጽኑዕ ፣ ወጽሙም ፡ ተሠጥቀ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ግዘቶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወ ልቡሰ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘምሉእ ፡ አበሳ ፡ ኢለምለመ ፡፡
excluding himself from the flock of Christ and that he had become entirely a corrupted member, he did not commit a fault regarding him; but he thought, with a good sentiment, to cut him off from the body, so that all the members would not be corrupted. He put his blood on his head. He excommunicated him. He shook the dust from his shoes at the door of the house of this impure man. 8. - The Lord manifested a miracle, at that moment, before the people who were there. The threshold of the door of this house split and became two pieces, even though it was a hard stone. O prodigy and wonder that this is! The stone, which was hard and deaf, split when it heard the excommunication of this Father, but the heart of this man, which was filled with sins, did not soften.


[p. 617] {#po.15.p617}
-፦ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ከሠተ ፣፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ትእምር ተ ፡ ዓባየ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ነዳየ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ እምዥሎሙ ፡ "ነዳያን ፡ ወሠዓርዎ ፣ እምሚ መቱ ፡ በሐሣር = ወኢተረፈ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ እምንዋዩ ፡ 8ጸራይቅ ፣ ወአመንይቦ ፡ እግ ቪአብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ በደዌ ፡ ዕፁብ ፡= ወመተሩ ፡ እደዊሁ ፣ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ሐካም ፡ ወሞተ ፡ በእኩይ ፡ ሞት ፡ ወኮነ ፣ አርአያ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወፈር ሁ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ኃጥኣን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ሞት ፡ ወነስሑ ፡=፡ -፦- ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ለምእዝ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ምስር ፣ ሊ ቀ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ አይሁዳዌ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግዕዘ ፡ ተንባላት ፥ ወኮነ ፣ ከማሆሙ ፡፡ ወሀለዎ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራ ፣ ፳ዓርክ ፡፣ አይሁዳዊ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይበውእ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፣
9. - Then the Lord manifested a great miracle upon this man: he became extremely poor, poorer than all * the poor. He was dismissed from his office with dishonor. Not a single penny of his riches remained to him. The Lord tormented him in his body with a violent illness. His hands were cut off in the days of King Hakim. He died a bad death. This was an example for every man. Many sinners feared the bad death that had happened to him and repented. 10. - In the days of this Father, Mu'izz, king of Egypt (Misr), had a general who had been a Jew, had entered the religion of the Muslims, and had become like them. This general had a Jewish friend. He


[p. 618] {#po.15.p618}
ኩሎ ፣ ጊዜ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይትናገር ፡፣ ምስሌሁ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ አይሁዳዊ ፡ ሶበ ፡፣ ረከበ ፡ ሞገሰ ፡ በኅበ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ተአርኮተ ፡ ሊቀ ፣ ሐራሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፣ ወተ መነየ ፣ ላዕለ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተመንነይኩ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታምጽእ ፡ ሊተ ፡ሊ ቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ከመ ፡ እትዋሣእ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡=፡
entered with him, at any moment, to the king and conversed with him. When this Jew had found favor with the king because of the friendship of the king's general, he desired a favor from the king and said to him: "I desire from you that you bring me the patriarch, so that I may dispute with him."
፦ ወፈነወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳዊሮ ስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ መቅፋዕ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆይፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አስሙናይን ፤ ወተዋሥኡ ፣ ምስለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አይሁዳዊ ፡ ወሞእዎ ፡ ወአስተኃፈርዎ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተፈ ሥሐ ፡ ወአክበሮ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ወለአለ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወሐሩ ፡ኀ በ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡፤ በሰላም ። ወተኃፍሩ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ አይሁዳዊ ፣ ወኮኑ ፡ የኅኀሥሠ ፡ ድቀቶሙ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡፡
11. - The king sent messengers and brought this Father. With him was Abba Severus Walda-Magfa' (Sawiros), bishop of the city of Ashmunayn. They disputed with this Jew, defeated him, and confounded him. When the king had seen this, he rejoiced and honored the patriarch and those who were with him with great honors. They went to their houses in peace. The general and the Jew were confounded. They sought the fall of the patriarch and the people of the Christians.
፦ ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ቦአ ፣ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ሐበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኣጤ ይቀከ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፣ እስመ ፣ ክርስቲያንሰ ፣ አልቦሙ ፣ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እስመ ፣ በወንጌ
12. - One day, the general entered to the king and said to him: "I assure you, my master, that the Christians do not have faith. Indeed, in their Gospel
