ሎሙ ፡ ይቤ ፣ እመሰ ፣ "ብክሙ ፡፣ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕጠተ ፡ ስናፔ ፣ ወትብልዎ፣ ለዝንቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ፍልስ ፡ ወደቅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወይፈልስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉ ሥ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ፈነወ ፡፤ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፣ አብርሃም ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ትቤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ወንጌል ፣ ዘይቤ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ብክሙ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡፤ በከመ ፡፣ ሕጠተ ፡ ስናፔ ፡ ወትብልዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ፍልስ ፣ ወይፈል ስ = ወይቤሎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ እወ ፡ በአማን ፣፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ወ ይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ አአላፋት ፣ ወትእልፊተ ፣ አአላፋት ፡ በሃይማኖ ትክሙ ፡ ወእፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታምጽእ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አሐደ ፡ እምኔክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትከሠት ፣ ዛቲ ፡ ትእምርት ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወካዕበ ፣ አንተ ፡ ርአሰ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡
Jesus says: If * you have faith as big as a mustard seed and if you say to this mountain: "Move and fall into the sea", it will move. When the king had heard this, he sent messengers and brought the patriarch Abba Abraham. He said to him: "What do you say about this word which is in the Gospel and which says: If you have faith as big as a mustard seed and if you say to this mountain: "Move", it will move?" The patriarch said to him: "Yes, truly, so says the holy Gospel." The king said to him: "Here you are by the thousands and myriads in your faith. I want you to bring me one of you, so that this miracle may be manifested through him. Furthermore, you are the leader of all the people of the Christians. It is fitting that this miracle be manifested through you."


[p. 620] {#po.15.p620}
ወይደልወከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትከሠት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ትእምርት ፡ በእዴከ ፡ ወሶበ ፣ ሰምዓ ፣ ሊቀ፣ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፣ ደንገፀ ፣ ወፈርሀ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ተዐገሠኒ ፣ ይዕለተ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ይኩንከ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡፡
When the patriarch had heard this word, he was terrified and feared extremely. He said to the king: "Be patient with me for three days." The king said to him: "Let it be done to you as you say."
-፦ ወወፅአ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣፡ ወአስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆአሳት ፣ ወለካህናት ፣ ወለመነኮሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ቅሩባን ፣ ኅቤሁ ፡= ወነበሩ ፣ በው ስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፥ ዘእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ በማእልቃ ፡ ዘ ምስር ፡ ፻ደመዓልተ ፡ ወ፻ ፡ ለያልየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸውሙ ፡ ወይጹልዩ ፡ ወያስተበቶ'ዑ ፡ ኀ በ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡=፡ ወዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ሌሊተ ፣ ሣልሲተ ፡፣ በጽ "በሕ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ አ ስተርአየቶ ፣ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፣ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፣ አምላክ ፡ በገጽ ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ወፍሥሕ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡፣ ለሊቀ ፡፣ ጳጳሳት ፣ ምንት ፣ ዘበጽሐከ ፣ ወይቤላ ፣ ኦእ
13. - The patriarch went out from before the king. He gathered the bishops, the priests, and the monks who were near him. They remained in the church of Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary of Ma'elga of Cairo (Misr), for three days and three nights, fasting, praying, and supplicating the Lord. When the third night had passed, very early * in the morning, Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary, generator of God, appeared to the patriarch with a luminous and joyful face and said to him: "What has happened to you?" He said to her: "O my Lady, you yourself know what has happened to me." She
ግዝእትየ ፤ አንቲ ፣ ታአምሪ ፡ ዘበጽሐኒ ።፡ ወትቤሎ ፣ ኢትፍራህ ፡፣ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ተወከፍኩ ፡ አንብዓከ ፡ ዘከዓውከ ፡፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያንየ ፡ ወይእዜኒ ፣ ተን ሥእ ፡ ወሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡ መርኅብ ፣ ዘሐጺን ፡ ዘይወስድ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፣ ወትረክብ ፤ ብእሴ ፡ እንዝ ፡ ይጸውር ፡ ቀሱተ ፣ ማይ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መታክፍቲሁ ፡ ወዐቱ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ዓይ ን ፡ አኃዝ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይከሥት ፡ ለከ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ወስሙ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ዘይሰሬ ፡ አሣእነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልሐ ፡ ዓይኖ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡፡
said to him: "Do not fear, for I myself have accepted the tears that you have shed in my church. Now rise and go toward the place of Iron, in the direction of the east. You will find a man carrying a pitcher of water on his shoulders. He has only one eye. Take him, for he himself will manifest this miracle for you. The name of this man is Simon (Sem'on), the shoemaker. Indeed, he himself tore out his eye because of the commandment of my beloved Son Jesus Christ."


[p. 621] {#po.15.p621}
ወተንሥአ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወረከቦ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእ ሲ ፡ ወአኃዞ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ መሀሮሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወነገሮ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አስ ተርአየት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፣ ማርያም ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ትቤሎ ፡ በእን ቲአሁ ፡፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ እስመ ፡
14. - The patriarch rose, went in haste to find this man, took him, and said to him: "Have pity on the people of the Christians." He explained to him how Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary had appeared to him and how she had spoken to him about him. This man prostrated himself before him and said to him: "Forgive me, O my Father, for I am a sinner and a guilty man. I cannot


[p. 622] {#po.15.p622}
አነ ፡ ኃጥእ ፡ ወአባሲ ፣ ወኢይክል ፡ ለዘ ፡ ትቤለኒ ፡ አላ ፣ ፈቃደ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ለ ይኩን ፡ በጸሎተ ፣ እግዝእትነ ፣ ማርያም ፡ ወጸሎትከ ፤ ትርድኦሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስ ቲያን '፡=፡ አላ ፤ እስአለከ ፣ ኦአቡየ ፣ ከመ ፣ ኢይትከሠት ፡ ግብርየ ፡ ለመኑሂ ፡ እስ መ ፡ አልብየ ፡ ክሂለ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጾር ፡ ክብረ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አላ ፡ ዘእቤለከ ፡ ግበር ፡ ጻእ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፣ ዘይቤለከ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ወምስሌከ ፡ ከህናቲከ ፣ ወሕዝብከ ፡ ወምስሌክሙሂ ፡ ወንጌላት ፣ ወመስቀላት ፣ ወማእጠንታት ፡ ወዕጣን ፡ ወሰምዕ ፡፡ ወይቁ ም ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሰራዊቱ ፣ ወኩሉ ፡ ሕዝቡ ፡ በ8ጽንፍ ፡ አንተ ፣፡ ወሕዝበ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ በ ፳ጽንፍ ፡ ወአነ ፡ እቀውም ፡ ድኅሬከ ፡ በማአከለ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወኢያእመረኒ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ወ አስተብሞዑ ፡ ኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወጽርሩ ፡ ወበሉ ፣ ኪርያላይሶን ፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ እግ
accomplish what you have told me, but may the will of the Lord be done through the prayer of Our Lady Mary and may your prayer help the people of the Christians. I ask you, O my Father, * that my work not be manifested to anyone, for I do not have the power to bear the glory of the world. But do what I will tell you. Go to the mountain that the king indicated to you. Let your priests and your people be with you. Let there be with you also the gospels, the crosses, the censers, the incense, and the candles. Let the king, his troops, and all his people stand on one side, and you yourself and your own people on another side. I myself will stand behind you, in the midst of the people, and no one will recognize me. Supplicate the


[p. 623] {#po.15.p623}
ዚኦ ፡ መሐረነ ፡ ሻወ8ጊዜ ፡፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አዝዞሙ ፣ ለሕዝብ ፡ ያርምሙ ፡ ወስግድ ፡ ወ ይስግዱ ፡ ወአነሂ ፣ እሰግድ ፣ ምስሌክሙ ። ወግበር ፡ ከመዝ ፣ የጊዜ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ተዓት ብ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ማሕየዊ ፡ አንጻረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ነሥኦሙ ፡፣ ለኤኢጴስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ወለካህናት ፣ ወለመነኮሳት ፣ ወለሕዝብ ፡ ወለመሃይምናን ፡፣ ወበጽሑ ፡፥ ኀበ ፣ ንጉ ሥ = ወወፅአ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሉ ፡ ሰራዊት ፡ ምስሌሁ ፣ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ምስር፣ ወቃህራ ፡ ወሰረረ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ደብር ፣ ወቆመ ፡ አብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፣ አብ ርሃም ፣ ወመሃይምናን ፤ ምስሌሁ ፡፣ በ8ጽንፍ ። ወቆመ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡፣ ወሰራዊቱ ፣ ወዙሎ ሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ካልእ ፣ ጽንፍ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለዩ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወመሃይምና ን ፡ ወስምዖን ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወጸርተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ኪርያላይሶን ፡ነዊኃ ፡ ጊዜ፣
Lord, cry out and say: Kyrie eleison, that is to say: O Lord, have pity on us, forty-one times. Then order the people to be silent. Prostrate yourself and let them prostrate themselves. I too will prostrate myself with you. Do this three times, making the sign of the life-giving cross in front of the mountain." 15. - When the patriarch had heard this, he took the bishops, the priests, the monks, the people, and the faithful. They arrived toward the king. The king left. All his troops were with him, as well as all the people of Old Cairo (Misr) and Cairo (Qahra). He climbed the bottom of the mountain. The Father patriarch Abba Abraham and the faithful stood with him on one side. The king, his troops, and all the people stood on the other side. Then the patriarch and the faithful prayed. * Simon was with them. They cried out, saying: Kyrie eleison, for


[p. 624] {#po.15.p624}
ወሰገዱ ፣ ፻ጊዜ ፡ ወለለ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይሰግዱ ፣ ያነሥአ ፡ አብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አዕ ይንቲሁ ፤ ኀበ ፣ ሰማይ ፣ ወየዓትብ ፣ በትእምርተ ፣ መስቀል ፣፤ ላዕለ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወይፈል ስ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወየዐርግ ፣ ኅበ ፡ አየር ፣ በቅድመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ ወሶበ ፣ ይሰግ ድ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወመሃይምናን ፡ ይወርድ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካኑ ፡ ወገብ ሩ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ቦጊዜ ወሶበ ፣ ርእየ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ወዙሎሙ ፡ ተንባላት ፡፣ ዘንተ ፡ተ ኣምራተ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወጸርጐ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ወፈርሁ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፍርሃተ ፡=፡
a long time. They prostrated themselves three times. Each time they prostrated themselves, the Father patriarch raised his eyes toward heaven and made the sign of the cross on the mountain. The mountain moved and rose into the air before all the people. When the patriarch prostrated himself, as well as the faithful, the mountain descended to its place. They did this three times. When the king and all the Muslims saw this miracle, they were extremely astonished. They cried out much and were seized with great fear.
--' ወአብጽሖ ፤ ንጉሥ ፣ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወአክበሮ ፡ፈ ድፋዶ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይትመነይ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘይፈቀድ ፤ ወኢፈቀደ ፡ ይኅሥሥ ፡እ ምኔሁ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ።፡ ወሶበ ፣ አገበሮ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለሊቀ ፣ ጳጳሳት ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሊቀ፣
16. - The king had the patriarch Abba Abraham brought. He honored him extremely and asked him to manifest to him the desire of what he would like to obtain. But he did not want to ask him for anything. When the king had forced the patriarch, the

patriarch said to him: "I want to rebuild the churches and especially the church of Saint Mercurius (Margoryos) in Old Cairo." He ordered him to rebuild the churches that he wanted and he gave him, in abundance, money from the royal palace. The patriarch bowed before him and said to him: "May the Lord prolong your days and strengthen your kingship. As for me, I do not want money." When he had said this to him, he loved him extremely, because he had seen his holiness and his contempt for the goods of this world. Then he mounted his * horse, with his troops. He stood with him during the reconstruction of the church of Saint Mercurius. When wicked people tried to prevent him from rebuilding it, he stood with him, until he had rebuilt it. This Father rebuilt many churches throughout the land of Egypt.

17. - When he had finished his good fight and had pleased the Lord, he died in peace, after having sat on the seat of Mark (Margos), the evangelist, for three years and six months. It is said, in the life of this Father, that the secretary whom he had excommunicated because of the concubines made him a poison and killed him. This saint went toward the Lord. All the people lamented for him. May the prayer and the blessing of this Father, the patriarch Abba Abraham, be with us. Amen. 3. holy virgin Ripsime ('Arsima), martyr, the translation of the bodies of the holy virgins * and the translation of her body and the bodies of the twenty-seven holy martyrs who were put to death with her. 2. - King Dertades said to this Ripsime: "Do you not have your heart? Life is so great! You will remain with us." She said to him: "The things of heaven are greater and are better." He ordered her to be led to the atrium, to be stripped naked there, and to have her neck cut. Her neck was cut. She received the crown of martyrdom and inherited the kingdom of heaven. May her prayer and her blessing be with her dear Za-Manfas-Qedous for centuries of centuries. Greeting to the translation of your body, in the praise of a numerous assembly, To the place where you are laid, Ripsime, until the resurrection takes place. 1. - On this day died the holy Father Matthew (Mâtéwos), the poor. 2. - This saint was superior of the monastery of the city of Syene (‘Eswân). He practiced a beautiful asceticism. He had numerous virtues. He cast out demons from men. The Lord gave him the grace of healing the sick. * Every being who was sick near him and whom one brought to him, Saint Matthew prayed over him and the Lord healed him by his prayer.

3. - One day, they brought to him a woman who had a secret illness. He knew her deed through the Holy Spirit. He ordered her to reveal her sin before the people who were there. She confessed that she herself had been married to two brothers. That is why the Lord had afflicted her with this illness. The saint prayed to the Lord. The earth opened its mouth and devoured her. She was an example for whoever heard speak of her.

II. - Revision or Vulgate. 2. Death of Abba Daniel of Dabra-Sihat. In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. The 7th of Tâhschâsch. 1. - On this day died Abba Daniel (Dân'êl) of Dabra-Sihat, of the convent of Abba Macarius (Magärès).


[p. 634] {#po.15.p634}
እስመ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ አባ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ዘገነዛ ፡ ለጳጥሪቃ ፣ ንግሥት ፣ ዘሠመ የ ፡ ስማ ፡ አንስጥዮስ ፤ ወኢተአምረት ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ብእሲት ፡ ይእቲ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ምታ።
2. - It is this Father Daniel who buried the queen * Patricia (Pâtrigâ), who was called by the name of 'Anestyos. In fact, she herself was known as a woman only to her husband.
፦ ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወበ ጺሖ ፡ ፍና ፡ ሰርክ ፡ ረከበ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ አቡድ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ መርካ ፡ ወይተልውዎ ፡ ብዙኃ ን ፡ አቡዳን ። ወለሰብአ ፡ ሀገርሰ ፣ ይመስሎሙ ፡ እቡደ ። ወአኃዘ ፡ አባ ፣ ዳንኤል ፡እ ዴሁ ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወነገሮ ፣ ትሩፋቲሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ አምሐልዎ ፡ ነገ ሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፣ ጐየ ፡ እምፀብዓ ፤ ዝሙት ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ አብደ ፡ ወሰሚዖሙ፣ሰ ብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡፡
3. - One day when he was going with his disciple to Alexandria (‘Eskendryâ), evening having arrived, he met a mad man, called Markä. Many madmen followed him. To the people of the city he seemed mad. Abba Daniel took his hand and led him to the patriarch, to whom he explained his virtues. When they had made him swear, he explained to them that he had fled far from the war of debauchery and that he had made himself mad. Having heard this, they glorified the Lord.
፦ ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ በጺሖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ሀገር ፡ ረከበ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ አረጋ ዌ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አውሎጊስ ፡ ዘይወቅር ፡ እብነ ፡ ወይሠይጥ ፡ ለኅብስት ፣ ወይትዌከፍ ፡ ነ ዳያነ ። ወአብኦ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ፣ ወተወክፎ ፡ በፍሥሓ ፡ወሶበ፣ርእየ ፡ ሠናይቶ ፡ ሰአለ ፡ እምእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ለአውሎጊስ ፡ በዘይትዌከ
4. - One day, having arrived in a city, he found an old man, called Eulogius ('Awlogis), who cut stones, sold them for bread, and received the poor. This man brought Abba Daniel into his house and received him with joy. When Abba Daniel had seen his beneficence, he asked the Lord to give money to Eulogius, with which he would receive the poor. Our Lord

appeared to him in a dream, in the guise of a child, and said to him: "Do you yourself vouch for him, affirming that he will not change his way of doing things?" Abba Daniel said to him: "Yes, I vouch for him." Then Eulogius found a large pot filled with gold. || He went to the king. He was appointed judge. || He abandoned his way of doing things. 5. - When Abba Daniel learned this, he went to the city of the king, in order to reprimand Eulogius. The king's soldiers struck him and brought him to the point of death. * While he was saddened by this, the same child appeared to him in a dream, ordered him to hang him, and said to him: "Why did you enter into the affairs of others?" Then came Our Lady Mary. She kissed the feet of the child and saved Abba Daniel and Eulogius as well.

6. - When another king reigned, this one wanted to kill him. He fled to another region, being afraid. Having arrived at his own country, he returned to his works of old. 7. - One day when he was going on the road under the light of the moon, he met a woman on a mountain. body. She had been dwelling there for thirty-eight years, practicing asceticism. She explained all her mystery to him and died. 8. - One day, they brought the letter of Leo (Leyon). They read it before the people. Abba Daniel leaped up. He tore the letter of Leo and said: "Anathema be the faith of Chalcedon (Këlgêdon)." Having heard these words, the soldiers struck him much and drove him from his convent.

9. - One day, he arrived at some virgins. He knocked at the door. Having known that it was Abba Daniel, they opened for him and went to meet him with joy. One of them had made herself mad and was lying at the door. Abba Daniel asked about her. The superior of the monastery said to him: "She is a madwoman." Abba Daniel explained to her that she was a saint and an ascetic. During the night, she wrote a letter, saying: "Forgive me, holy ones, for having angered you." Having said this, * she disappeared. Having learned this, the virgins were very sad and repented.

will make myself like Abba Daniel and immediately they will open for me." He did as he had thought. He said to them: "I am Abba Daniel." Having heard, the nuns went to meet him with joy, washed his feet, and uncovered their faces. One of them was blind. When she had washed her face with the water of the footbath, her eyes opened. Seeing this, the nuns said to the chief of the brigands: "You are blessed, Abba Daniel." The chief of the brigands, upon seeing this, repented and became a monk under Abba Daniel. 11. - Abba Daniel remained in great asceticism. When the moment of his death approached, an angel of the Lord explained it to him. He was a little sick. He prayed to Christ and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with our king ᾿Ιγᾶβοιι for ages of ages. Hail to you, prince of the blessed priests of Dabra-Sihat, Daniel. You were clothed in palm leaves,


[p. 638] {#po.15.p638}
እንዘ ፡ ትሴፈዋ ፡ ለትርሲተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ጽጊት ። ዘኢያድምፅዋ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ክቡራን ፡ ነገሥት ። ዕበ ፡ ሰአለ ፡ ኀቤኪ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ስመኪ ፡ ይሰሚ። በእደ ፡ አውሎጊስ ፡ ማሕመሚ ። አፅጋረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትስዕሚ ። በከመ ፡ አድኃንኪ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ቀዳሚ ። አድኅኖትየ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ድግሚ ፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ተዝክራ ፡ ለዲ"ዮንጥርስ ፣ ብፅዕት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
Waiting for the adornment of the heaven in flowers, Which illustrious kings do not obtain on earth. When Daniel had prayed to you, invoking your name, For Eulogius who was causing him affliction, You kissed for him the feet of Christ. Just as you saved the soul of Daniel of old, Renew, Mary, my salvation. 3. Memory and saläm of the blessed Diyonterès. On this day also is the memory * of Diyonterès, the blessed. May the Lord have mercy, through her prayer, on our king 'Iyâsou for ages of ages.


[p. 639] {#po.15.p639}
ሰላም ፥ ለዲዮንጥርስ ፡ እምገቢረ ፡ ኅሩም ፡ ወአበሳ ። ዘአብደረት ፡መጥዎ ፡ ነፍሳ ። በምክረ ፡ዳንኤል ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ መንኩሰ ፣ ዳንኤል ፡ ነቢየ ፥ ሱሳ ። ሶበ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ በዊኦ ፡ መቅደሳ ። ሐመረ ፣፤ ሥጋሁ ፣ አንጽሐ ፡ ለፍትወት ፡ እምርኩሳላ ፡፡
Hail to Diyonterès, who, rather than doing what is forbidden and is sin, Preferred to render her soul. Upon the counsel of the blessed Daniel, the chief of the brigands became a monk and took the name of Daniel, the prophet of Susa (Sousä). When this man had prayed, after having entered the sanctuary, He purified the vessel of his flesh from the impurity of concupiscence. 4. Death of Abba Matthew of Dabra-"Aswân.

1. - On this day also died the illustrious Father Abba Matthew (Mâtêwos), ascetic of 'Asafoni, superior of the famous monastery of Dabra-'Aswân. 2. - This saint was raised, from his childhood, in the fear of the Lord. He became a monk in one of the convents. He became an ascetic through fasting and prayer. They built for him a dwelling in the desert. He made, with his hands, good baskets and gave the price he received from them as alms to the poor, as his disciples Abba Serapion (Saräbyon) and Abba Hedrâ have explained, in recounting his work, his life, his miracles, and his wonders. 3. - They themselves have recounted, saying: "One day when we were sitting outside the door of his dwelling in the desert, we heard a voice inside the dwelling, as if someone were conversing with another. When we had entered, we found no one, except our Father alone. We said to him: "O our Father, behold we

ዘይዛዋዕ ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቦእነ ፡ ኢረከብነ ፡ ዘእንበሌከ ፡ ወይቤለነ ፡ በብዝኃ ፡ ትሕ ትናሁ ፡ ወፍድፋዴ ፡ ሠናይቱ ፡ ኦደቂቅየ ፣ አንሰ ፣ ተዘከርኩ ፡ ኃጣውእየ ፣ ወተዛ ዋዕኩ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ርእስየ ፡ ወአዘከርክዋ ፡ ለነፍስየ ፡ ኩነኔያተ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢየኃልፋ ፡ ወፃዕ ረ ፣ ሞትሂ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትረክበኒ ። አሌ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ለማቴዎስ ፡ ነዳይ ፡ አመ ፡ ያዓርቅዎ ፡ አ ልባሰ ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ፡ ወያቀውምዎ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ መንበረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፡ ማእከ ለ ፡ መዓርገ ፣ መላእክት ፣፤ ሰማያውያን ፡ ወማኅበረ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ አለ ፡ የዓቅቡ ፡ ንጽ ሐ ፡ ወለበስዎ ፣ ከመ ፣ ልብስ ። ወዘበከመዝ ፡ ነገር ፡፣ መዓዶሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያ ቬክሮሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ኃጥኣን ፡ ወዕሜተ ፣ ጻድቃን ።
heard outside, as if there were someone who was conversing with you. When we entered, we found only you." He said to us in the greatness of his humility and the eminence of his virtue: "O my children, as for me, I remembered my sins and * I conversed with myself. I recalled to my soul the torments that will not pass and also the torment of death that will reach me. Woe to Matthew, the poor, when they strip him of the garments that are upon him and make him stand before the throne of the living Lord, in the midst of the ranks of the heavenly angels and the assembly of the just, who have kept purity and have clothed themselves with it as with a garment." It is with such words that he exhorted his spiritual children, reminding them of the torment of sinners and the reward of the just.
፦ ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አቡነ ፡ አባ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ወተጋድሎቶ ፡

5. - The demons and the impure spirits frightened him day and night. He saw them face to face. They themselves ran before him and followed him in every place, under varied and diverse aspects. But our Father was not afraid of them. When he extended his hands and signed his forehead with the sign of the cross, at that moment the demons vanished and dispersed, like the smoke that is before the face of the wind.

እምነ ፡ አዝዕብት ፡ መፍርሃን ፡ ጥቀ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰክቡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሣጤ ፡ ማኅደር0፣ ወፈሪሆ ፡ ዓፀወ ፡ ኖኅተ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ናሕስ ።
beasts larger * than hyenas, very terrible, lying inside the dwelling. Being afraid, he closed the door and climbed onto the roof.
፦ ወናሁ ፡ አቡነ ፡ አባ ፣ ማቴዎስ ፣ መጽአ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ገ ዳም ፡ ወያነብብ ፡ መዝሙረ ። ወዕበ ፡ ርእዮ ፣ ለረድኡ ፡ አክሞሰሰ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦሰራ ብዮን ፡ ለምንት ፣፡ ኢቦአከ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደር ፡ እስከ ፣ እመጽእ ፣ ኀቤከ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፣ ረከብኩ ፣ በውሣጤ ፡ ማኅደር ፡ ፪አራዊተ ፡ዓበይተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰክቡ ፣ ወፈሪ ሀየ ፡ ኪያሆሙ ፡ ኢቦእኩ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ የዋህ ፡ ወንጹሐ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ እመነኒ ፡ ኦ ወልድየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ፲ወ፪አመት ፡ እንዘ ፡ እነብር ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ።፡ ወአነ ፡ ው እቱ ፣ ዘሐፀንክዎሙ ፡ ወዘንተኒ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ወማየ ፡ ዘአዘዝኩከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታምጽእ ፡ በእ ንቲአሆሙ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ዕበ ፡ አርኃወ ፣ ፕኅተ ፣ መጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ፪አራዊት ፡ ወለሐሱ ፡ ሰኩናሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ መስተባይፃነ ፡ በተጋንዮ ፡ ዐቢይ ። ወ ከዓወ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ማየ ፡ እምነ ፡ ቀሱት ፡ ወሰትዩ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፣
7. - Behold our Father Abba Matthew came, walking in the desert and reciting psalms. When he saw his disciple, he smiled and said to him: "O Serapion, why did you not enter the dwelling, until I came to you?" Serapion said to him: "O my Father, I found inside the dwelling two large beasts, which were lying down. Having been afraid of them, I did not enter." The gentle and pure-souled old man said to him: "Believe me, O my son, for here for twelve years I have been dwelling with them. It is I who have raised them. The bread and the water that I ordered you to bring, it is for them." Then, when he opened the door, the two beasts came toward him and licked the heels of the saint. They were for him companions, with great submission. He poured the water from the pitcher for them. They drank. Then they left for the desert and went away.
ወሑሩ። -፦ ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አባ ፣ ማቴዎስ ፡ ነበረ ፡ እንዘ › ይገብር ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ እስከ ፡ መልአ ፡ ስሙዓቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መካን ። ወኮኑ ፡ ያመጽኡ ፣ ኀቤሁ ፣ ዙሎ ፣ ድው ያነ ፤ በዘዘዚአሁ ፣ ደዌሆሙ ፤ ወይፌውሶዕሙ ፡ በኃይለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንተ ፡ ሀ ለወት ፡ ምስሌሁ ።
8. - This Abba Matthew continued to perform miracles, until his fame had filled every place. They brought toward him all sorts of sick people, afflicted with various illnesses. He healed them by the power of the Lord who was with him.


[p. 644] {#po.15.p644}
፦ ፳እምነ ፡ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ወአሐተ ፡ ፅለተ ፡፤ አምጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ብእሴ ፣ በሐ መ ፡፤ ዘቦቱ ፡ ጋኔን ። ወሶበ ፡ ነጸሮ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፣ ከልሐ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ቃል ፡ ወነጽሖ ፡ ለ ብእሲ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ። ወጸ”ለየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ዘይት ፡ ወዓተበ ፡ በትእምርተ ፣ መስቀል ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፳አምላክ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ድኅ ነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እምዝኩ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወገብአ ፣-ልቡ ፣ ወሰዓመ ፡ አእጋረ ፣ቅዱ
9. - Here is one of his miracles. One day, they brought toward him a mute man, who had a demon. When the demon had seen the saint, he cried with a loud voice and threw the man to the ground. The saint * prayed over the oil and made the sign of the cross in the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. Immediately this man was delivered from this demon. His intelligence returned. He kissed the feet of the saint and went away, rejoicing.
ስ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ። ወዕበ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ሕዝባውያን ፡ ያቄርብ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ማእደ ፡ ወይፌ ንዎሙ ፣ በሰላም። ወሶዕበ ፣ ይወስዱ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ በረከተ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲሆ ሙ ፡ ወያነብርዎ ፡ላዕለ፣ ድውያን ፡ወይትፌወሱ ፡ ሶቤሃ፣ወይሴብሕዎ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ።
10. - When laypeople came toward him, he offered the Eucharistic sacrifice for them and sent them away in peace. When they carried into their houses a small object that he had blessed and placed it on the sick, they were healed immediately and glorified the Lord.


[p. 645] {#po.15.p645}
ክልእ ፡ ተኣምር ፣ ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ‹፡ ወለተ ፡ ሠናይ ተ ፡ ላሀይ፣ ጥቀ ፡ ዘባቲ ፡ ጋኔን ፣ ወብዙኃ ፣ ጊዜያተ ፣ ትሠጥጥ ፡ አልባሲሃ ፡ ወኢይ ቀርባ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ኃዘን ፡ ወገዓር ። ወጸለየ ፡ አባ ፡ ማቴዎስ፡ላዕለ፣ ዘይተ ፡ ቀንዲል ፡ ወቀብዓ ፡ ለወለት ፡ በስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወተፈወሰት። ሣልስ ፡ ተኣምር ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ኃጥ እተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ወድቀት ፡ በኃጢአት ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ዘእኪት ፡ ጥቀ ። ወሶዕበ ፡ ፀንሠት ፡ ወበ ጽሐ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ወሊዶታ ፡ ዓፀባ ፡ ወሊድ ፡ ወተመንደበት ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ። ወይቤ ላ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኦወለትየ ፡ ተአመኒ ፡ ኃጢአተኪ ፡ ወኢተሐስዊ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔ ር ። ወትቤሎ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ነበርኩ ፣ እንዘ ፣ እሰክብ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፪አኃው ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ያእም ር ፡ መኑሂ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፀነስኩ ፡ አድኃፅኩ ፡ በሥራይ ፡፣ ሕፃነ ፣ ወወሀብክዎ ፡ ለከለባ ት ፡ ወሰሚዖ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ጸለ"የ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ አብቀወት ፡ ምድር ፣ አፉሃ ፡ ወውሕወዉታ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ አርአያ ፡ ለካልኣን ።
11. - Second miracle. One day, they brought toward him a young girl, very pretty, who had a demon. Many times, she had torn her clothes. No one among the people approached her. Her parents were in great sadness and in groans. Abba Matthew prayed over the oil of the lamp and anointed the young girl in the name of Christ. She was healed. 12. - Third miracle. One day, they brought toward him a sinful woman who had fallen into a great sin, which was very bad. When she was pregnant and the day of childbirth arrived, it was difficult for her to give birth; she was tormented day and night. The saint said to her: "O my daughter, confess your sin and do not lie before the Lord." She said to him: "As for me, I continued to sleep with two brothers, without anyone knowing. When I was pregnant, I aborted, by means of a drug, the child and I gave it to the dogs." Having heard this, the saint prayed * to the Lord. Immediately the earth opened its mouth and devoured her. She was an example for the others.


[p. 646] {#po.15.p646}
ወብዙኅ ፣ ተኣምራቲሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ኢየኃልቅ ፡ በተና ግሮ ፡ ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ያንሶሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ሰሐበቶ ፡፣ ዝዕብ ፡ አልባ ሲሁ ፡ በአስናኒሃ ፡ ወአርአየቶ ፣ እጐሊሃ ፡ አለ ፣ ወድቁ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ዓዘቅት ። ወሶዕበ ፡ አውፅአ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ሰገደት ፡ ወለሐሰት ፡ ሰኩና ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወሖረት ። -፦ ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፅረፍቱ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ራእየ ፡ አመ ፡ ለወርኃ ፡ › ታኅሣሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይጹውዕዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከብካበ ፡ መርዓዊ ፡ አበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ወአባ ፣ መቃርስ ፣ ወአባ ፣ ጳኩሚስ ፣ ወአባ ፣ ቴዎድሮስ ፣ ወአባ፣ሙ ሴ ፡ ጸሊም ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ሲኖዳ ። ወይቤልዎ ፣ ሠናይ ፣ ምጽአትከ ፡ ኅቤነ ፣ ከመ ፤ ትኅድር ፤ ምስሌነ ፣ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት ፣ ወአብጽሕዎ ፡ ኅበ ኖኅተ፣0፣ ጽርሕ ፤ ዐቢይ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ገነታት ፣ ወመናብርት ። ወሰምዓ ፡ ቃለ ፣ ዘይብል ፡ አርኅዉ፡ ፤! ጥኃተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ማቴዎስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ወተመጠወ ፡ ፻አክሊላተ ፡፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፣ ዓለም ።
13. - Numerous are the miracles of this saint. However, I will not fail to recount this one. One day when he was walking in the desert, a hyena pulled his clothes with its teeth. It showed him its young that had fallen into a well. When he had pulled them out for it, it prostrated itself before him, licked the heels of his feet, and went away. 14. - Then when the moment of his death approached, he had a vision, on the 3rd of the month of Tâähschâsch: it was as if the holy Fathers Abba Anthony (‘Entonyos), Abba Macarius (Magäârès), Abba Pachomius (Pâkuemis), Abba Theodore (Téwodros), Abba Moses (Mousê), the Black, and Saint Abba Sinodà were calling him to the wedding of the Bridegroom. They were saying to him: "Good be your coming toward us, to dwell with us in the heavenly Jerusalem (lyarousälêm)." They brought him to the door of a great palace that had gardens and thrones. He heard a voice that said: "Open the doors, so that Matthew may enter." Then he died. He received three crowns. May the Lord have mercy, through his prayer, on our king 'Iyâsou for ages of ages.


[p. 647] {#po.15.p647}
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ለዘሱታፌሁ ፡ አሐዱ ። ምስለ ፡ አብ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ወልዱ። ማቴዎስ ፡ ነዳይ ፡ እምፍትወተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወእምፈቃዱ ። እምብገኃ ፣ ጊሩቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ለመዱ። ክሌ አራዊተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ያበልዕ ፡ እጊዞ ፡ በእዱ።
I bid farewell to Matthew, the poor, who lived in communion with the Father and with the Son, far from the concupiscence of the world and its desire. Because of the greatness of his virtue, he gave food to the wild beasts that lived familiarly with him, taking the food with his hand.


[p. 648] {#po.15.p648}

### THE 8TH OF TAHSAS

| - Primitive recension. 1. Death of Abba Hierocles, Patriarch of Alexandria. In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. 1. - On this day died the holy Father Hierocles (Yäroklä), patriarch


[p. 649] {#po.15.p649}
ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፣ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፣ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፲ወ፻ ፡
of the city of Alexandria ('Eskendryä). He is the thirteenth in the number of the patriarch Fathers.
ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮኑ ፣ አበዊሁ ፣ ከሓድያነ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ወለድ ዎ ፡ አምኑ ፡ ወተጠምቁ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ።፡፡ ወኮኑ ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ይአመኑ ፡ መ ሀርዎ ፣ ለወልይሙ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ አረማዊያን ፡ ወሶበ ፣ አምኑ ፡መሀርዎ ፡ ጥበበ ፣ ክርስ ቲያን ፡ ወአጽንፅዎ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወንጌለ ፡ ወሐዋርያተ።
2. - The parents of this holy Father were infidels. But after they had borne him, they believed and were baptized with the Christian baptism. Before believing, they had taught their son the wisdom of the pagans. But when they believed, * they taught him the wisdom of the Christians. They made him learn by heart the books of the Church: the Gospel and the Epistles.
፦- ወእምዝ ፡ ሜሞ ፡ ድሜጥርዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዲያቆነ ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሜሞ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወዐቀበ ፣ መርዔ ቶ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ፡ ወፈጸመ ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ወሕገ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡፡ ፦ ወቦበ ፣ አፅረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ድሜጥርዮስ ፣ ኀረይዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡
3. - Then the patriarch Demetrius (Demêtryos) ordained him deacon. Afterwards he ordained him priest in a church of the city of Alexandria. Abba Hierocles kept his flock in good vigilance. He fulfilled his task and the law of the Church. 4. - When Abba Demetrius died, this Father was elected and instituted


[p. 650] {#po.15.p650}
ሊቀ ፡ጳጳሳተ ፡ ላፅለ ፥፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወዐቀበ ፡ መርዔቶ ፣ ለክርስቶስ ፡ በሠና ይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ፣ ወአጽንዖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፣ ርትዕት ።፡ ወሜጦሙ ፣ ለብዙኃን ፣ አረማዊያን-፣፤ ወአብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡፣ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወመጠዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዲዮናስዮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ፍትሐ ፡ በ ማእከለ ፣ መሃይምናን ፡ ወካነ ፣፤ ቅዱስ ፣ ያሮክላ ፡ ይሜህሀሮሙ ፣ ወይጌስጾሙ ፡ ወ ይመይጦሙ ፡ ለዓላዊያን ፡ ወይመርሖሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ያበውኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖ
patriarch of the city of Alexandria. He kept the flock of Christ in good vigilance and strengthened his faithful in the right faith. He converted many pagans, brought them into the faith of Our Lord Christ, and baptized them with the Christian baptism. [He] charged Saint Dionysius (Diyonäsyos) to establish discipline among the faithful. Saint Hierocles taught, reproved, converted the infidels, and directed them until he had brought them into the right faith.
ት፣ርትዕት። -፦ ወነበረ ፣ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፣ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ፲ወ፻ ፣ ዓመተ ፣ ወአዕረፈ፣ በሰላም = እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፣ አሜን
5. - He sat on the see of Mark (Märgos), the evangelist, for thirteen years. He died in peace. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer. Amen.

2. Martyrdom of the holy Barbara and Juliana. 1. - On this day also are the holy Barbara (Barbärä) and Juliana (Yolyânä), martyrs.
ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ በርባራ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ወለቱ ፣ ለብእሲ ፡ መስፍን ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ባእ ል ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ዘእምብሔረ ፡-ምስራቅ ፣ ወስሙ ፡፤ ድዮስቆርዮ ስ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፣ ለመክስምያኖስ ፡ ከኃዲ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ዲዮስቆርዮስ ፡ ለ"ወለቱ ፡ በር በራ ፡ ማኅፈደ ፡ ጽኑአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይርአያ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ግሙረ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅፈድ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ብለኔ ፡ ወያርህዉ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ፪መሳኩተ ፡=፡ ወሶ በ፣ ርእየት ፣፡ ቅድስት ፣ በርባራ ፣ ፪መሳኩተ ፣ አዘዘቶሙ ፡ ለነዳቅያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርኅዉ ፡፤
2. - This Saint Barbara was the daughter of a great prince, extremely rich, of the royal house of a country of the East. He was called Dioscorus (Dyosqoryos). He lived in the days of Maximian (Maksemyânos), the impious. Dioscorus had a fortified tower built for * his daughter Barbara, so that no one might ever see her. Then he ordered a bathroom to be made in the tower and two windows to be opened there. When Saint Barbara saw the two windows, she ordered


[p. 652] {#po.15.p652}
ካልዕተ ፣ መስኮተ ። ወእምዝ ፥ ገብረት ፣ ላዕለ ፣ ማኅፀበ ፡ ማይ ፡ ትእምርተ ፣ መስ ቀል ፡ ማኅየዊ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፦ ክርስቶስ ፡፡

፦ ወሶበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ አቡሓ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማኅፈድ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ዘገብሩ ፡ነደ ቅት ፡ ወተአደዉ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፣፡ ወተስእአለ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ወነገርዎ ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እስመ፡ወ ለትከ ፡ በርባራ ፦ ይእቲ ፡ አዘዘት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ተስእለ ፡ እምኔሃ ፣ ወይቤላ ፣ ለም ንት ፡ ገበርኪ ፣ ዘንተ ፣ ወትቤሎ ፡ አዕምር ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ እስመ ፡፣ ስሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ይትፌጸም ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብር ወአነ ፡ ገበርኩ ፡ የመሳኩተ ፡ በስመ ፡ ስሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወ ዘንተ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ በአምሳለ ፣፡ መስቀሉ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ተመየጥ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፣ እምስሕተትከ ፣ ወአምልኮ ፡ ለአ
3. - When her father entered the tower, he saw what the builders had done, who had transgressed his orders. | He questioned them about this. They told him and said to him: "Your daughter Barbara herself ordered this." When he questioned her and said to her: "Why have you done this?", she said to him: "Know, O my father, that it is by the Holy Trinity that every work is accomplished. I myself had these three windows made in the name of the Holy Trinity and this cross in the image of the cross of Our Lord Jesus Christ, by whom the salvation of the whole world has come about. Convert yourself, O my father, from your error and adore the God who created you."
ምላክ ፡ ዘፈጠረከ ። ፦ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፣ ተምዓ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወተቁጥዓ ፡
4. - When her father had heard these words, he became extremely angry

and indignant against her. He drew his sword and ran after her. There was a rock in front of her. This rock split. She entered it and the rock covered her. 5. - Afterwards she returned to her father. Her father took her and delivered her to the judges, who inflicted great torture upon her. | There was there * a woman called Juliana. She saw Saint Barbara, who was in the midst of tortures; she wept over her. Our Lord Christ appeared to Saint Barbara, strengthened her, and consoled her. 6. - Afterwards her father ordered that her head be cut off and that the head of Juliana, her companion, be cut off. Their heads were cut off. They received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Fire descended from heaven and consumed her father as well as Marcian (Marqyânos), the judge. 7. - The pool dug in the bathroom, upon which was


[p. 654] {#po.15.p654}
ወዲቤሁ ፡ትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ኮነ፣ ውስቴቱ ፡ ፈውሰ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘይደዊ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይትኃፀብ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡፡
the image of the cross, became a source of healing for every sick person who bathed in it.

8. - Then they took the bodies of the saints and placed them in a church outside the city called Galälyä. The body of Saint Barbara is now in the church of the saints Abba Cyrus (Qiri) and John (Yohannès), martyrs, in the city of Cairo (Mesr). May their prayer and their blessing be with us. Amen.

3. - This Samuel was pure, from his childhood, like Samuel the prophet. He maintained the desire for monasticism in his heart at all times. One day, he found the opportunity to become a monk. | He left his father's house to go to the desert of Scetis ('Asgêtès). | He did not know the way. An angel of the Lord made himself look like a monk to him, appeared to him, and said: "Where are you going?" Saint Abba Samuel said to him: "As for me, I want to go to the monastery of Scetis." The angel who looked like a monk said to him: "I too am going there." They went together on the road until they reached the monastery of Scetis.

4. - The angel of the Lord handed him over to an old monk, called Abba Agathon ('Agâton), who lived in a cave and was himself extremely righteous. The angel of the Lord had explained to Saint Abba Agathon about Abba Samuel and had said to him: "Receive Samuel with joy and clothe him with the garment of the holy robe of monasticism. This one will become a true son to you; he will strengthen your old age. You yourself teach him all the monastic laws." When the angel had said this to Abba Agathon, he disappeared from him.

into the church, he prayed over the hair shirt, the belt, the cowl, and the monastic robe. He clothed him with it, saying: "May the God of my holy Fathers Abba Anthony ('Entonès) and Abba Macarius (Magärès) be with you, O my son Samuel. May He be for you a helper in all your torments." Saint Abba Agathon taught Abba Samuel humility and silence. Abba Samuel said at all times: "Forgive me, O my Father, make friendship with me, and direct me." He prostrated himself before Abba Agathon. He said to him: "Remember me, O my holy and blessed Father, so that the Lord may make me capable of accomplishing His will." Saint Abba Samuel was instructed by his holy Father Abba Agathon in every monastic way. The Holy Spirit helped him in every work. Abba Samuel remained with the old man Abba Agathon for three years, obeying him in everything he ordered him. Then Saint Abba Agathon died.

6. - Saint Abba Samuel fasted twice a week. He practiced great asceticism. He was ordained priest in the church of Saint Abba Macarius of Scetis. A few days later, the letter of Leo (Leyon), the impious, from Chalcedon (Kalgëdon), was brought to the desert of Scetis and read. When the elder monks heard the exposition of the letter, they were extremely sad. Abba Samuel was * animated by the zeal of the Holy Spirit. He stood up in the midst of all the people, seized this impure letter, tore it, and said: "Anathema is whoever shall change the right faith of our holy Fathers."
ርትዕተ ፡ ዘኦበዊ፣ነ ፡ ቅዱላን ፡፡ ' ፦ ወዕበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ላእኩ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዝብጥዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ በድንቡሳት ፣፡ ዘሐጺን ፡፣ ወይስቅልዎ ፣ በመዝራዕቱ ፡ ወይጽፍአዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡፣ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ጸፍእዎ ፡
7. - When the king's minister saw this, he became extremely angry. He ordered the saint to be struck with iron hammers, to be suspended by his arms, and to be slapped on the face. They did all this to him. When they


[p. 660] {#po.15.p660}
ተመልሃ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ እምዓዕይንቲሁ ፡= ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስድድዎ ፡ እምደብረ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡
slapped him, one of his eyes was torn out. Then the minister ordered him to be driven out of the monastery of Scetis.
=- ወአስተርአዮ ፣ መልአከ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ሳሙኤል ፡ወ አዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ወይሕድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቀለሞን ፡ ወሖረ ፡ በጊዜሃ ፡ ወኅ ደረ ፡ በሕየ ወሶበ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሜሀሮሙ ፡ ለዙሎሙ ፡ ሰ ብእ ፡ከመ ፣ ይጽንዑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ወሰምዓ ፣ ዜናሁ ፣ መቆቀዝ ፣ ዘኮነ ፣፣ ሥዩመ ፣ መኩንነ ፡ ወሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ብ ሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ፣ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወቀሠፎ ፡ ቅሥፈተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወሰደዶ ፡ እምደብሩ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ወኅደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በገዳመ ፣
8. - An angel of the Lord appeared to Saint Abba Samuel and ordered him to go away and live in the monastery of Qalamon. He went away immediately and lived there. When he had lived there for a few days, teaching all the people to be firm in the right faith, the Magogaz, who had been instituted prefect and patriarch of the city of Alexandria ('Eskendryä) and of all the land of Egypt (Gebs), learned of his fame. He came to find Saint Abba Samuel. He scourged him with a great scourging. He drove him from his monastery. Abba Samuel went away and lived in a church, in the desert of Qalamon.
ቀለሞን ፡=፡ ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ መጽኡ ፡ በርባር ፡ አረማዊያን፣ ኅበ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ወነሥእዎ፣ ከመ ፡ ይስድዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያ
9. - In those days the pagan Berbers (Barbäâr) came there. They took him to take him to their country. He asked Our Lord Christ


[p. 661] {#po.15.p661}
ድኅኖ ፡ እምእዴሆሙ ፡፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይጽዕንዎ ፣ ዲበ ፣ ፳ገመል ፤ ኢይክል ፣ ውእ ቱ ፡ ገመል ፡ ይትሀወክ ፡ ግሙረ ፡ ወእምድኅ”ረ ፡ ዘበጥዎ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ወኅደግዎ ፡ ወ ሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡፡
to deliver him from their hands. When they had made him mount a camel, the camel could not move at all. * After they had beaten him a lot, they left him and went away to their country.
ወተመይጠ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ቀለሞን ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በሀየ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይጸመድ ፣፡ ወይትጋደል ፡ በፍኖተ ፡ ምንኩስና ።፡
10. - Saint Abba Samuel returned to his monastery of Qalamon. He remained there, practicing devotion and asceticism in the ways of monasticism.
-፦- ወእምዝ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ በርባር ፡ አረማዊያን ፡ምዕረ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ወፈርሃ ፡ እምኔ ሆሙ ፡ ወሰምዓ ፡ ቃለ ፣ እንዘ፣0፣ ይብል ፣ ኢትፍራህ ፤ ኦሳሙኤል ፡ አላ ፡ ሖር ፡ ወንበ ር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወኢትትናገር ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወአነ ፡ እሬስዮሙ ፡ ከ መ ፡ ኢይርአዩከ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ቦኡ ፡ በርባር ፣ አረማዊያን ፡ ውስት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአስይፍቲሆሙ ፡ ምሉሓት ፣፡ ውስተ ፣ እደዊሆሙ ፡
11. - Then the pagan Berbers came a second time. He was afraid of them. He heard a voice that said: "Do not be afraid, O Samuel, but go, remain in the church and do not speak with them. I myself will see to it that they do not see you." He remained thus. Afterwards the pagan Berbers entered the church. Their drawn swords were in their hands; they had weapons.


[p. 662] {#po.15.p662}
ወንዋየ ፡ ሐቅል ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ።፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ይኬልሑ ፡ በልሳኖሙ ፡ ዘመፍርሀ ፡ ፈ ድፋዶ ፳ ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይኔጽሮሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ በቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡፡
with them. They themselves uttered, in their own language, extremely terrible cries. The saint watched them committing sin in the sanctuary.
-፦ ወኢተክህሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይነጽር፣ ድፍረቶሙ ፡ በውስተ ፣ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ አ ላ፣ ተናገሮሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ምንትኑ-ዝዘትገብሩ ፡ ኦዓላዊያን ፡ እለ፣ አልብክሙ ፡ አም ልኮ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ የዓሥየክሙ ፡በከመ ፡ ምግባሪክሙ ፡ ኦርኩሳን ፡፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡እሙ ንቱ ፡ አረማዊያን ፡ አንተኑ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ትነብር ፡ ወንሕነኒ ፡ ኢንሬእየከ ።፡ ወአኃዝዎ ፡ ወአ ሰርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወቀሠፍዎ ፡ ቅሥፈተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ቀ ርበ ፡ ለመዊት ።= ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፣ ለመዊት ፡ ፈትሕዎ ፡ እማእሰሩ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ በገጹ ፣ ወኮነ ፡ ከመ፣ ምውት ወእምዝ ፡ ሰሐብዎ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ከሓድያን ፡ እስከ ፡ አብጽ"ሕ ዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካነ ፡ አግማላት ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይዘብጥዎ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ በአሣእኒሆሙ ፡=፡ ወእ
12. - It was not possible for him to watch their insolence in the sanctuary. But he spoke to them and said: "What are you doing, O infidels who have no piety? The Lord will repay you according to your works, O impure ones." The pagans said to him: "Do you dwell here? As for us, we do not see you." They seized him, tied him to a column of the church, and scourged him with a great scourging, to the point that he was near death. When he was near death, they untied his bonds. He fell on his face and became as one dead. Then the infidels dragged him until they * had brought him to a place where there were camels. They struck him on the head with their shoes. Then they made him mount a


[p. 663] {#po.15.p663}
ምዝ ፡ አጽዓንዎ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ገመል ፡ ወኢተክሀሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትሐወክ ፡ አእመካኑ ፡ ወዘበጥ ዎ ፡ ዝብጠተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡=፡
camel. But it was not possible for the camel to move from its place, even though they had struck it with great blows.
ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ይበኪ ፡ ብቫየ ፡ መሪረ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሕማ ሙ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገመል ፡ኅበ ቅዱስ ፣ ወተናገሮ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወይቤ ሎ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዘበጡከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይደልወከ ፡ ለመዊት ።፡፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ተዐደውከ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤለከ ፡ አርምም ፡ ወኢትትናገር ፡፡ ወሶበ ፣ ሰምዓ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ሳሙኤል ፡ በከየ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ በአማን ፡ አበስኩ ፡ አላ ፡ ከሓሊ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅድግ ፣ ሊተ ፡ ተዓድዎትየ ።፡
13. - Saint Abba Samuel shed bitter tears regarding his sufferings. The camel turned toward the saint, spoke to him in the language of men, and said to him: "You were struck justly: you deserve death. Indeed, you yourself transgressed the word of the Lord, your God, when He said to you: 'Be silent and do not speak.'" When Saint Abba Samuel heard these words, he shed bitter tears and said: "In truth, I have sinned, but the Lord is powerful enough to forgive me my transgression."
-፦- ወእምዝ ፡ ጴወውዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ እምቅድሜሁ ፡ ፄወው ዎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ዘገዳመ ፣ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወተራከቡ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡በ ሀየ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይትናዘዙ ፣ በበይናቲሆሙ ።፡
14. - Then the Berbers took him captive into their country. Before him, they had taken captive Abba John (Yohannes), superior of a convent in the desert of Scetis. Both met there. They consoled one another.
-፦ ወኮነ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ያጌብሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምልክ ፡ ፀሓየ ፡ ወኢሰምዓ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፡
15. - The master of Abba Samuel forced him to worship the sun, but


[p. 664] {#po.15.p664}
ግሙራ ።፡፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አሰረ ፡ እግሮ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግረ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ዓመት ፡ በምክረ ፡ ሰይጣ ን ፣ ወሰደዶሙ ፡ ውስት ፡ ገዳም ፡ ከመ ፡-ይዕቀቡ ፡ አግማላተ ፡ ወሐለየ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ከ መ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይወድቅ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ በኃጢአት ፣፡ ወይትኤዘዝ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዘይቤ ሎ ፡ በዘአመከሮ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወበዝንቱ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ይትዌሰክ ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ኃይል ፡ወጽንኝ ፡ ልብ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡፣ ደወየ ፡ እግዚኡ ፣ ወቀርበ ፣ ለመዊት ።፡ ወጸለየ ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፡፤ ቅዱስ ፣ "አባ ፣ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወተፈወሰ ፡ እምደዌሁ፡፡
he did not listen to his order at all. Then his master tied his foot to the foot of a servant girl, on the advice of Satan. He drove them into the desert, in order to guard the camels. His master thought that the saint would fall into sin with her and that he would obey him in everything he had told him, as the devil had advised him. In all these trials, the energy and courage of Saint Abba Samuel increased. He remained thus, until his master fell ill and was near death. Saint * Abba Samuel prayed over him and he was healed of his illness.
ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይደዊ ፡ ያመጽኡ ፡፤ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይጹሊ ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፣ ወይቀብኦ ፡ በዘይት ፡ ወይትፌወስ ፡ አምደዌሁ ፡=፡
16. - His fame spread throughout the country. They brought to him whoever was sick. He prayed over the sick person, anointed him with oil, and he was healed of his illness.


[p. 665] {#po.15.p665}
ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፣ ኃጢአትየ ፡ ዘገበርኩ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ወአፍቀሮ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘትፈቅድ ፡ ንግረኒ ፡ ወአነ ፡ እገብር ፡ ለከ ፡፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ አነ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ እግባእ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ደብርየ ።፡ ወሶቤሃ ፣ አስተዳለወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አ ምኃ ፡ ወንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ወአስተፋነዎ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ላእካነ ፡ እስከ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብሩ ፡=፡ ወተጋብኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፤ ውሉዱ ፡ ወበዝሩ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አአላፈ ፡፡ ወአስተርአየት ፣ ሎቱ ፣ እግዝእትነ ፣ ቅድስት ፣ ድንግል ፣ ማርያም ፡ ወትቤሎ ፣ እስ መ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ማኅደርየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ።፡ ወእምውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ኢተመይጡ፥፤ አረማዊያን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡5፡
17. - When his master saw this, he was extremely astonished. He prostrated himself before him and said to him: "Forgive me the sin that I have committed against you." He loved him extremely, asked him, and said to him: "Everything that you want, set it before me and I myself will do it for you." Saint Abba Samuel said to him: "I myself want to return to my monastery." Immediately he prepared for him many gifts and money. He took leave of him in peace. He sent servants with him, until Abba Samuel reached his monastery. 18. - His spiritual sons gathered around him. They were extremely numerous; they had become thousands. Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary appeared to him and said to him: "This place shall be my dwelling eternally." From that moment on, the pagans did not return to the monastery of Saint Abba Samuel.


[p. 666] {#po.15.p666}
-፦ ወደረሰ ፤ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተግሣጻተ ፣ ወድርሳናተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወተነበ የ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ምጽአቶሙ ፡ ለተንባላት ፡ እሉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ውሉድ ፡ አጋር ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ መንግሥቶሙ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ እለ ፣ ያመነድብዎሙ ፡፣ ለሕዝበ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ በዙሉ፣ በ
19. - This saint composed many exhortations and sermons. He prophesied about the coming of the Muslims - these are the sons of Hagar ('Agâr) - about their kingdom, and about the infidels who would persecute the people of the Christians in all countries.
ሓውርት ፡=፡ -፦ ወሶበ ፣ ቀርበ ፡ ጊዜ ፣ ዕረፍቱ ፣ አስተጋብኦሙ ፣ ለውሉዱ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጽንዑ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወይግበሩ ፡ ትእአዛዛቲሁ ፣ ወይት ጋደሉ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ሃይ"ማኖት ፣ ርትዕት ፡ እስከ ፣ ነፍስ ፣ ደኃሪት ።= ወእምድ ኅረዝ ፡ ሐመ ፤ ሕማመ ፡፣ ንስቲተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወወረሰ ፣ መንግሥተ ፣
20. - When the moment of his death approached, he gathered his spiritual sons; he ordered them to be firm in the fear of the Lord, to fulfill His commandments, and to struggle for the * right faith until the last breath. Then he fell ill with a minor illness. He died in peace and inherited the eternal kingdom.
ዘለዓለም ፡= -፦ ወተብሀለ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ አሐዱ ፣ እምነ ፡ ውሉዱ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ወሶ በ፣ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ተመይጠት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወተንሥ አ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወለዙሎሙ ፡ አኃው ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ኩነኔሆ
21. - It is said concerning him that one of his spiritual sons died. When Saint Abba Samuel came to him, his soul returned into him. He rose and recounted to Saint Abba Samuel and to all the brothers that he had seen the torment of


[p. 667] {#po.15.p667}
ሙ ፡ ለኃጥኣን ፡ ወፅረፍቶሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ ወሰ ከበ ፡ወአዕረፈ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ቀዱስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ አባ ፡ ሰሙኤል ፡የሀሉ፣ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን 5፡
the sinners and the rest of the righteous. Then this brother returned, lay down, and died. May the prayer of this holy Father, the great Abba Samuel, be with us. Amen. 4. Martyrdom of Abba Paesi and his sister Thecla
ወበዛቲ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ኮነ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤሲ ፡ ወቴክላ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡=፡
1. - On this day also are Saint Abba Paesi ('Ési) and his sister Thecla (Têklà), martyrs.
ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አቡጺር ፣ ዘእምዕራብ ፡ እምደ ወለ ፡ እስሙናይን ወኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ንዋይ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወገራውህሀ ፡ ወአባግፅ ፡፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይሁብ ፡ ጸጐረ ፡ አባግዒሁ ፡ ለነዳያን ፣ ወለምስኪናን ፣ ወይሁቦሙ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፳ ወኮነ ፡ ፈራሄ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ንዋዩ ፡ ይፈደፍድ ፡ ወይበ
2. - This saint Paesi was from the people of the city of 'Abousir, which is located to the west of the territory of 'Esmounäyn. He had extremely numerous riches, fields, and sheep. He gave the fleeces of his sheep to the poor and the unfortunate. He gave them numerous alms. He


[p. 668] {#po.15.p668}
ዝኅ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ቴክላ ፡ ፈራሂተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ።፡ ወስሙ ፣ ለአቡሆሙ ፡ ኤል ያስ ፡ ወስማ ፡ ለእሞሙ ፡ ማርያም ፡፡ -፦ ወኮኑ ፣ ይስእልዎ ፡ ለአባ ፣ ኤሲ ፣ ወልይሙ ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፣ ኦወልድነ፣ን ሕነ፣ ንፈቱ ፡ ከመ ፣ ናስተዋስብከ ፡ በሕይወትነ ፣ ወንትፈሣሕ ፡ ብከ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ን ሙት ፡፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ግሙራ ፡= ' ፦ ወአንሰ ፡ አንከርኩ፣ ራእይየ ፡ ወሰባሕክዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወእቤሎ ፡
feared the Lord extremely. His riches were abundant and numerous. His sister Thecla feared the Lord. The name of their father was Elijah ('Élyäs) and the name of their mother was Mary (Märyâm). 3. - Elijah and Mary asked Abba Paesi, their son, and said to him: "O our son, we ourselves desire to marry you during our lifetime and to rejoice in you, before we die." But he himself did not want to agree to this thing at all. 4. - * For my part, I was astonished at my vision. I glorified the Lord and

ለመልአክ ፡ ኦአግዚእየ ፡ ለመኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ጽርሕ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወክቡር =፡ ወይቤለ ኒ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ተውሀቦ ፡ ለፊቅጦር ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሀርማኖስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራ፤ ዘመነነ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ስርጉቶ ፡ ወጾረ ፡ መስቀሎ ፡ ወተለዎ ፡ ለእግዚኡ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ዘ ንተ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣፡ ዘኢየኃልቅ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ዝዓለም ፡ ኃላፊ ፡፡ ወአንሰ ፣ እቤሎ ፣፡ ኦአግዚእየ ፡ እፈቅድ ፣ ከመ ፡ ታጠይቀኒ ፡ ምንት ፡ እሉ ፡ መናብርት ፡ ወምንት ፡ እሉ ፡፣ ዕፀው ፣ ዘሠናይ ፣፤ ፍሬሆሙ ፡ ወምንት ፡ እሉ ፡ አክሊ ላት ፣ ክቡራት ።= ወአውሥአኒ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወይቤስኒ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ኤሲ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዙሉ፣ መካናተ ፡ ዕረፍት ፤ ወፍሥሓ ፣፤ ዘአስተዳለዎሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ይገ ብሩ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፣ ወሰማዕታት ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡፡ ስማዕ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ከመ ፡ አጠይቀ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ በስሞሙ ፡ ለሰማዕት ፡ ቅዱስ
I said to the angel: "O my master, to whom does this beautiful and magnificent palace belong?" The angel said to me: "All this has been given to Victor (Figtor), son of Germanus (Hermânos), the general, who renounced the kingdom of this world and all its pomp, carried his cross, and followed his Master. That is why Our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him - has given him this great kingdom which will not end, in place of the kingdom of this passing world." 5. - But I said to him: "O my master, I want you to let me know what these thrones are, what these trees with beautiful fruits are, and what these magnificent crowns are." The angel answered me and said to me: "O Abba Paesi, all these are the places of rest and joy that the Lord has prepared for the people who make the memory of the saints and the martyrs on earth. Listen now, so that I may assure you that every

man who has built a church in the name of the holy martyrs, or who has buried their body, or who has given an offering from his labor on the day of their memory, or who has lit a lamp, or who has given food to the poor, or who has bought books for their church, or who has written the combat of * their martyrdom, or who has done good on the day of their memory, according to the measure of his power, were it but a cup of cold water, will not lose his reward, as Our Lord says in the holy Gospel. When this man who has made the memory of one of the martyrs or of one of the righteous dies, the martyr or the righteous one will come, will prostrate himself before the Lord - glory to Him - and will say: "My Lord, give me this soul, for it made my memory on earth." It will be given to him immediately.

6. - If this soul has been extremely sinful, a word will go forth from the Lord Almighty, through the mouth of Michael (Mikä'êl), the archangel, who will say: "The Lord Almighty says: 'Leave this soul in torment for a single instant. Then give it to the martyr who has interceded for it.'" Grace will be given to it from the Lord God. It will be done to this soul as the Lord has ordered. It will be given to the martyr or the righteous one who has prayed for it. He himself will receive it and lead it into his dwelling. He will order that it be plunged into the pool, that it be clothed in beautiful garments, that it be made to sit on one of the thrones that you have seen, and that it be crowned with a crown that will not wither. It will rejoice with all the saints and it will live * eternally.

7. - Then the angel carried me on his luminous wings and brought me into the prison. He greeted me and ascended back to heaven. I myself was extremely astonished regarding the great grace that the Lord gives to those who struggle for His holy name.

8. - In the morning, the judge ordered Saint Abba Paesi to be brought from the prison. He was brought. With him was his sister Thecla. He tortured them with a great torture: by wheels, by burning fire, and by iron nails. The Lord strengthened them, made them endure these tortures, and made them emerge safe and sound, without injury. When he was tired of torturing them, he delivered them to the judge of the city of 'Endênäw, in order that he might take them to Upper Egypt (Lä'läy-Gebs). When they had sailed for eight days,

the boat stopped on the bank of the river. The judge ordered the heads of Saint Abba Paesi and his sister Thecla to be cut off. The saints rejoiced extremely and prayed. After they had finished their prayer, their heads were cut off with the sword. They received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. 9. - The Lord ordered a priest, called Abba 'Ori, from the city of Satanouf, to take the bodies of the saints. He || took their bodies and buried them in precious garments and with pure incense. He deposited them in a pure place and hid them until the end * of the days of the persecution. A church was built for them. Their bodies were deposited there. Great miracles and wonders occurred through them.

10. - The number of those who were martyrs with them is four hundred and seventy-seven people.

11. - Revision or Vulgate 5. Salâm to Abba Hierocles, Patriarch of Alexandria. I say greeting to the blessed Hierocles, Patriarch, who succeeded Demetrius. May he receive the remuneration of pastors and the reward of doctors in the midst of the saints. After having guarded his sheep from the wolves, he emigrated to the kingdom of heaven and went to happiness. 6. Salâm to the holy Barbara and Juliana. Greeting to Barbara who made her faith public, By showing the symbol of the Trinity at the window of her house. The sword did not frighten her; the iron saw did not make her suffer.


[p. 675] {#po.15.p675}

Greeting, greeting to Juliana, her companion, Whom they suspended by both breasts. 7. Salâm to the blessed 'Enbâmrênâ.
ሰላም ፡ ለዘአጥብዓት ፡ ሕሊና ። በውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ታልህቅ ፡ እንተ ፣ ኢኮነ ፡ ሕፃና ። እንዘ ፡ ያመስልዋ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ አለ ፡ መነንዋ ፡ በኢልቡና ። አንከርዋ ፡ ማኅበራና ፡ ለብፅዕት ፡ እንባምሬና ። ከመ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ በድና ፡=፡
Greeting to her who made her will strong, To the point of raising in the desert one who was not her child. Having taken her for a man and having repudiated her unintelligently, The companions of the blessed 'Enbâmrênâ were astonished That she was a woman, when they saw her corpse. 8. Salâm to Abba Samuel, superior of the monastery of Qalamon.
ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ መጋቤ ፡ ሥርዓታ ፡ ወሕጋ ። ለደብረ ፡ ቀለሞን ፡ ምልዕተ ፡ ጸጋ። እስከ ፡ አዕረጎ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ኅበ ፡ ላዕላይት ፡ እንግልጋ ። እምካልኣኒሁ ፡ አመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ንትጋ ። ለመልአከ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ጸለይክከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አንተ ፡ በሥጋ ፡፡
Greeting to you, Samuel, who established the rule and the law Of the monastery of Qalamon, full of grace, To the point of having raised it to the rank of a high assembly. When a void occurred among its members, You prayed to the angel of heaven, while you were still alive. 9. Proper fragment of the Martyrdom of Abba Paesi and his sister Thecla.


[p. 676] {#po.15.p676}
አላ ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ጸልዩ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፣ ወእምየ ፡ ቡሩካን ፣ እስመ፣ ኩሉ ፡ ዘትኤዝዙኒ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ አነ ፡ እትዌከፎ ፡ እምኔክሙ ፡ በፍሥሓ ፡ ወኢይገብሮ ፡ ለዝ ንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ወኢትትናገሩኒ ፤ በእንቲአሁ ፣ ወኀደግዎ ፣፤ ወኢተናገርዎቻ ፡ በእንተ ፡ሰ ብሳብ ፤ ግሙራ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ያፈቅርዎ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሞተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ እንዘ ፣ መዋዕሊሁ ፤ ዓመት ፡፣ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡፤ መዋዕል ፡ ሞተት ፡ እሙ ፡እን ዘ ፣ መዋፅሊሃ ፣ ፳ዓመታት ።
1. - Abba Paesi said to his parents: "Pray for me, O my blessed father and mother. Indeed, everything that you order me, I myself will accept from you with joy, but I will not accomplish this work. Do not speak to me again of this matter." They left him and never spoke to him again of marriage, for they themselves loved him extremely. Then his father died, while he was eighty years old. A few days later, his mother died, while she was sixty years old.
-፦ ወበውእቱ ፤ አመት ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፣ ከመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ክ ርስቲያን ፣ ይስግዱ ፣ ለጣዖታት ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤሷ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድ ርያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚኖጥ ፣ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋዮ ።
2. - That year, Diocletian (Diyogletyânos), the impious, ordered that all Christians worship idols. Saint Abba Paesi went to the city of Alexandria ('Eskendryâ), in order to * sell all his possessions.
ወሀለዎ ፡ አርክ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወደወየ ፡ ደ ዌ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤሲ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ሐይወ ፡ እምደዌሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤሷ ፡ ወጳውሎስ ፡ አርኩ ፡ ይሣየጡ ፡ እምንዋዮሙ ፡ መባልዕተ፥
3. He had a friend in the city of Alexandria named Paul (Päwlos). This man fell ill with a serious sickness. Abba Paesi stayed with him until he was healed of his sickness. 4. A great persecution of the Christian people arose in those days. Saint Abba Paesi and Paul, his friend, used their money to buy

good and sweet food and brought it to the saints who were in the city of Alexandria. Likewise, they visited those who were in prisons, in great torment for the name of Our Lord Christ. They comforted them and served them, behaving like slaves. The saints prophesied over them and said to them: "You shall receive the crown of martyrdom."
፦፦ ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አምጽእዎ ፡ ወአብጽሕቻ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ወል ደ ፡ ሀርማኖስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ አባ ፣ ኤሲ ፡ ወእኅቱ ፡ ቴክላ ፣ ቀንዑ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መነነ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ዝ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃላፊ ፡ ወ ቀርበ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤሲ ፣ ኀበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወተአመነ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ መ ኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዎ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ኩነኔ ። ወቀሠፍዎ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ በአስዋ ጥ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ በመጥባህት ፡ መለያልዩሁ ፡ ወረሰዩ ፡ መሓትወ ፡ አሳት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋ ሁ ፡ ወአሰርዎ ፡ በሰናስለ ፡ ሐን ፡ ወአንደዱ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እቶን ፡ እስከ ፡ ተለዓለ ፡ ላህቡ ፡ ፈድ”ፋደ ፡ ወወገርዎ ፡ ለአባ ፣ ኤሲ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እሳት ።
5. In those days, they brought and caused Saint Victor (Figtor), son of Germanus (Hermânos), to arrive at the city of Alexandria. When Abba Paesi and his sister Thecla saw him, they envied him. Indeed, he himself had renounced the kingdom of this passing world. Abba Paesi approached the judge and confessed Our Lord Christ. The judge ordered him to be tortured with great tortures. They gave him many blows with straps; they cut his limbs with a knife; they placed torches of fire on his body; they bound him with iron chains; furthermore, they lit a fire in a furnace until the flames rose * extremely, and they threw Abba Paesi into this fire.

6. Abba Paesi prayed, saying: "O my Lord Jesus Christ, it is You who saved the three children from the furnace of fire, the prophet Daniel (Dân'êl) from the mouths of the lions, and Your martyr Victor from the flames of the bath. Likewise, O Lord, help me, save me, and send me Your angel, so that he may save me from these tortures, for to You be glory for ever and ever." When he had said such words, behold, the angel Souryal descended from heaven. He stood at the right hand of the virgin saint Abba Paesi. He said to him: "Greetings to you, O righteous man, Abba Paesi, virgin. Do not be afraid, for the Lord is with you. He Himself will save you from all your torments." Immediately the fire became like cool dew. The angel split the walls of the furnace of fire. He took the virgin saint Abba Paesi by the hand and brought him out of the furnace safe and sound, without injury.
ሙስና። -፦፦ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤሲ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወጸርኃ ፡ ወይቤ ፣ ኅፈ ር ። ኦመኩንን ፡ አንተ ፡ ወንጉሥከ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወጣዖታቲከ ፡ ርኩሳን ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤሲ ፡ ሕያዎ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ፡ አንከሩ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወጸርጉ ፡፣ በ8ቃ
7. Immediately the virgin saint Abba Paesi went to find the judge. He cried out and said: "Be confounded, O judge, you yourself as well as your impious king and your impure idols." When the people saw the virgin saint Abba Paesi safe and sound, without


[p. 679] {#po.15.p679}
ል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ገሀደ ፡ ነአምን ፡ በአምላኩ ፡ ለአባ፣ ኤሲ ። ወመተሩ ፡ ርእ" ዕሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ።
injury, they were extremely astonished. They cried out with one voice, saying: "We are Christians openly; we believe in the God of Abba Paesi." They cut off * their heads with the sword. They received the crown of martyrdom
፦ ወአምዝ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰድዎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ኤሲ ፡ ውስተ ፥ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወወሰደዎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ኤሲ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ መኩንን ። ሽ
in the kingdom of heaven. 8. Then the judge ordered the lictors to lead Abba Paesi to prison. They led Abba Paesi away, as the judge had ordered.
፦ ወአስተርአያ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለእኅቱ ፡ ቴክላ ፡ ወአዘዛ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ከመ ፡ ተሐውጾ ፡ ለእጉሃ ። ወተንሥአት ፡ ወሖረት ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ እንዴናው ፡ ወበጽሐት ፡ ኀበ ፣ ባሕር ፡ ወኃሠሠት ፡ ሐመረ ፡ ወኢረከበት ፡ ወአስተርአየት ፡ ላቲ ፣ ሐመር ፣ መንፈሳዊት ፡፣ ወተፅዕነት ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፣፡ ወሀለወት ፣ ው ስተ ፣ ይእቲ ፡ ሐመር ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፣ ድንግል ፣ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላ ክ ፡ ወኤልሳቤጥ ፣ አዝማዲሃ ፡ ወኮና ፡ ይናዝዛሃ በእንተ ፡እጉሃ ፣ አባ ፣ ኤሲ ። ወኤ ልሳቤጥ ፡ ትቤላ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ሀለወኒ ፣ ፳ወልድ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፣ በዓመፃ ። ወክ ልእታሂ ፡ ትቤላ ፣፤ እስመ ፡ ሊተኒ ፡ ሀለወኒ ፣ 5ወልድ ፣ ወሰቀልዎ ፡ ወቀተልዎ ፡ በቅን ዓት ። ወቴክላሰ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ኢታአምር ፡ መኑ ፡ እማንቱ ፡ ወኢሀለየት ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አስተር
9. An angel of the Lord appeared to his sister Thecla. He ordered her to go to the city of Alexandria to visit her brother. She arose and went away from the city of 'Endenaw. She arrived at the river. She looked for a boat, but she did not find one. A spiritual boat appeared to her. She boarded it. In this boat were Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary (Maryam), the God-bearer, and Elizabeth ('Elsabet), her relative. They comforted her concerning her brother Abba Paesi. Elizabeth said to her: "I, I had a son. They cut off his head iniquitously." The other also said to her: "I too, I had a son. They crucified him and killed him with cruelty." Thecla did not


[p. 680] {#po.15.p680}
አያ ፣ ራእይ ፣ ውእቱ ፡፣ ላቲ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ በጽሐት ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወአእመረት ፣፡ ከመ ፡ ዘአስተርአያ ፣ ላቲ ፣ ራእይ ፣ ውእቱ ። ወተስአለት ፡ በእንተ ፡ እሃ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤ ሲ ፣ ወአብጽሕዋ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወተጋብአት ፡ ቦቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ቶ ፣ ተፈሥሐት ፡ ይእቲ ፣ ወውእቱኒ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ዐቢየ ፣ ፍሥሓ ፡ ወተሠናአዉ ፡ ከ መ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ ኅቡረ ።
know who they were. She did not think that a vision had appeared to her. Immediately she arrived at the city of Alexandria. Then she knew that it was a vision that had appeared to her. She inquired about her brother Abba Paesi. They brought her to him in the prison. She met with him. When she saw him, she rejoiced. He also rejoiced with great joy. They agreed to become martyrs together.
ወዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፣ አስተርአዮ ፡ መል” አከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ሱርያል ፡ ለአባ ፡ ኤሲ ፡ ወጾሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ክነፊሁ ፣ ብርሃናዊ ፣ በትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረጎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወአርአዮ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኢየሩሳሌምሃ ፡ ሰማያዊ ተ ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወተቀበልዎ ፡ ወተኣምጎዎ ፡ ወአርአዮ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ማ ኅደሪሆሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ ወሰማዕታት ፡ አለ ፡ ጻመዉ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ስሙ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔ ር ፣ ወርእያ ፣ ለኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሀገር ፣ ዐባይ ፣ ዘኢይክል ፡ ልሳነ ፣ ሰብእ ፣ ይትናገር ፡ ዕበያ ፡ ወክብራ ፡ ወስነ፣ ራአያ ፡ ወመራህብቲሃ ፣ ሥርግዋን ፡ በወርቅ ፡ ወበብሩር ፡
10. When it was the middle of the night, * the angel of the Lord Souryal appeared to Abba Paesi. He carried him on his luminous wings, by the order of the Lord, brought him up into heaven, and made him see the city of the Lord, the heavenly Jerusalem (Iyarousalêm). All the saints came out, went to meet him, greeted him, and furthermore showed him the dwelling of the righteous and the martyrs who had labored for the name of the Lord. He saw the heavenly Jerusalem, the great city, whose majesty, glory, and beautiful appearance the tongue of men cannot recount. Its squares are adorned with gold, silver, and


[p. 681] {#po.15.p681}
ወበአዕባን ፣ ክቡራት ፣ ወውስቴታ ፡፣ ብርሃን ፡ ዘይፈደፍድ ፡ እምብርሃነ ፡ ፀሓይ ፡ እምት እልፊተ ፡፤ አእላፋት ፡ ምክዕቢተ ።
precious stones. In it there is a light that exceeds the light of the sun thousands and thousands of times.

11. The angel of the Lord led him and showed him an extremely large palace, in which were three hundred marble columns. In this palace, the guards were adorned and girded with belts of gold and precious stones. In this palace dwelt one hundred soldiers. They were adorned and girded with belts of gold. The height of each column was five hundred cubits. There is nothing like it in the world. I said to the angel: "To whom does this great palace belong? Indeed, no one among the men of this world could build a similar palace." The angel said to me: "O my brother Abba Paesi, all the kings of the earth, if they were gathered with all their subjects, could not pay the price * of a single one of these three hundred columns that support this palace." As for me, seeing this, I was astonished and I glorified the Lord.


[p. 682] {#po.15.p682}

10. Salam to Abba Paesi and to his sister Thecla. I say greeting to your brain, which suffered by the perforation of the knife and by the burning flames, Paesi, you who were crowned with an adornment of light like a diadem. Greeting to your sister Thecla, whose limbs were anointed with the perfumed oil of Mary (Maryam). 11. Salam to the companions of Abba Paesi and his sister Thecla. Greeting to you, companions of Thecla and Paesi, women and men. You are the image of my parents, while I am a stranger and a pilgrim. So that you may snatch me from the serpent that lays snares for me, I place my heart under your power.


[p. 683] {#po.15.p683}

12. Death of Saint John Damascene. 1. On this day also died John Damascene (Yohannes za-Damasqo). 2. This saint was the son of Mansour, one of the nobles and great personages of Damascus. He was raised in wisdom and in the fear of the Lord. He was instructed in the teaching of the philosophers by his master Quelherou-Quezma, a monk, philosopher, and disciple of Cyril (Qorili). When he had finished his education, Quezma asked Mansour that Abba John should become a monk. Abba John went to the convent of the holy martyr Mar-Saba (Mari-Saba). When he had gone there, his father died. He became the secretary to the judge of the city. He did not hide his secrets from him. In those days arose Constantine (Quastantinos), son of * Leo (Leyon), the heretic, who made war on the divine images. He was overturning the churches. This saint Abba John, while he did not have the degrees


[p. 684] {#po.15.p684}
ኩሎሙ ፡፤ መሃይምናን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያጸንዖሙ ፡ በሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስግዱ ፡ለ ሥዕላት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡እንዘ ፣ ያመጽእ ፣ ሎሙ ፡ስምዓ ፣እመጻሕፍት ፡አምላካዊያት ። -፦ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ሐቀየ ፡ ስነኒሁ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወአም ጽአ ፣ ጸሓፌ ፡ ወአጽሐፎ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ብርዓ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ኅበ ፣ መኩንነ ፡ ደ ማስቆ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዋድዮ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ኅቤክ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ሊ ተ ፡ዘንተ ፡ ክታበ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጸባእ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ወእማሀርክ ፡ ሀገረከ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ አምነ ፡ ወመተሮ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ዘየማን ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ይጽሕፍ ፡ ባቲ ።
of ecclesiastical ordination, sent a letter to all the faithful, strengthening them in the right faith; he exhorted them to worship the holy images, bringing them the testimony of the divine Scriptures. 3. When the heretical king learned this, he gnashed his teeth against him, brought a scribe, made him write in the manner of John's handwriting, and sent the writing to the prefect of Damascus, accusing John. He said to the prefect: "John who is with you has sent me this note, so that I might make war on you and plunder your city." Having heard this word, the prefect believed it. He cut off the saint's right hand, with which he wrote.
፦ ወነሚኦ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሥዕላ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማ ርያም ፡ ወሰአለ ፣ በአንብዕ ፡ ጽሩቅ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡ ኦአግዝአትየ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ተ ፃባዕኩ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ሰጊድ ፡ ለሥዕልኪ ፡ በጽሐኒ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡ ፈውሰኒ ፡ በጸሎ ትኪ ፡ ወበተኣምርኪ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኖመ ፡ ንስቲተ ፣ አስተርአየቶ ፣ እግዝእትነ ፣ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ በክልኤ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወሜጠት ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወነቂሆ ፡ አእኩታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፣ ማርያም ።
4. Having taken his hand, Saint John went toward the image of Our Lady Mary. He prayed with great tears, saying: "O my Lady, is it not when I struggled for the worship of your image that this happened to me? Now heal me by your prayer and by your miracles." When he had slept a little, Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary (Maryam), twice virgin, appeared to him. She restored his hand as it was before. Having awakened, he thanked Our Lady Mary.


[p. 685] {#po.15.p685}
-፦ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምኔተ ፡ ማሪ ፡ ሳባ ፣ ወመንኩሰ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አበ ፣ ምኔት ፣ ወአ በ፣-ምኔትሰ ፡ መጠዎ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምሀሮ ፡ "ሥርዓተ ፡ ምንኩስ ና ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፣፤ ኢትግበር ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ እምትምሀርትከ ፣ አፍኣዊ ት ፡ ዘቀዲሙ ፡ዳአሙ ፡ ተመሐር ፡ አርምሞ ፤ ወእምብገኃ ፡ ትሕትናሁ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሞ አ ፡ ትዕቢተ ፡ ሰይጣን ።
5. He went to the convent of Mar-Saba and became a monk under the superior of the convent. The superior of the convent entrusted him to a spiritual elder, so that he might teach him * the monastic rule. The elder said to him: "O my son, do not do anything of your former foreign learning, but learn to keep silence." By the greatness of his humility, John conquered the pride of Satan (Saytan).
፦ ወበአሐቲ ፡ እመዋዕል ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አረጋዊ ፣ እመነኮሳት ፡ ወሀለዎ ፡ እጐ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውፅእ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሰቆቃወ ፡ ወቃለ ፡ ብካይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እ ሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እፈርህ ፡ እምትእዛዘ ፡ መምህርየ ፡ አረጋዊ ፣ ወይቤሎ፣0፣ ኢይከሥት ፡ ለመኑሂ ፡ ወሶበ ፤ አስተብቀ'ዖ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ደረሰ ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ሰቆቃወ ፣ ዘጥ ቀ ፡ማኅዘኒ ፡-ለዘይሰምዖ ።
6. One day, an elder among the monks died. He had a brother. This man asked John to compose for him lamentations and plaintive words concerning his brother. John said to him: "I fear the order of my master, the elder." The brother said to him: "I will not reveal this to anyone." When he had begged him much, John composed for him lamentations that were very saddening for whoever would hear them.
-፦ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምን ፡ መምሀሩ ፡ ተምዕዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወሰደይ ፣፡ እማኅደሩ ። ወተ ማኅፀነ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ አዕሩግ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አገበርዎ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኩስተረ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘመነኮሳት ፡ እምህሮ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ገብረ ፡
7. When his master learned this, he was angry with him and drove him from his dwelling. John took refuge with all the elders. When they had forced the elder to take him back, the latter said to them: "If he cleans the latrines of the monks, I will have pity on him." Having heard this, John did
