በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ወርእዮ ፣ ለዮሐንስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ትሩፋቲሁ ፣ ተቀበሎ ፡ ወአብ ኦ ፡ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘቶ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ለ አረጋዊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅድጎ ፣ ወይድርስ ፣ ድርሳናተ ፡ ወሀሎ ፡ ድርሳናቲሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዮም ። ወሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ዘኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሜሞ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ። -፦ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሥዕላት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ወሃይማኖት ፡ ር ትዕት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይዛለፎሙ ፡ ለነገሥት ፣ ወለመኳንንት ፡ እስከ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ርስዕና ፡ ጥሉል ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ተስዕረ ፡ "እምዓለም ፡ ወፈለሰ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘአፍቀሮ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፣፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ እንዘ ፡ እነግር ፡ ጽድቆ ። ለዮሐንስ ፡ ዘደማስቆ ። ሶበ ፣ መምህሩ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ አእምደሪሰ ፡ ድርሳናት ፣ አስተረኃቆ ።
as the elder had ordered. Having seen the virtues of John, this elder went to meet him and brought him into his house. Then Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary ordered the elder to let him compose sermons. His sermons still exist today. The patriarch of Jerusalem (Iyarousalêm) ordained John a priest against his will. 8. He continued to struggle for the holy images and the right faith, rebuking kings and magistrates, until he reached a green old age. When he had reached it, he detached himself * from the world and emigrated toward the Christ whom he loved. May his holy blessing be with his dear Za-Manfas-Qedous for ever and ever. I say greeting, exposing his holiness, to John Damascene. When his master, the elder, had prevented him from composing sermons,


[p. 687] {#po.15.p687}
ማርያም ፡ አኅደገቶ ፣ በዘይፌጽም ፡ ጻሕቆ ። እስከ ፡ በርስዓን ፡ ጥሉል ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ልሂቆ ፡=፡
Mary (Maryam) turned the elder away from this decision, so that John might finish his work, until he died, after having aged in a green old age.
ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ይርስቅላ ፡ ወአልቆሎኒጦስ ፡ ወአውጊንዮስ ፡ አቡ ሃ ፡ ለአንባምሬና ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
13. Memory of Yerseqla, of 'Elgolonitos, and of Eugene, father of Marina. On this day also died Yerseqla, 'Elgolonitos, and Eugene ('Awginyos), father of Marina ('Enbamrena). May their holy blessing be with our king 'Iyasou for ever and ever. 14. Salam to the Redeemer.
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ መቤዛዊ ፡ አምላከ ። ምሥጢረ ፡ ትስጉቱ ፡ ይንብቡ ፡ ከናፍረ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ዘሆከ ። በእንቲአሁ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያቀድም ፡ ሰቢከ ። ቦኅበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ፈኑ ፡ እዴከ ። ሽ ወቦ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አንሥእ ፡ ኃይለከ ።
I say greeting to the redeeming God, who moved the lips of the prophets, so that they might express the mystery of His Incarnation. David (Dawit), beginning to preach on this subject, said here: Send your hand, and said there: Rouse your power.


[p. 688] {#po.15.p688}
፲፲፲. ጅ፲ዐዕሯጀ6. -፦ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ወንጹሕ ፣ መምህረ ፡ ትሩ ፋት ፡ አባ ፡ ተክለ ፡ አልፋ ፡ ዘደብረ ፡ ዲማ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ምኔት ። -፦ ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መንኩሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ንኡስ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ብዙ ኃ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወይገብር ፡ ሰጊደ ፣ እስከ ፡ ይውሕዝ ፡ ሐፉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማይ፣ ወይሁብዎ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘአጥረየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ልብሱ ፡ ወ መንጸፉ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ስሙዓቱ ፡ በኩሉ ፣፡ ምድረ ፡ ጐዣም ፡ ወውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፤ በሓው ርተ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡=
III. - Proper. 15. Death of Abba Takla-'Alfa, superior of the monastery of Dima. 1. On this day also died the holy, pure Father, doctor, full of virtues, Abba Takla-'Alfa, superior of the monastery of Dima. 2. This saint became a monk while he was young. He practiced much asceticism through fasting and prayer. He performed genuflections until his sweat flowed like water. He gave as alms to the poor and the unfortunate everything he possessed, even his clothing and his blanket. His fame spread throughout the whole territory of Gojjam (Guajam) and in all the provinces of Ethiopia ('Ityopya).
-፦ ወተብሀለ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወነጸሮ ፡ 8መነኮስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የዓርግ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይወርድ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተክለ ፡፤ አልፋ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ምድር ፡ ነጸሮ ፡ ለውእ ቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ወአውገዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይንግር ፣ ለመኑሂ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ፈሪሆ ፡ ውዳሴ ፡ ከንቱ
3. This is said concerning him. A monk saw Saint Takla-'Alfa ascending into the heights and descending to earth. Takla-'Alfa noticed this monk. He forbade him under pain of interdict to tell this fact to anyone, except after his death, for he feared vain praise.
፦ ወአርአየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡፣ ብዙኃ ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ ወመንክረ ፣ ዘኢይትኃለቀ፦ ፡ በሕይወቱ ፡ ለአለ ፣ ይጹውዑ ፡ ስሞ ፣ ወይትመኃፀኑ ፣ በጸሎቱ ፡፡
4. The Lord showed through his intercession many miracles and countless wonders, during his life, to those who invoked his name and commended themselves to his prayer.


[p. 689] {#po.15.p689}
፦ ወደወየ ፡ ንስቲተ ፣ ወአዕረፈ ፣ በሰላም ፡፡ -፦ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሞቱ ፣ ይትከሠት ፣ ተኣምራት ፤ ወመንክራት ፣ ውስተ ፡መ ቃብሪሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ዮም =፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሃሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ስአለ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡
5. He was a little ill and he died in peace. 6. After his death, miracles and wonders have manifested at his tomb until today. May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear Se'ela-Syon for ever and ever. Amen.

### THE 9 TAHSCHASCH
አሜን። ' አመ ፡፪ለታኅሣሥ ።
I. - Primitive recension.
፪. -፦ ፻፳0666:0810ኳ. ጅጀህር)1176. በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 8አምላክ ፡፡
1. Death of Abba Pamim, the confessor. In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.


[p. 690] {#po.15.p690}
፦ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በአሚን ፡ ተአማኒ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ዘ እንበለ ፡ ክፅወተ ፡ ደም ።
1. On this day died Saint Pamim (Ba'amin), the confessor, who was a martyr without the shedding of blood.
፦ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምኔተ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ሐብ ፡ አምደወለ ፡ እስሙናይን ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ቅሩብ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ተርሳ ።፡ ወኮነ ፡ መጋቤሁ ፡ ለ፳ብእሲ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ፍቁረ ፡ በኅበ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ንጽሕናሁ ፡ ወጽድቁ ።፡ ወኮነት ፡ ብእሷቱ ፣፡ ለው እቱ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ታፈቅሮ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ በአሚን ፡ ወተአምኖ ፡ ፈድፋደ ።፡፡ ወሐለየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በአሚን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ማኅለቅተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፣ ወኀደገ ፣ መልእክቶ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ባዕ ል ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ ውስቴታ ።፡፡
2. - This saint was from Menêta-Dagiqa-Hadib, in the territory of Esmounäyn, in the land of Egypt (Gebs), near the city of Tarsä. He was the steward of a rich man. He was loved by all because of his purity and his justice. The wife of this rich man loved Saint Pamim and trusted him extremely. Saint Pamim dreamed of the end of this world. He abandoned the service of this rich man, went to a monastery that was in the laura, and became a monk there.
ዲ. ፦ ወሰበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በአሚን ፡ ጎደጎ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወነሥኣ ፡ ለብእሲቱ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፣ ወሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ፣ ይትመየጥ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ወይቤልቻዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፣ ኢይትከሀለነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንትፈለጥ ፡
3. - When this rich man learned that Saint Pamim had left him and had gone away, he was extremely sad. He took his wife with him. They went to find him and asked him to return to their home. They said to him: "As for us, it is not possible for us to ever separate from you; we will not"


[p. 691] {#po.15.p691}
እምኔከ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ወኢነኃድገክ ፡፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አንሰ፣ ኢይትከሀለኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ አብጻ ዕኩ ፡ ነፍስየ ፣ "ለአግዚአብሔር ። ወኃደግዎ ፣ ወሖሩ ፡ እንዘ ፣ የሐዝኑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ፍልጠቶሙ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡= ፦ ወተጋደለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወተጸምዶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወበትጋህ ፡ ወኢየአክሎ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አላ ፡ ፈቀ ደ ፤ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሰማዕተ ፣፡ ወይክዓው ፡ ደሞ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ = ወሖረ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንዴናው ፡ ወረከቦሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቤንንዎሙ ።፡ ወተአምነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወኩነንዎ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወቀሠፍዎ ፡ ወአውዓዬዩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፣ በአሳት ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ መለያልያቲሁ ፡ ወወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንኩራዙራት ፡ ወወደዩ ፡ አብትርተ ፣ ሐጺ ን ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፣ ወአውዓዩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ።፡ ወኮነ፣ ውስተ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ፣ ሹኔኔ ፡ ጽኑዓ ።፡ ወእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ያነሥኦ ፣ ወያስተዔግሦ ፡ ወይሬስዮ ፡ ጥዑየ ፡
leave you." He said to them: "As for me, it is not possible for me to return to your home, for I have vowed myself * to the Lord." They left him and went away, being extremely sad because of their separation from him. 4. - This saint practiced great asceticism. He served the Lord for many years through fasting, prayer, and vigils. This did not suffice for him. He wanted to become a martyr and shed his blood for the name of Our Lord Jesus Christ. He went to the city of Antinoë (‘Andênäw). He found many Christians who were being tortured. He himself also confessed the name of Our Lord Christ. He was tortured with great tortures: he was scourged; his body was burned in the fire; his limbs were cut off; he was put on wheels; iron bars were put in the fire and his body was burned with them. He was firm in all these tortures. Our Lord Christ exalted him, gave him courage, and rendered him safe and sound, without injury.
ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ።፡ -፦ ወእንዘ ፡ውእቱ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይትኬነን ፡ ሐልቀ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ ወነግሠ ፣፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውፅአዎሙ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እለ ፡ ሙቁሓን ፡ በእንተ ፣ ስሙ ፣ ለክርስቶስ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ለቅዱ ስ ፡ በአሚን ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፣ ኀበ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ አለ ፤ ውስተ ፡ አብ ያተ ፡ መዋቅሕት ፡ ወያጠይቆ”ሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ጐለቆሙ ፡ › እምጉጐልቆሙ ፡ ለሰማዕታት ፡ እለ ፡ ተሰምዩ ፣ ተአማንያን ። ወንጉሥሰ ፣ ቄስጠንጢ ኖስ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡፣ ፳ወ፪ ።፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምውስቴቶሙ ፡አባ፣፥
5. - While he himself was being tortured thus, the worship of idols disappeared. Constantine (Quastantinos), the just, reigned. He ordered all the Christians who were imprisoned for the name of Christ to be brought out of prison. Our Lord Christ appeared to Saint Pamim. He ordered him to go and find all the saints who were in the prisons and to assure them * that he himself, Our Lord Christ, would count them among the number of martyrs, those who would be called confessors. King Constantine ordered seventy-two of them to be brought. There was among them Abba Nob, the confessor. They went toward him.


[p. 692] {#po.15.p692}
ኖብ ፡ ተአማኒ ፡ ወሐሩ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡=፡ -፦ ወቅዱስሰ ፡ በአሚን ፡ ኅደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዘአፍኣ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ እስሙና ይን ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይፌውሶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ድውያ ! ን ፡ ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ በሓውርት ፡=፡
6. - As for Saint Pamim, he lived in a monastery that was outside the city of Esmounäyn. The Lord gave him a great grace: he healed all the sick. His fame spread throughout all the lands.


[p. 693] {#po.15.p693}
-፦ ወኮነት ፡ ንግሥት ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ደዌ ፣ ዓቢይ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ሀሎ ፡ በኀቤሃ ፡ 8ዲያቆን ፡ ፈራሄ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኮ ነ ፡ ያነብብ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ራእየ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ አቡቀለምሲስ ።፡፡ ወቀንአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ መገብተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሖረ፣ ኅኀበ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ወ ይቤሎ ፡ አአምር ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ይሰክብ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዝእትየ ፡ ንግሥት ፡ብእሷትከ ፡ወያመከኒ ፡በአንብቦ ፡ መጽሐፈ ራእየ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡፡
7. - Upon the queen, wife of the king of Rome (Romé), there came an extremely serious illness. There was with her a deacon who feared the Lord. He read to her, every day, the book of the Vision of John (Yohannès), the evangelist, that is to say, the Apocalypse. One of the king's stewards was jealous of him. He went to find the king and said to him: "Know, O my master, that the deacon John sleeps with my mistress, the queen, your wife. He uses the reading of the book of the Vision of John as a pretext every day."
-፦ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ ወተንሥአ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወቦ አ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽርሐ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ኅበ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ንግሥት ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ዲያ ቆን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ቅድሜሃ ፡ ለንግሥት ፣ ወያነብብ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ራእየ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡=፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሥእዎ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡፣ ዲያቆን ፣ ምስለ ፣ መጽሐፉ ፣ ወያ”ስ ጥምዎዖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡፪ዕደው ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፣፡ ሐመር ንኡስ ፡ ወአስጠምዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡፡ ወሶበ ፡ አስጠምዎ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ፪ዕደው ፣ አምሳ
8. - When the king heard this, he was extremely sad. He rose immediately and entered the royal chamber where the queen was. He found the deacon standing before the queen and reading the book of the Vision of John. Immediately he ordered the deacon to be taken with his book and to be * swallowed up in the sea. Two men took him, put him in a small boat, and swallowed him up in the sea. When they had swallowed him up, these two men

saw like a luminous man who snatched him from the sea with his book, carried him away, and deposited him on an island. When the two men saw this, they were extremely astonished. They went to their homes and did not tell the king what they had seen. 9. - When the queen had seen what had been done to the deacon, she was extremely sad. Her belly swelled extremely. She was struck by an illness and she remained afflicted with this illness for fifteen years. Many doctors were brought for her. It was not possible for them to heal her of her illness. A learned man advised her and said to her: "If you go to find the saints who are in the land of Egypt (Gebs), you will be delivered from your illness." She rose immediately. With her there was a numerous escort. She arrived in the land of Egypt. She visited many monasteries and many churches. She was not healed of her illness.


[p. 695] {#po.15.p695}
ወሶበ ፣ በጽሐት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ አንዴናው ፡ ወእለ ፣ ርእይዋ ፡ መኳንንት ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ምጽአታ = ወነገረቶሙ ፡ ምክንያተ ፣ ምጽአታ ፣ ወአም ከርዋ ፣ ከመ ፡ ትሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡፣ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ በአሚን ፣ ወተጽዕነት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፥ ወሖረት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ በአሚን ። ወነገርዎ ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ንግ ሥት ፡ መጽአት ፡ ኅቤከ ፡ ወትፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትባረክ ፡ ”እምኔከ ።፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ብየ ፡ አነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡ ምድር ።፡ ወበዓቢይ ፡ ስአለት ‹፡ እምነ ፡ አኃው ፡
10. - When she arrived at the city of Antinoë, the magistrates who saw her were extremely astonished at her arrival. She explained to them the reason for her arrival. They advised her to go and find Saint Abba Pamim. She embarked on a boat and went to the monastery of Saint Abba Pamim. It was explained to Abba Pamim and they said to him: "Behold, the queen has come to find you. She wants to be blessed * by you." He said to them: "What have I myself in common with the kings of the earth?" Upon the pressing request of the brothers, he went out to her.
ወፅአ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡=፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየቶ ፡ ንግሥት ፡ ሰገደት ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፥ ላዕለ ፡ ዘይት ፡ ወቀብኣ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወሐይወት ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ እምደዌሃ ።፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በአ ሚን ፡ አእምሪ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ዘበጽሐኪ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ዘአስጠ ሞ ፡ንጉሥ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ሕያው ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ውስ
11. - When the queen saw him, she prostrated herself at his feet. The saint prayed over some oil and anointed her with it. She was healed immediately of her illness. Saint Pamim said to her: "Know that this illness came upon you because of the deacon whom the king swallowed up in the sea. He is alive until now and he

ተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ደሴት ፡ ዘማእከለ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወመጽሐፈ ፥ ራእየ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ አቡቀለምሲስ ፡ ምስሌሁ ።፡፡ ወዕበ ፡ ሰምዓት ፣ ንግሥት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አንከረት ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሰብሐቶ ፡ ለእግ ዚአብሔር ፣፤ ወተፈሥሐት ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ፍሥሓ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡=፡ ወአቅረበት ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ በአሚን ፡ ንዋየ ፣ ብዙኃ ፣ወአምኃ ፣ ወኢነሥአ ፡ ወኢ ምንተኒ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ጻሕለ ፡ ወጽዋዓ ፣ ወመስቀ ለ ፡ ዘወርቅ ። ወእምዝ ፣ ተመይጠት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትሴብሖ ፡ ለእግዚ
dwells on the island that is in the middle of the sea. The book of the Vision of John, 'Abougalamsis, is with him." When the queen heard this, she was extremely astonished. She glorified the Lord. She rejoiced with great joy concerning the life of this deacon. She offered Saint Pamim much money and gifts. He took nothing except sacred objects for the church: a paten, a chalice, and a golden cross. Then she returned to the city of Rome, glorifying the Lord.

12. - When she met with the king, she explained to him everything that had happened to her and what Saint Pamim had explained to her concerning the deacon who had been swallowed up and was alive until now. When the king heard this, he was extremely astonished. * He immediately sent servants to the island. They found the deacon alive. The book of the Vision of John was with him.

They brought him to the king. When the king saw him, he rejoiced. He prostrated himself before him and said to him: "Forgive me the fault that I committed against you." The deacon answered and said to him: "May the Lord forgive us our faults together." Then this deacon was instituted patriarch of the city of Rome. He translated the Vision of John, 'Abougalamsis. 13. - As for Saint Pamim, he practiced asceticism, night and day, without respite. There was near him a holy bishop who was celebrating the feast of a martyr, with many faithful, in the monastery of a martyr. Heretical Arians had taken for themselves a false bishop. They were celebrating the same feast and leading many of the people astray. This holy bishop came to find Saint Pamim. He explained to him his sadness concerning these heretics.


[p. 698] {#po.15.p698}

Saint Pamim asked the Lord to break the plan of these heretics. Then he took in his hand a palm branch. All the monks who were with him took likewise, each of them, a palm branch in their hand. They went against * these pagans and these Arians and drove them away. The Lord dissipated their counsel. They did not return there until now.

14. - As for Saint Pamim, behold, he himself grew old and fell ill with the illness from which he was to die. He gathered the brothers, gave them orders, consoled them, and explained to them that his day to go to the Lord was approaching. They were extremely sad because of their separation from him. Then he surrendered his soul into the hand of the Lord. The brothers gave him a beautiful funeral. They chanted psalms over him as was fitting. His body became

II. - Revision or Vulgate. 2. Salâm to Pamim, the confessor. Greeting to you, Pamim, lying on a bed of burning iron. You have been the protector of orphans and widows. Greeting to the people who were with you, in the house of trial and pain, imprisoned for Christ.

3. Memory of the death of Badalomyâ, of Romyâ, and of Zacharias, the monk. On this day is the death of Badalomyâ, of Romyâ, and of Zacharias (Zakäryâs), the monk. May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear Za-Manfas-Qedous for centuries of centuries.

### THE 10TH OF TAHSCHASCH

|. - Primitive Recension. 1. Translation of the body of Severus, patriarch of Alexandria. In the name of the Father, of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. 1. - On this day they brought the body of Saint Abba Severus (Säwiros), patriarch of the city of Alexandria, to the monastery of Zegâg, which is outside the city of Alexandria ('Eskendryä). 2. - This saint died in the city of Sehâ at the home of a rich man, a Christian, a friend of God, called Dorotheus (Dortäwos). Then Dorotheus sent the body with faithful and believing people, on a boat, to the monastery of Zegâg, which is outside the city of Alexandria. He ordered them not to go into the small canal, but to go into the great lake and from there to go up to the port.


[p. 702] {#po.15.p702}
ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ ቀርጠሶ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ደቡብ ፣ ”ወምስሌሆሙ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምዕራብ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወኢረከቡ ፡ ማየ ፣ ዘይጸውር ፡ ሐመ ሮሙ ፡፡ ወጻመዉ ፡ ጻማ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡፣ ይስሕብዋ ፡ ለሐመር ፡፣ ወተመ ክነዩ ፣ ከመ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፣ ለሐመር ፡ ወኢተክህሎሙ ፡ ወሐዘኑ ፡ ወደንገፁ ፡ ወተሐጥአ ፣ ጥበቦሙ ፡።፡
When they had arrived at the city of Qartasô toward the north, having * with them the body of the saint, they went a little toward the west, but they did not find enough water to carry their boat. They took great pains to drag the boat themselves. They sought the means to make the boat advance, but it was not possible for them to succeed. They were sad and dismayed and their wisdom failed them.

3. - But the Lord, friend of men, who had hidden the Israelites from their enemies, had opened a path for them in the Red Sea and had made them pass through, hid the body of Saint Severus from the heretical Meletians. Indeed, they had hated him during his life and likewise they hated him after his death, for his word cut into their hearts like a sword. Then the Lord made a miracle manifest. He made the boat advance a little in the water,


[p. 703] {#po.15.p703}
ማይ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ጻጴምዕራፍ ፡ እስከ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ዘየዓርጉ ፡ እምኔሁ ።፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ጾርዎ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወአብጽሕዎቻ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ዝጋግ፣0 ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡፤ ሎቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ ባዕል ፡፤ ዶርታዎስ ፡ ወኮነ፣ ፍሥሓ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፤ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡
for seven miles, until they reached the place from which they would disembark. Then they carried the body of the saint, brought it to the monastery of Zegâg, and deposited it in the sanctuary that the rich Dorotheus had built for him. There was great joy in all the land of Egypt (Gebs) and especially in the city of Alexandria.

4. - The Lord performed great miracles and wonders through his body. One of the saint's teeth had fallen from his mouth while he was alive. A monk from among the monks of the monastery of Zegâg took it and wrapped it in * a piece of silk. It became a means of healing for whoever had an illness. Indeed, the monks themselves brought it to the city of Alexandria, placed it on the sick, and they were healed. The Lord made Saint Severus much greater after his death than during his life. May his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen.

2. Death of Abba Theophanes, patriarch of Alexandria 1. - On this day also died the holy Father Abba Theophanes (Tä'ofänyos), patriarch of the city of Alexandria ('Eskendryä). He is among the number of the sixtieth patriarchs. 2. - After he had sat on the seat of Mark (Märqos), the evangelist, for four years and six months, he died assassinated. May the Lord have mercy on us through the prayer of his saints. Amen. 3. Death of Saint Nicholas, bishop of Myra. 1. - On this day also died the holy and just Father Nicholas (Nigoläwos), bishop. This name literally means conqueror of the people.

2. - This just and holy Father was from the people of the city of Myra (Mirä). The name of his father was Epiphanius ('Épifänyos); the name of his mother was Yon. They were rich in the city of Myra. They feared the Lord

extremely. They had no child. They were in great sadness concerning this. Continually they prayed and besought the Lord to give them a son, so that they might rejoice in him and that he might inherit their riches. They were thus without a child, until they grew old and the time to have a son had passed for them. They despaired of asking for a child. 3. - * Diocletian put Saint Nicholas in prison. But the Lord reserved him, so that he might become a great branch of the tree of faith. He remained in prison, until the Lord had made Diocletian (Diyogletyânos) disappear and had appointed Constantine (Quastantinos), the just emperor. Constantine brought all the confessors out of the prisons. This saint came out of prison, being of the


[p. 706] {#po.15.p706}
ሙ ፡ ተአማንያን ፡ እምውስተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ።፡ ወወጽአ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እም ጐልቆሙ ፡ ወገብአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔሩ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ከ መ ፡ ይጽንዑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ እስከ ፣ አመ ፣ ተጋብኡ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ጉ ባኤ ፡ ኤሏጺስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ፻፻፲ወ፳ ፦ በሀገረ ፡ ኒቅያ ፡ ወኮነ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እ
number of the confessors. He returned to his country and continued to teach the people, so that they might be firm in the right faith, until the assembly of the council of the three hundred and eighteen bishops met in the city of Nicaea (Niqyâ). This Father was one of them. He rebuked Arius ('Aryos), excommunicated him, cursed him, and drove him away.

4. - When this saint had finished his combat and had guarded his flock, he emigrated toward the Lord, after having sat on the episcopal seat for more than forty years. All the days of his life are eighty years. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer. Amen. Amen. So be it. So be it.

II. - Revision or Vulgate. 4. Salâm to the Translation of the body of Severus, patriarch of Alexandria. Greeting to the arrival of your body on this day, For it was brought, so that it might be at the monastery of Zegâg, Severus, destroyer of the heretic by the sword of the faith. One of your teeth that had fallen during your life has become itself the healing of the sick until today. 5. Salâm to Abba Theophanes, patriarch of Alexandria.
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፣፤ ለታኡፋንዮስ ፡ በሀቁ ። እምጐልቁ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ጐለቁ ። ዘምስለ ፣ ሳዊሮስ ፡ አብ ፡ ወኒቆላዎስ ፡ ልጹቁ ፡ ያንሥአኒ ፡ እምንዋየ ፡ ወያንቅሐኒ ፡ በጽድቁ ። እስመ ፡ መዋፅዕልየ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ በሀኬት ፡ አሕለቁ ፡=፡
I say greeting and again greeting to Theophanes, Who is counted among the number of the patriarchs And who is a neighbor of Severus, the Father, and of Nicholas. May he snatch me from my goods and awaken me by his holiness, For I have consumed all my days in laziness.

6. Fragment proper to the Death of Saint Nicholas, Bishop of Myra. 1. - When the parents of Saint Nicholas knew that they were sterile, that their days of fertility had passed and that they had grown extremely old, they ceased to ask for a child. From that day on, the Most High Lord was merciful toward them. He gave them Saint Nicholas (Nigoläwos). He made him holy, blessed, and perfect. [He] manifested in him the beginning of the practice of holiness.
-፦፦ ወውእቱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ”ማእከለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፪ሰዓት ፣ ወይኤምር ፡ በዝንቱ ፡በእንተ ፡፤ ተንሥኦቱ ፡ ለገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ። ወበጊዜ ፣፡ ይጠ ቡ ፡ አጥባተ ፡ እሙ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ይጠቡ ፡ ዘእንበለ የማናይ ፡ ጥብ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፣ ወይከሥት ፣ በዝንቱ ፡ ከመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ በኩሉ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ኢይሰቲ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እምነቅ ዓ፣ ምግባር ፡ ዘየማን ።
2. - He himself, when he was born, rose and stood upright * in the midst of the people for two hours. [He] showed thereby his readiness for the practice of justice. At the time when he was suckling at his mother's breasts, he would suckle only the right breast. [He] manifested thereby that he himself, all the days of his life, would drink only from the source of righteous works.

3. - He fulfilled the canons of the Apostles from his childhood. Indeed, on the day of Friday and Wednesday, he would only suckle his mother's breasts at the moment of the ninth hour of the day. 4. - When he was very young, his parents handed him over to a master. He continued to be instructed by the Holy Spirit extremely, at the same time as he was instructed by his master. In a few days, he learned all the teaching and all the law of the holy Church. He was ordained a deacon. He progressed in spiritual exercises. 5. - Then he became a monk in a monastery in which the son of his father's brother was superior. This saint practiced great asceticism in this monastery. Simple humans cannot do so. Because he practiced great holiness, he was ordained a priest while he was nineteen years old.
ወወሀቦ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ጸጋ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወገቢረ፣ ተኣምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ወ ፈውሶ ፡ ድውያን ። መኑ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ይትናገር ፡ተኣምራቲሁ ፡ ወመንክራቲሁ ፡ ዘኮነ፣ይ ትዌሰክ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ መካን ፡ በሕይወቱ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፍል ሰቱ ፡ ይደልወነ ፡ ”ከመ ፡ ንዝክር ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ገብረ ፡ በሕይወቱ ።
6. - The Lord gave him great grace: the accomplishment of miracles and wonders and the healing of the sick. Who could recount his miracles and his wonders, which increased every day, in every place, during his life and after his death? We must * mention some of those he performed during his life.


[p. 710] {#po.15.p710}
-፦ ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በሀገሩ ፡ ፳ብእሲ ፡ ባዕል ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ኃልቀ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ንዋዩ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ነዳየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኢረከበ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘይሴሰይ ፡ ሲሳ የ ፡ ፅለት ። ወሀለዋሁ ፣ ፪አዋልድ ፡ ወልህቃ ፡ ወኃለፈ ፥ ጊዜ ፡ ሰብሳቦን ፡ ወኢያስተ ዋሰቦን ፣ ለመኑሂ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ንዴቶን ፣ ወአሕለዮ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ርኩ ሰ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ቤተ ፡ ዘማውያን ፡ ወያንብሮን ፡ ለ፪8ውሉድ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡-ከመ፣ ይዘምዋ ፡ በዓስብ ፡ ወይርከብ ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ ወሲሳየ ፣ አዋልዲሁ ፡ አምዝሙት ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኒቆላዎስ ፡ ዘሀለየ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ብእሲ ፡ ወተንሥ አ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኒቆላዎስ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ፻ዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ እምንዋየ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወቄ ጸረ ፡ በፀርቅ ። ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይጽባሕ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ ወገረ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ብ እሲ ። ወሶዕበ ፡ ነቅሐ ፡፣ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፣ እምንዋሙ ፡ ረከበ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ወተፈ ሥሐ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዐቢየ ፣ ፍሥሓ ፣ ወአስተዋሰባ ፡ ለእንተ ፡ ትልህሀቅ ፡ ወለቱ ፣ ወከማሁ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ወገረ ፣ ሎቱ ፡፣ ፻ዲናረ ፣ ወርቅ ፣ ዳግመ ፡ ወአስተዋሰባ ፡ ለካልእት ፡ ወለቱ ። ወሣልሰ ፡ ወገረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፻ዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ወገረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ብአሲ ፡
7. - There was in his city a rich man. After many days, all his riches disappeared. He became poor, to the point that he could not find for himself the daily food with which to feed himself. He had four daughters. They had grown up and the time for their marriage had passed. But he did not marry them to any man because of their poverty. Satan (Saytân) suggested an impure thought to him: to make a house of prostitution and put his four daughters there, so that they would fornicate for a wage and that he would find his food and the food of his daughters through debauchery. The Lord revealed to Saint Nicholas what this man had thought of. Saint Nicholas rose at night. He took a hundred gold denarii from his patrimony and tied them in a piece of cloth. Before the morning shone, he threw them into the house of this man. When this man was awakened from his sleep, he found this gold. He rejoiced over it with great joy. He married off his eldest daughter. Likewise, Nicholas threw a hundred gold denarii to him a second time. This man married off his second daughter. A third time the saint


[p. 711] {#po.15.p711}
ንቁሐ ፡ ወኢነሥሐ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ አላ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምቤቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአይ ፡ መኑ ፡ ዘወገረ ፣ ወርቀ ። ወሶበ ፣ ወፅአ ፡ ረከቦ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኒቆላዎስ ፡ ወአአመረ ፣ ከመ ፣ው እቱ ፡ ወገረ ፡ "ሎቱ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ቦጊዜ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ሰገደ ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፣ ወገነየ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ብዙኃ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዕሜትከ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ እስመ ፣ አድኃንከኒ ፣፡ እምንዴተ ፡ ንዋይ ፣፡ ወእምወዲቅ ፡፣ ውስተ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ዘሐለይኩ ፡ እግበር ፣ ወአስተዋሰቦን ፣ ለአዋልዲሁ ፡ ለራብዒት ፡ ወለሣልሲት ።
threw a hundred gold denarii to him. But when he threw them to him, the man was awake. He did not take the gold, but he went out of his house to see who it was that was throwing the gold to him. When he had gone out, he found Saint Nicholas. He knew that it was he who * had thrown the gold to him three times. Immediately this man prostrated himself before him, at his feet, thanked him much, and said to him: "Your reward will be great in the kingdom of heaven, for you have saved me from the lack of money and from the fall into the sin that I had thought of committing." He married off his daughters, the fourth and the third.
ወእምዝ ፡ ሰደዶሙ ፡ ኒቆላዎስ ፡ ለሰይጣናት ፡ ብዙኃን ፣ አምላዕለ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወእምነ ፡ ዕፀው ፡ ዘኮኑ ፡ የኃድሩ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ወያስሕትዎሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፈወዕሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፣ ድውያን ፡ ወባረከ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ንስቲት ፡ ፍተታ ተ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወአጽገበ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ ሕዝበ ፤ እምኔሁ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ዘተርፈ ፡ አምውእ
8. - Then Nicholas drove out many demons from many people and trees, in which they dwelt to lead people astray. This saint healed many sick people. He blessed a few pieces of bread and satisfied many people with them. They took what remained of this bread and they found much more of it than there had been before.
ቱ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወረከብዎ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ፦ ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይሠየም ፡ጳጳሰ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ራእየ ፡፣ ዘከመ ፣ መንበር ፡ ዐቢይ ፡
9. - Before he was instituted as bishop, he had a vision. There was like a great seat set up. Magnificent priestly garments

ንቡር ፡ ወአልባሰ ፣ ክሀነት ፡ ክቡር ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ብርሃናዊ ፡ ዘይብሎ ፡ ልበስ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ አልባሰ ፡ ወንበር ፡ ዲበዝ ፡ መንበር ። -፦ ወካዕበ ፣ ርእየ ፣ በክልእት ፣ ሌሊት ፡ ከመ ፦ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወ ሀበቶ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ክህነት ፣ ወእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወሀቦ ፤ ወንጌለ ። -፦- ወሶበ ፣ ሞተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኤዲስ ፡ ቆይስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሚራ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፣፡መ ልአከ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወነገሮ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ቅዱ ስ ፡ ኒቆላዎስ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ወአምሳሎ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሐ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ነገሮሙ፣ ለኤሏስ ፡ ቆልሳት ፡ ዘርእየ ፣ ወአምኑ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይእቲ ፡ ራእይ ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ። ወነሥኦ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኒቆላዎስ ፡ ወሜሞ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሚራ ።
were on this seat. There was also like a luminous man who was saying to him: "Put on these garments and sit on this seat." 10. - Again, he saw, during another night, that Our Lady Mary (Mäâryâm) was giving him priestly garments and that Our Lord Christ was giving him a gospel. 11. - When the bishop of the city of Myra died, an angel * of the Lord appeared to the patriarch of the city of Rome (Romê). He explained to him about Saint Nicholas. He made known to him his name and his appearance. When the patriarch was awakened, he explained to the bishops what he had seen. They believed that this vision had come from the Lord. He took Saint Nicholas and instituted him bishop of the city of Myra.
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፣ መዋዕል ፤ ነግሠ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወአምለ ከ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምልኩ ፡ ለጣዖታት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አኃዞሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፣ መሃይምናን ፡ ወሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአኃዞ ፡
12. - A few days later, Diocletian (Diyogletyânos), the impious, reigned. He rendered worship to idols. He ordered all the people to render worship to idols. Immediately he arrested many of the faithful. [He] learned the fame of this


[p. 713] {#po.15.p713}
ወኩነኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፤ ዐቢየ ፣ ብዙኃ ፣ አመታተ ፡ ወእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ያጸ ንዖ ፡ ወያስተዔግሦ ፡ ወየዓቅቦ ፣ አእምኩነኔ ፣ ወያነሥኦ ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ሙስና ። ወዕበ ፣ ደክመ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፣፡ እምኩንኖቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኒቆላዎስ ፡ ወደዮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፣ሞቅሕ። ሰላም ፡ ለኒቆላዎስ ፡ እንተ ፡ ተወደሰ ፡ ዝክሩ ። በውስተ ፡ ሚራ ፡ሀገሩ። ተደሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወሕዘቢሁ ፡ አንከሩ ። ለዕለተ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ነጸሩ ፡=፡ መጠነ ፡ ፪ሰዓት ፡ ቀዊሞ ፡ በእግሩ ፡፡
saint. [He] arrested him and tortured him with great tortures for many years. But Our Lord Jesus Christ strengthened him, gave him courage, protected him from the tortures, and raised him up safe and sound, without injury. When Diocletian was tired of torturing Saint Nicholas, he put him in prison. Hail to Nicholas whose memory is celebrated in Myra, his city. The people were stunned and his fellow citizens were astonished, when they saw him, the day he was born, standing on his feet for two hours. 7. Martyrdom of the saints Thalassius and Lazarus.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ተላስስ ፡ ወአልዓዛር ። ' ወዝንቱ ፡ ተላስስ ፡ እምሰብአ ፣ ባቢሎን ፡ ዘይወለ ፡ ነነዌ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሳቦር ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ስግድ ፡ ለእሳት ፡ ወሁዕ ፡ ለአማልክት ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ተላስስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ለእግ
1. - On this day also were martyrs Thalassius (Talâsès) and Lazarus ('Al'âzâr). 2. - Thalassius was of the people of Babylon (Bâbilon), of the territory of Nineveh (Nanawé). Sapor (Sâbor), king of Persia (Fârès), said to him: "Adore the fire and sacrifice to the gods." Thalassius said: "As for me, I adore the Lord and I

ዚአብሔር ፡ አሰግድ ፣፡ ወሎቱ ፡ አሠውዕ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈጠረኒ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ሳቦር ፣ ያምጽኡ ፡ መቃኒተ ፡ ሥቃይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፍርሆ ፡ ለተላስስ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ኢፈርሃ ፡ እምትግር ምቱ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይቅሥፍዎ ፣ ፻ቅሥፈተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ነዋኅ ፡ ሰዓት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሥዕ ፡ ተላ ስስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታዕርፍ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተላስስ ፡ ሊተሰ ፡ ኢይትዓወቀኒ ፡ ዙነኔከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ ያድኅነኒ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ቀሠፍዎ ፡ ፪፻ጊዜ ፡ ወተከሉ ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወዘበጥዎ ፣ ፎመክድነ ፣ እስከ ፣ ይስእ"ን ፡ አውሥኦተ ፣ ወመተሩ፣ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ።
sacrifice to him, for he himself created me." Sapor ordered the instruments of torture to be brought, in order to frighten Thalassius. As Thalassius was not afraid of this scarecrow, Sapor ordered him to be struck with a hundred blows for long hours. He said to him: "Sacrifice, Thalassius, in order to rest from these tortures." Thalassius said to him: "I do not know your tortures, for my God will deliver me." Again, he was struck two hundred times. They drove spikes into his eyes. He was struck with seventy blows with a shield, to the point that he could not * answer. They cut off his head with the sword.
ወእምዝ ፡ አቅረብዎ ፡ ለአልዓዛር ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሳቦር ፡ ንጉሠ፣ ፋርስ ፡ ሥዕ ፡ ለአማልክት ፡ ወዕበ ፣ ዓበየ ፣ ሰጊደ ፣ ወሠዊዓ ፡ ለአማልክት ፡ ወረውዖ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ እሳት ። ወፈጸሙ ፡ ስምዖሙ ፡ በሠናይ ። ትንብልናሆሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፣ ፍቁሩ ፣፤ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ሰላም ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ተላስስ ፡ ቅሁፍ ።
3. - Then they brought Lazarus forward. Sapor, king of Persia, said to him: "Sacrifice to the gods." When he had refused to adore the gods and to sacrifice to them, they immediately threw him into the fire. They finished their martyrdom excellently. May their intercession be with their dear Za-Manfas-Qedous for ages of ages. Hail to you, Thalassius, scourged,


[p. 715] {#po.15.p715}
ወአልዓዛርኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሣጤ ፡ ነድ ፣ ግዱፍ ። አድኅኑኒ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እመሥገርተ ፡ ልሳን ፡ ወአፍ ። ኣ ከመ ፡ ያመሥጥ ፡ ወይጠል ፡ እመሥገርተ ፡ገሪፍ ። ወከመ ፡ ይድኅን ፡ እምጸንፈርት ፡ዖፍ ፡=፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አፅረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ሱርስት ። ወዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፡ ወለተ ፡ ክቡራን ፡ አምሰብአ ፣፡ ቀነስጥንጥንያ ። ወሐ ፀያ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወሶበ ፣ ሰምዓት ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ትቤሎ ፡ለአቡሃ ፣ አብሐኒ፣ ቅድመ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሖር ፡ ወእስግድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ወበተመይጦትየ ፡ ይከ ውን ፡ፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወይቤላ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ባኢ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ኀበ ፣ መጻምርትኪ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ከብካብኪ ፡ ተሐውሪ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወትፌጽሚ ፡ ብፅዓተኪ ። ወትቤ
And Lazarus, thrown into the interior of the flames. Save me from the snare of the tongue and the mouth, As the doe escapes from the meshes of the net And as the bird saves itself from the trap. 8. Death of Saint Soureset. 1. - On this day also died Saint Soureset. 2. - This saint was the daughter of noble personages of Constantinople (Questentenyä). Her father betrothed her to the son of a noble man. When she learned this, she said to her father: "Permit me first to go and prostrate myself in a sanctuary. Upon my return, the will of the Lord will be done." Her father said to her: "Enter first to your husband. When your wedding is finished, you will go with him to fulfill your vow." She said to him:


[p. 716] {#po.15.p716}
ሎ ፡ ተካየድክዎ ፣፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ እጸሊ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እንትኩ ፡ መካን ፡ ቅድስ ት ፡ እንዘ ፡ አነ ፣ ድንግልት ፡ ወእመ ፡ ሐሰውኩ ፡ ቃልየ ፡ ይረክበኒ ፡ ሥቃይ ፡ እምእ ግዚአብሔር ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አቡሃ ፣ ፈነዋ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወዓልት ፣፡ ወአዕማት ፡ አለ ፣ ይትለአ ክዋ ፣ ወወሀባ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፻፻ዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀብ ፡ ምጽዋተ ። -፦ ወበጺሓ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ፆደት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሎን ፡ መካናት ፡ ቅዱላት ።ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምኔተ ፡ ግብጻውያን ፡ረከበት ፡ አረጋዌ፣መነኮሰ ፡ ለቢሶ ፡ ሠቀ ፡ ወነገረቶ፣ ዙሎ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ልባ ። ወይቤላ ፣ ፈቃደ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለይኩን ፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተደለዉ ፡ እሊአሃ ፡ ለሐዊር ፡ ቦአት ፡፣ ውስተ ፣ መካነ ፣ ጽሙና ፣ ወጸሐፈት ፣ መጽሐ ፈ፣፤ ኀበ፣ ወላዲሃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፤ አንሰ ፣ አቅረብኩ ፡፣ ነፍስየ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወኢትኅሥሥኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢትረክቡኒ ፡ ወአሠረት ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ በልብሳ ፡ ወሜመቶ፣ ውስተ ፡ ንዋያ ፡ ወአምሰለቶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ተሐውር ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ።
"I have pledged myself to the Lord to go and pray in a holy sanctuary, * while I was a virgin. If I lie to my word, torment will reach me from the Lord." Her father, having heard this, sent her with guards and servants to serve her. He gave her gold - about three hundred gold denarii - so that she might give alms. 3. - Having arrived in Egypt, she visited all the holy sanctuaries. When she arrived at the convent of the Egyptians, she met an old monk, clothed in sackcloth. She explained to him everything that was in her heart. He said to her: "May the will of the Lord be done." Then, when her people had prepared to leave, she entered a sanctuary reserved for the solitary life. She wrote a letter to her parents, saying: "As for me, I have consecrated myself to the Lord. Do not look for me, for you will not find me." She attached the letter to her garment. She placed it in her luggage. She pretended to leave herself with her servants.

sanctuary of Golgotha (Golgotä), before our departure." One of them said to her: "Behold, our people have preceded us. How will you go alone?" She said to him: "What does that matter to you? I will go alone and I will return." Then she took her servant and entered the sanctuary of Golgotha. She said * to her servant: "Move away from me a little." 5. - Then she disappeared alone and arrived at the old monk. When he saw her, he rejoiced. She threw herself at his feet. She gave him the three hundred gold denarii, in order to distribute them to the poor. She asked him to make her a nun. Then he rose, shaved her head, and clothed her in monastic habits, that is to say, in sackcloth. He prayed over her and permitted her to go where the Lord would lead her. She entered a cave, while she was eighteen years old. She remained there for twenty-seven years, without seeing the face of men.
አይ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወሀሎ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሲላስ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ቃራ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ ፈላሲ ፡ ገዳማዊ ፡ ዘየኃድር ፡ በዓታተ ፣ ቀልሞን ፣ ወለለበዓላት ፡ ይሔው
6. - There was an ascetic monk, called Silas (Siläs), from the country of Qârâ. He had as a friend a hermit monk who lived in the caves of Qalmon. At each feast, he would visit him, bring him a little food, and be


[p. 718] {#po.15.p718}
ጾ ምስለ ፡ ንስቲት ፡ መብልዕ ፡ ወይትባረክ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወሶበ ፣፡ በጽሐ ፡ ፋሲካ ፡ ነሥ አ ፡ ኅዳጠ ፤ ኅብስተ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ይኅዓሥሦ ። ወሶዕበ ፡ ኃጥአ ፡ ደክመ ፡እ ምኃሚሦቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዔይል ፡፤ አድባረ ፡ ወአውግረ ፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ረከበ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ምክያ ደ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወተለወ ፡ ውእተ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ወረከበ ፡ ሙባአ ፡ ንኡሰ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ባርክ ፡ ላዕሌ የ ፡ኦአባ ፣፤ ቅዱስ ። ወዕበ ፡ ኃጥአ ፡ ዘያወሥኦ ፡ ቦአ ፣ ውስቴቱ ፡ ወረከበ ፡ መነኮሰ ፡ ንቡረ ፡-ጽምወ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ክልእ ፡ ለክልኡ ።
blessed by him. When Easter arrived, he took a little bread according to his custom and went to look for him. When he had not found him and he was tired of looking for him, while traversing the mountains and the hills, then he found the trace of a man's footsteps. He followed this trace. He found a small entrance. He said: "Bless me, O holy Father." When he had found no one to answer him, he entered the cave. He found a monk sitting quietly. They prostrated themselves before one another.
፦ ወሰአለ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለከ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ትጹሊ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ እ ስመ ፡ ካህን ፣ አንች ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምን ፡ ሲላስ ፡ ደንገፀ ፣ እፎ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ምሥጢሮ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወሐለየ ፣ በልቡ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እ ንጋ ፡ አው ፡ ሕፅው ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ከሠተት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ምግባራ ። ወአቅረበ ፡ ላቲ ፡ መብል ዓ ፡ ወኢበልዓት ። ወእምዝ ፡ እዕረፈት ፡ ወቀበራ ፤ ውስተ ፡ መካና ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሉታ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡=፡
7. - [He] asked him to say the prayer. But the monk said to him: "* It is for you to say the prayer, for you are a priest." When Silas heard this, he was stunned to learn how this monk knew his secret. After the prayer, they remained together. He thought in his heart, saying: "It is therefore a woman or a eunuch." Then she revealed her work to him. [He] offered her food, but she did not eat. Then she died. He buried her in the place where she was. May the Lord have mercy, through her prayer, on our king 'Iyâsou for ages of ages.


[p. 719] {#po.15.p719}
ሰላም ፡ ለሱርስት ፡ ዘቀሩስጥንጥንያ ፡ ሀገራ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንተ ፡ አፍቀራ። ሀየንተ ፡ አልባስ ፡ ዘክብር ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ሠቅ ፡ ገቢራ ። ነበረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓት ፡ እንበለ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ያእምራ ። ኑኃ ፣ ዓመታት ፡ ሂወካዕበተ ፡ ፳ራ ።= ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ጥዋሽ ። -፦- ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሕፅወ ፡ እምንዕሱ ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳ጳደብ ር ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋፅለ ።
Hail to Soureset, of the city of Constantinople, Whom the Lord loved. In place of precious garments, having taken garments of sackcloth, She remained in a cave, without anyone perceiving her, For forty-seven years. 9. Death of Abba Tewâsch. 1. - On this day died Abba Tewâsch. 2. - This saint was a eunuch from his childhood. He became a monk in a monastery. He continued to practice asceticism for many days.
፦ ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ እለ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸሊ ፡ው ስቴታ ፡ ረከበ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትበኪ ። ወሶዕበ ፡ ተስአላ ፡ ትቤሎ ፡ አነ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ አ ይሁዳዊት ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ እኩን ፡ ክርስቲያናዊተ ። ወነሥኣ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ፈቂዶ ፡ከመ ፡ ይር ብሓ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ ወኢይኩኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ በኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ወአጥመቃ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡
3. - One day when he was going to Alexandria ('Ela-'Eskendryä), in order to pray there, he met a woman who was weeping. When he had questioned her, she said to him: "I am a Jewish woman. I want to become a Christian." He took her with him, wanting to win her soul and wanting that there should not come upon him the condemnation

before the Lord. He baptized her with the Christian baptism. He began to traverse the markets with her and to beg. * The people of Alexandria were scandalized about him. They thought that it was his wife. They arrested him and brought him to Abba John (Yohannès), the merciful. They said to him: "Here is a man who dishonors the monks." He ordered the two monks to be beaten, separated, and chained. 4. - Abba John saw in a dream this monk showing him his back which was wounded. Abba Tewâsch was saying to him: "Why did you wound my back, without my having committed a fault?" Having awakened, Abba John ordered the monk to be brought. He ordered him to remove his garment, in order to see his wounds. By the will of the Lord his garment fell. He perceived that it was a eunuch. Abba John wept. He dismissed from their rank those who had brought him and had struck him. He barred them from the Eucharist for three years. He wanted to give him a hundred denarii, but Abba Tewâsch did not accept them from him. He went to his monastery and died, that day, in great piety.


[p. 721] {#po.15.p721}
ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡፣ ዓለም ፡=
May his intercession be with his dear Za-Manfas-Qedous for ages of ages.

10. Memory of the death of Mêlâyos and of Täwfyâ. On this day also is the death of Mêlâyos and of Täwfyâ. May their intercession be with us for ages of ages.

### THE 11 TAHSCHASCH

|. - Primitive recension. 1. Death of Abba Bakimos. In the name of the Father, of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.

1. - On this day died the holy Father Abba Bakimos. 2. - This saint was of the people of the city of Fis, of the territory of Médjel (Masil), in the north of Egypt (Gebs). While he was guarding his father's sheep and he was twelve years old, the angel of the Lord appeared to him in the appearance of a young man. He said to him: "Will you come, so that * we may depart together and become monks?" Abba Bakimos said to him: "Yes." Both agreed to rise and depart to the desert of Scetis ('Asqêtès). They met three old monks. Saint Abba Bakimos remained with them for twenty-four years. They died.


[p. 723] {#po.15.p723}
ት ፡ ወአገትዎ ፡ ከመዘ ፡ ይነስክዎ ። ወአእመረ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሕሊናሆሙ ፥ ወነፍሐ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ በኀይለ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወተዘርዉ ።፡፡

the Holy Spirit their design. He blew upon them by the power of the Lord and they were dispersed.


[p. 724] {#po.15.p724}

4. - Afterward he discovered a new valley. [He] remained there for three years, fasting each week. At the end of the week, he would take a handful of dates from the valley, eat them, and drink a little water. 5. - He recited two thousand four hundred prayers during the night and two thousand four hundred during the day. His prayer was: Our Father who art in heaven. He continued, for twenty-four years, to fast for periods of forty days. At the end of the fortieth day he would eat only once. He fasted once for eighty days, so that his skin adhered to his bones. Then the angel of the Lord brought him bread to eat and water to drink. These provisions remained with him for many years, without being exhausted. 6. - The Lord appeared in a nocturnal vision to Saint Bakimos and ordered him to return to his country. * He rose, departed, and arrived at a place situated outside of his country. He built himself a small dwelling there, where he remained alone. He became a refuge and a port for whoever arrived at him and a beautiful hope for all the people. They were consoled by his teachings and filled with emulation for his piety and his asceticism.
፦፦- ወበአሐቲዕለት፣።ጾሮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኀበ፣ ምድረ ፣ አፍራጦስ ፡፤ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኮኑ ፣ ይትዐደዉ ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ እምፍኖተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣

8. Another time, he was carrying baskets, going toward the world to sell them and buy his food. He became tired on the road. He set them down and sat, in order to rest a little. The power of the Lord transported him with the baskets and set him down in the place he wished.

9. Once, Saint Abba Sinoda (Sinodä) saw a column of precious stones that was extremely high. He was terrified and said: "What is this great column?" The angel of the Lord appeared to him and said: "It is Abba Bakimos." Immediately Saint Abba Sinoda arose and set out on foot, until he reached the land of Saint Abba Bakimos. He did not know him

by face. When he had met him and they had greeted one another, Abba Bakimos wanted to cook a little broth. He said to Abba Sinoda: "Take this pitcher, go fill it with water, and bring it to me." When he had filled the pitcher and brought it to him, he found the broth boiling and cooked. Then he knew that it was Abba Bakimos. He renewed his greetings and bows with him. He explained to him how he had had a vision regarding him. He stayed with him for a few days. 10. While they were walking together one day, they found a skull. Abba Sinoda stirred it with his staff, saying: "Rise, O dead one, so that you may explain and teach us what you have seen." The Lord commanded the soul of this dead man to return into him. He

rose immediately, prostrated himself before them, and explained to them entirely the state of Sheol and of those who are tormented there, each according to their categories. He explained to them regarding himself that he was a pagan. He said to them: "Below our feet, in Sheol, there are Christians who believed in Christ and did not fulfill his commandments, but followed the customs of the Gentiles in impurity." The saints said to him: "Lie down now and sleep." The dead man became as he was before. Then Saint Abba Sinoda greeted Saint Abba Bakimos and returned to his monastery.

the place where he was. Then he was a little ill. He saw the assembly of the saints coming toward him. He surrendered his soul into the hand of the Lord. The angels took it and chanted before it up to heaven. All the days of the life of Saint Abba Bakimos were seventy years. He remained twelve years in the world and fifty-eight years in asceticism. May his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen. 2. Commemoration of Saint Bartholomew, bishop.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ተዝካሩ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፣ በርተሎሜዎስ ፡ ኤጺስ ፡ ቆስ ፡፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡5፡
On this day also is the commemoration of Saint Bartholomew (Bartalomëwos), bishop. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us. Amen.

II. Revision or Vulgate. 3. Salâm to Abba Bakimos. Greeting to you who raised the dead in the desert. Accustomed to carrying baskets, You lived, Bakimos, from the wages of your toil and the remuneration of your labor. The Lord showed Abba Sinoda A luminous throne and a white crown of glory. 4. Salâm to Saint Bartholomew, bishop. Greeting to you, Bartholomew, our Father. It is just, for you were a bishop with righteousness, That by your holiness the power of your prayer may preserve us From whoever sets snares for us. As does the eyelid that preserves the eye.


[p. 730] {#po.15.p730}

### THE 12TH OF TAHSCHASCH
አመ ፡ ፲፪ለታኅሣሥ ። ፪. --፦- ፲066606108. %፤፲1ርበ101፻6. በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ፡፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝክሩ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፣ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፣ መላእክት ፡፡ ትንብልናሁ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡=፡
I. Primitive Recension. 1. Commemoration of Saint Michael, archangel. In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day is the commemoration of Saint Michael (Mikä'êl), archangel. May his intercession and his blessing be with us for ever and ever. Amen.


[p. 731] {#po.15.p731}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ካዕበ ፣ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ህድራ ፣ ዘእምደብረ ፡ እስዋን = ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡፡ -፦- ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ጉባኤ ፣፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኤሏስ ፣ ቆኦሳት ፡ ቀሳውስት ፡ ወዲያቆናት ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ በቀዳሚት ፣ ዓመተ፣ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለዳኪዮስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፣ በሚመቱ ፡ ለቆርኖልዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡፡ ወዶናስዮስ ፡ ሊ ቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡፡ ወቢላንያንዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፣ አንጾኪ ያ ፡፡ ወግርማኖስ ፡ ኤስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡፡
2. Commemoration of Abba Hedrä of Dabra-'Eswân. On this day also is the commemoration of Saint Abba Hedrä of Dabra-'Eswân. May his prayer and his blessing be with us forever. Amen. 3. Council of Rome. 1. On this day also is the assembly of the council of the holy bishops, priests, and deacons, in the city of Rome (Romé), the first year of the reign of Decius (Däkiyos), the infidel, under the pontificate of Cornelius (Qornolyos), patriarch of the city of Rome, of Dionysius (Donäsyos), patriarch of the city of Alexandria ('Eskendryâ), of Flavian (Bilänyânyos), patriarch of the city of Antioch ('Ansokiyä), and of Germanus (Germânos), bishop of the city of Jerusalem ('lyarousälêm).
ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ማኅበ”ር ፡ በእንተ ፣ ብናጥስ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዘስሕተ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እ
2. This council took place regarding Novatian (Benätès), the priest, who was wandering


[p. 732] {#po.15.p732}
ስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይክሕድ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ስደት ፡ ኢይትወከፍዎ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይኔስሕ ። ወዘወድቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝሙት ፡ ኢይትወከዋዎ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ንስሓ ፡ ካዕበ ። ወገሠጾ ፡ አብ ፡ ቆርኖልዮስ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ወኢተገሠጸ ። ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ፳ ፡ ኤጺኢስ ፡ ቆልሳተ ፡ ፲ወቿ ፡ ቀሳ ውስተ ፡ ወዲያቆናተ ፡ እማእምራነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወተዋሥኡ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብናጥስ ፡ ቀ ሲስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡-ነገር። --፦- ወተመክነየ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ በመልእክቱ ፡ ለዕብራዊያን ፡ እስ መ ፡ እለ ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ወጥዕሙ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ሰማያዊተ ፡ ወተወክፉ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ለአመ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ኢይትከሀሎሙ ፡ ይትሐደሱ ፡ በንስሓ ፡ ምፅረ ፤ ዳግመ ።፡ ወተሰጠውዎ ፡ እሉ ፡ አበው ፡ ወከሠቱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እስመ ፡
and saying: "Whoever has apostatized in the time of persecution, let him not be received when he does penance. He who has fallen into adultery, let him not be received either, if he does penance." Father Cornelius rebuked him regarding this, but Novatian did not amend himself. He gathered against him sixty bishops, eighteen priests, and deacons from among the scholars of the city of Rome. They discussed with Novatian, the priest, this question.


[p. 733] {#po.15.p733}
ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ኢይብል ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘይኔስሕ ፡ አላ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በእን ተ ፡ ዘይፈቅድ ፡ ይጠመቅ ፣ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ይወድቅ ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ ኃጢአት ።፡ እስመ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትናሰ ፡ ትከውን ፡ አሐተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወንስሓሰ ፡ ትትረከብ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡።
3. Novatian took as an argument the word of the apostle Paul (Päwlos) in the Epistle to the Hebrews: Those who have received the light, have tasted the heavenly grace, and have received the grace of the Holy Spirit, if they have fallen into sin, cannot be renewed by penance a second time. These Fathers answered him, unveiled it to him, and said: "The apostle Paul did not say this regarding
፦ ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዘወድቀ ፡ በክሕደት ፣ አው ፣ በኃጢአት ፡ ኢይትዌከፍኑ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ንስሓ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወለዳዊትኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ኢተወክፈኑ ፡ ንስሓሁ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ወለጴጥሮስኒ ፣ ሐዋርያ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ክሕደ ፡ ኢተወክፈኑ ፡ ንስሓሁ ፡ ወከንቶ፣ ወሀቦ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣፤ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ ወከንቶ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፣
the one who does penance, but he said this regarding the one who wants to be baptized with Christian baptism every time he falls into sin. Indeed, Christian baptism is given only once, but penance is obtained every time one has need of it.


[p. 734] {#po.15.p734}
ይዕቀብ ፡ አባግዒሁ ፡ ነባቢያነ ፡ ወይከውን ፣ ኩሉ ፡ዘተጠምቀ ፡ በእዴሁ፣ ኢይጠመቅ ፡ አላ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ አንተ ፡ ተሐጉጐሉ ፡ ወዝንቱሰ ፡ እበድ ፡ ዓቢይ = አላ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ንስሓ ፡ትትረከብ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘክሕደ ፡ በሃ ይማኖት ፡ አው ፡ ዘወድቀ ፡ በኃጢአት ፡=፡ ረሐቅ ፣ እምዝንቱ ፡ ምክር ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ኦብናጥ ስ ፡ ወነስሕ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወኢትኩን ፡ ጸላኢሁ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፣ ወጸላኢሆሙ ፡ለ ኩሎሙ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡፡
4. If one who has fallen into apostasy or into sin were not received when he does penance, as you yourself say, the Lord would not have accepted the penance of the prophet David (Däwit) and would not have accepted the penance of the apostle Peter (Pêtros) when he had denied him. In vain would the Lord have given him the grace of the Holy Spirit the Paraclete; in vain would he
ወኢተመይጠ ፡ እምክሩ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ወኢተወክፈ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ማኅበ ረ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡፡ ወረገምዎ ፡ ወአውገዝዎ ፡ ወሰደድዎ ፡ ወአውገዝዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፤ እለ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ በቃሉ ፡፡ ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ አበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለ መ፣ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡፡
have commanded him to guard his rational sheep. Whoever has been baptized by his hand has not been baptized, but all, as you yourself say, have perished. Now this is a great folly. Yet Our Lord Christ - glory to him - established that penance is obtained for anyone who has denied the faith or has fallen into sin. Turn away from this impure opinion, O Novatian, do penance regarding this, and do not become the enemy of the Lord and the enemy of all men."


[p. 735] {#po.15.p735}
፲፪. ፲:6:1810.. 0ህ “ሀህ19816. ሰላም ፡ ለጉባኤክሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ከኒሳ ። ኤጴስ ፡ ቆአሳት ፡ ስሳ ። ባሀለ ፡ ብናጥስ ፡ ታጽርኡ ፡ ዘተመሰለ ፡ እንስሳ ። ኢይትወከፉ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ንስሓሓ ፡ ለአመ ፡ ገብረት ፡ አበሳ ። እስመ ፡ ኢትነጽሕ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ እምርኩሳ ፡፡
5. He did not turn away from his evil opinion and he did not accept the proposal of the assembly of the holy bishops. They cursed him, excommunicated him, and drove him out. They excommunicated all those who believed his word. May the prayer and the blessing of all these holy Fathers be with us for ever and ever. Amen.
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት =፡ -፦ እስመ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ፈነዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ባቢሎን ፣ ወኮ ነ ፡ ራብዓ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፻ደቂቅ ፡ አናንያ ፡ ወአዛርያ ፤ ወሚሳኤል ፡ ውስተ ፡ እቶነ፣ እሳት ዕበ ፡ ገደፎሙ ፡ ናቡከደነጾር ፡ ንጉሠ ፣ ባቢሎን ፣ ወተለዓለ ፡ ነበልባለ ፡ እሳት ፡
II. Revision or Vulgate. 4. Salâm to the council of Rome. Greeting to your council held in a single assembly, Sixty bishops, In order to put a stop to the saying of Novatian, who had become like an animal. "If a soul has committed sin, let not its penance be accepted, he said, For it is not purified, a second time, from its impurity."


[p. 736] {#po.15.p736}
መጠነ ፡ ሻቋወ፪በእመት ፡ ወአውዓዮሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ያነድዱ ፡ አሳተ ።፡ ወሚክኤልሰ ፡ መል ኦክ ፡ ዘበጦ ፡ በበትረ ፣ ዚአሁ ፡ ለላህበ ፣ እሳት ፣ ወአጥፍኦ ፡ እምላዕለ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ ወአ ድኃኖሙ ፡ ወኢለከፎሙ ፡ እሳት ፣ "ግሙራ ። ወረሰዮ ፡ ለእቶነ ፣ እሳት ፣ ከመ፣ ጠ ል ፡ ቂቄሪር ፡ ዘጊዜ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡፡
When Nebuchadnezzar (Näboukadanasor), king of Babylon, had thrown them into the furnace, the flame of the fire rose forty-nine cubits and consumed those who had lit the fire. The angel Michael struck the flame of the fire with his rod and extinguished it from upon the three children. He delivered them and the fire did not touch them at all. He made the furnace of fire like the cool dew of the morning hour.
-፦ ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ሰብሕዎ ፣ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ይትባረክ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ስቡሕኒ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልዑልኒ ፤ ውእቱ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወተነበዩ ፡ ጸጊዜ ፡ከመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ጳ፻ዓመት ፡ ይትወለድ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሰብሕዎ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ይባርክዎ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ለእግዚ አብሔር ፤ ወበዝንቱኒ ፡ ተነበዩ ፡ ቋወ፻ጊዜ ፣ ከመ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ይነብር ፡ ዲበ፣ ምድ ር ፡ በወ፻ዓመተ ። ወበእንተዝ ፣ ሠርዑ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ በዓሎ፣ ለሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላአክት ፡፡ ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፣፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
3. At the same moment they glorified the Lord, saying: Blessed is the Lord, the God of our Fathers; he is glorified; he is exalted forever. Seven times they prophesied that at the end of seven hundred years Christ would be born. Then they glorified the Lord, saying: Let all the works of the Lord bless the Lord. By these praises also they prophesied thirty-three times that Christ would dwell on the earth for thirty-three years. This is why the apostles, our Fathers, have decreed that the feast of the archangel Michael be celebrated. May his intercession be with his dear Za-Manfas-Qedous for ever and ever.


[p. 737] {#po.15.p737}

Greeting to you who preserved the saints from agitation, And who chained the enemy of their soul. When the Son came, whom the mouth of the prophets had preached, You followed him, Michael, up to the fifth firmament, So that the army of his angels might escort him. 6. Death of Abba Samuel of Wäldebâ. 1. On this day also died Abba Samuel (Sämou'êl) of Wäldebâ. 2. The name of the father of this saint was Stephen ('Estifânos) and the name of his mother was 'Amata-Mäâryâm. They were of excellent stock. They gave birth to this Father in the land of Axum ('Aksoum). When he grew up, they taught him the books of the Church. 3. Then, when his father and his mother were dead, he went to Dabra-Bankual

to Abba Madhanina-'Egzi'e. He took the monastic habit. He continued to practice asceticism through fasting and prayer. He ate only foul vegetables filled with worms. His drink was water. He continued to serve the monks, by grinding grain and drawing water. 4. When his family reached him, he went to another place. He practiced devotion through fasting, through prayer, by standing and by prostrating himself, so that his feet had become swollen. 5. From there he went to the desert. He remained forty days and forty nights without tasting anything. Lions, leopards, and all kinds of fearsome beasts came toward him, prostrated themselves before him, and licked the dust of his feet. 6. One day, while traveling, he reached a river that was full of water. He had with him a book and fire. After having said a prayer, he entered the water. The water engulfed him and made him descend to the bottom. When by the power of the Lord it had cast him onto the other bank, he noted that the fire was not extinguished and that the book, neither, was not obliterated.

7. Satan (Saytân) tested him with numerous artifices, by taking various aspects of fearsome beasts. But he himself was not terrified, for his spirit was attached to trust in his God.

8. While Abba Samuel was praying, Our Lord Jesus Christ came toward him. He signed all his limbs with his saliva and filled him with strength. From that day on, Abba Samuel chained his feet and wore a hairshirt. Every night, he would enter the water, recite the Psalms of David (Däwit) five times in a row, and strike his back with countless blows.

10. Then numerous disciples attached themselves to him. The first among them was Abba Za-Raphael (Za-Roufä'êl). One day, Abba Samuel met

with Abba Gabra-Masqal of Dabra-Lagäso. Indeed, they only saw each other at that moment. They spent the day recounting the greatness of the Lord. At the time of supper, when they had said the prayer, the supper descended for them from heaven. They ate and gave thanks to the Lord. 11. Then one day, he met a hermit. When they had begun to explain the greatness of the Lord and the mysteries that he had worked for them, our Father Samuel said: "Here for me are twelve years that I have been standing in the high heavens and that I have been incensing the throne of God with the twenty-four priests of heaven." When he entered the church to celebrate the mass, the bread and the chalice descended for him from heaven.
ወዕበ ፡ ያነብብ ፡ ውዳሴሃ ፣ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ተለዓለ ፡ እምድር ፡ መጠነ ፡ እመት ፡ ወመጽአት ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወ ወሀበቶ ፡ ዕንቄ ፡ ዘያበርህ ፣ ወዕጣነ ፣ ንጹሐ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፣ ፍልሰቱ ፡ መጽአ ፣ ኅቤሁ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ሊቀ ፡መ ላእክት ፣ ወመሠጦ ፡በክነሬሁ ፡ ወአርአዮ ፣ ዙሎ ፡ ተድላሃ ፡ ለኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡
12. Once when he was reciting the Lauds (the Weddäsë) of Our Lady Mary (Märyâm), he was raised from the earth by a cubit. Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary came toward him and gave him a brilliant gem and pure incense. 13. Then when his passing was near, the archangel Michael (Mikä'êl) came toward him. He snatched him up on his wings, made him see all the glory of Jerusalem

celestial (Jerusalem) and brought it before the throne of the Lord. He received the promise (the kidan) of divine protection for whoever would invoke his name and commemorate him. Then he returned to his couch. He explained to his disciples everything he had seen. Then he died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with his scribe for ever and ever. Hail to you, Samuel, who made the rule flourish and who were the vine-shoot of the clusters of the grape of life. Grant me your help and give me your hand to bring me to where you have ascended. Your help, indeed, delivered a female dog, so that she might remain constantly with the blind. 7. Salam to Abba Hedra of Dabra-'Eswan. Hail to you, pious Hedra, of Dabra-'Eswan. When we invoke you always, in every * place,


[p. 742] {#po.15.p742}
ይሰደድ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ወይኾየይ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ከመ ፡ አስሐትያ ፡ ይሰደድ ፡ በነፋስ ፣ ቀጢን ፡፡ -፦ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፣፡ ስምዓ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አንቂጦስ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ።
May Satan be driven away and flee through your prayer. As snow is driven away by a light wind. 8. Martyrdom of Saint Anicetus and Saint Photinus 1. - On this day also was the martyrdom of Saint Anicetus ('Angitos), in the time of Diocletian (Diyogletyanos), the infidel.
-፦ ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አንቂጦስ ፣ ሰማዕት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ መዓብልተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዘአግበሮ ሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፍርሆሙ ፡ ለምእመናን ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ እማእከ ሎሙ ፡ በጥቡዕ ፡ ልብ ፣ ወተዛለፎ፣፤ለንጉሥ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ አዘ ዘ ፡ ይእስርዎ ፡ ወያዕርግርዎ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቲያጥሮን ፣ ወይስድዱ ፡፣ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አንበሳ ፣ ጸዋ ገ ። ወበጺሖ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ የማናየ ፡ እዴሁ ፣ ወመዝመዘ ፡ ገጾ፣ ወመላ ትሒሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አንቂጦስ ።
2. - When this holy martyr Anicetus had seen the instruments of torture that the emperor had had placed before him, in order to frighten the faithful, he stood up in their midst with an intrepid heart and rebuked the emperor. Having heard, the emperor Diocletian ordered him to be chained, to be taken up to the amphitheater, and to have a furious lion released against him. The lion, having reached him, extended its right paw and caressed the face and cheeks of Saint Anicetus.


[p. 743] {#po.15.p743}
ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርአሶ ፣ በሰይፍ ፡ ወሶ በ ፣፡መልሐ ፡ ሰይፎ ፡ ሰገራታዊ ፡ ርእደ ፡ ወስእነ ፡ ተሐውሶ ። ፦ ወካዕበ ፣ ወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንኩራራት ፡ ዘርቡብ ፡ ታሕቴሁ ፡ አፍሓ መ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወበታሕቴሁ ፡ መናግንተ ፡ መንኩራዙር ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትበተክ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ፍ ጡነ፣ሮእምዝኒ ፣ አድኃኖ ፣፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። ወካዕበ ፡ ወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽሕርት ፣፡ ዘአፍልሑ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ አረረ ። ወእ ንዘ ፡ ይኔጽሩ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ መጽአ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወመሠጦ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ጽ ሕርት፣ ወአቀሞ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ።
3. - When Diocletian had seen this, he ordered his head to be cut off with the sword. When the lictor had drawn his sword, he trembled and could not move. 4. - Furthermore, he was put into a wheel, under which burning coals were spread. Beneath it, the mechanism of the wheel was arranged in such a way that his spirit would be torn from his body quickly. From this torture * also the Lord delivered him without injury. 5. - Moreover, he was put into a cauldron, in which lead was boiled. While the people were watching, the angel of the Lord came, snatched him from the cauldron, and placed him before Diocletian.

6. - When Photinus (Fotinos) saw this miracle, he stood up naked, greeted his brother Anicetus, and cursed the tyrant, saying: "O miserable one, how is it that you are defeated by my brother?" Having heard, Diocletian ordered shackles to be put on their necks and fetters on their feet and for them to be thrown into prison. Furthermore, he ordered them to be taken out of prison and torn

with iron claws, so that their bodies and bones would be broken. Again, he ordered them to be thrown into prison, to be led to the amphitheater, and to be stoned with stones. Nothing touched them. Furthermore, they were flogged severely. Salt was put on their wounds. Moreover, they were thrown into the furnace of a bathhouse. They were found telling of the greatness of the Lord.
፦ ወርእዮ ፤ መምዓሊ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ያንድዱ ፡ እቶነ ፡ አሳት ፡ እስከ ፡ ይ ትሌዓል ፡ ነበልባሉ ፡፣ ወወረውዎሙ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ወቀዊሞሙ ፡ ዲቤ ሁ ፤ ገብሩ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ነዊኃ ፣ ወሐተሙ ፡ ገጾሙ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈ ስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 8አምላክ ፣ ወአወፈ"”ዩ ፣ ነፍሶሙ ፣ ውስተ ፡ እደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። -፦ ወነበረ ፣ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አፍሓመ ፡ እሳት ፣ እምጊዜ ፡ ፪ሰዓተ ፣ መዓል ት ፡ እስከ ፡ 8ሰዓተ ፣ መዓልት ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘለከፈ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ወኢውዕየ ፡ ሥዕርተ ፡ር አሶሙ ፡ ምንተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ነሥኡ ፣ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡
7. - Having seen this, the tyrant became angry. He ordered a furnace of fire to be lit, until the flames rose high. The holy martyrs were thrown into it. Standing upright in the furnace, they made a long prayer, signed their faces in the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, one God, and * committed their souls into the hand of the Lord. 8. - Their bodies remained on the burning coals from the second hour of the day until the ninth hour of the day. Nothing touched their bodies. The hair of their heads was not burned in the least. When it was night, some

faithful took their bodies, shrouded them as was fitting to do to honor them, and buried them in a beautiful tomb. Miracles were manifested through them. May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear Za-Manfas-Qedous for ever and ever. With a pleasant thought I say hail to Anicetus, martyr, arrested with Photinus. When the word of the emperor had ordered him to be thrown to the beasts, He transformed the ferocity of the lion into gentleness, So that the latter caressed the cheeks of Anicetus and wiped his beard.

So that I, a young man, poor in all knowledge, may find myself there Where the virgins strike the tambourine. 9. Memory of 'Awsis, martyr, of Abba Anthony and of John,
ወካክፅበ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአውሲስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወአባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፣ ወዮሐንስ ፡ ተአማኒ ፡ ወመምህር ፡ ዘሮሜ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፤ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ፣ ኢያሱ ፣ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም =፡ አሜን። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሩፋኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡=፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይዕቀቦ ፡ ለፍቁሩ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አላሴ ፣ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ሩፋኤል ፡መንፈስ ፡፡ መልአከ ፡ ጥዒና ፡ ወፈውስ ፡፡
confessor and doctor of Rome. On this day also is the memory of 'Awsis, martyr, of Abba Anthony ('Entonyos) and of John (Yohannes), confessor and doctor of Rome (Rome). May their prayer and their blessing be with our king 'Iyasu for ever and ever. Amen. 10. Feast of the Archangel Raphael. On this day also is the angel Raphael (Roufa'el). May the Lord guard his dear Walda-'Elaase for ever and ever. Hail to you Raphael, spirit, Angel of health and healing.


[p. 747] {#po.15.p747}
አመ ፡ ሰአሉ ፡ ኅቤክከ ፡ ዕደወ ፡ ተፅናስ ፡=፡ ከመ ፡ አብዓልኮሙ ፡ ለጵጵስና ፡ ወንግሥ ፡=፡ አብዕለኒ ፡ በንጽሕከክ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ርኩስ ፡
When miserable men had prayed to you, Just as you enriched them with the episcopate and with royalty, Just so enrich me with your purity, I who am an impure man.

I. - Primitive recension. 1. Conception of Saint Anne. In the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day Saint Anne (Hanna), the righteous, conceived Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary (Maryam). May her intercession be with us. Amen.

2. Martyrdom of Saint Barsanuphius. 1. - On this day also is Saint Barsanuphius (Barsenoufyos), martyr, in the days of the Muslims. 2. - This saint was a monk in the church of the Archangel Gabriel (Gabr'el), in the city of Old Cairo (Mesr), on the bank of the river. This saint practiced great asceticism. He debated with * the leaders of the Muslims concerning the right faith. He made manifest to them the divinity of Our Lord Christ. That is why the Muslims became angry with him. They tortured him with great torture and cut off his head with the sword. He received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May his prayer and his blessing be with us for ever and ever. Amen. Amen.


[p. 749] {#po.15.p749}

3. Death of Saint Apraxius. 1. - On this day died Saint Apraxius ('Abrakos). 2. - This saint was from the people of Upper Egypt (La'lay-Gebs). He put on the monastic habit when he was twenty years old. He practiced beautiful asceticism. When Satan (Saytan) was tired of fighting against him, was defeated in waging war against him, and could no longer do anything against him, he came to find him openly, face to face. He said to him: "Behold, of your days there still remain to you fifty years." He wanted by this to make him fall into laziness. The holy old man answered him and said to him: "You sadden me: for my part, I thought I would live another hundred years. That is why I would have given myself to laziness. But if it is so, as you say, we must fight before death." Then he redoubled his asceticism and he practiced great devotion.


[p. 750] {#po.15.p750}

That year, he migrated to the Lord. He was finishing his seventieth year of asceticism. May his prayer and his blessing be with us for ever and ever. Amen. 4. Death of Abba Misael, hermit.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሚሳኤል ፡፣ ገዳማዊ ፡ በደብ ረ ፡ ቀለሞን ፡= ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ፡፡
On this day also died the holy Father Abba Misael (Misa'el), hermit, at the monastery of Qalamon. May his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen. Amen.

In the church of Gabriel, messenger of the Annunciation. After a leader of the land of the Muslims, because of the right faith, Had tortured his body with many tortures, He obtained by his beheading a high place in heaven. 6. Salam to the Conception of Saint Anne. Hail to your conception in the womb of the blessed Anne, Mary, bush of Sinai (Sina). I have heard the sound of the Word who will judge my soul. In order to see the place from which your salvation will come, Intelligence makes my heart ascend to the high places. 7. Salam to Saint Apraxius.
ሰላም ፣ ለአብራክዮስ ፡ ዘመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ትሩፋተ ። መጠነ ፡ ዓመታት ፡ ዛሶበ ፡ አሰፈዎ ፣፡ ሐሰተ ። ለሰይጣን ፣ ሞኦ ፡ በብሂል ፡ ዘንተ ። እፎ ፡ አጽራዕኩ ፡ ጻምዎ ፡ ወተሐከይኩ ፡ ሀኬተ ። እንዘ ፡ ይመስለኒ ፡ ዘአሐዩ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ክረምተ ፡፡
Hail to Apraxius, whose life was remarkable. When the demon had falsely promised him fifty new years, He defeated Satan, saying this: "How would I interrupt the labor and give myself to laziness, Since I believe I will live for many years?"
