8. Salam to Abba Misael. Hail to you, Misael, priest, Anchorite of the convent of Qalamon. Give me a sign to enter where sadness is forgotten, As the womb forgets its pains, When the child is born from it. 9. Life of Abba Macarius. 1. - On this day also is the memory of Abba Macarius (Magaryos), who fed himself on partridges.
-፦ ወዝንቱ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃሪ ፡ ኃሠሠ ፡ እምንአእሱ ፡ ተሊዎቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ። ወተምህረ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአእመረ ፡ ኅልፈቶ ፡ ለዓለም ፡
2. - This Abba Macarius (Magari) applied himself, from his childhood, to follow the living Lord. He studied the books of the Church. He understood that the world was ephemeral and that there was a reward for the righteous and a


[p. 753] {#po.15.p753}
ወከመ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ዕሜት ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ ወፍዳ ፡ ለኃጥኣን ። ወበእንተዝ ፡፣ ኃደገ ፣ ዓለመ ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ 8ደብር ። ነ
punishment for the sinners. That is why he abandoned the world and became a monk in a convent.
--፦- ወእምህየ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ምሀዋረ ፡ ዓሠር ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጸ፳ደብር ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡- ዙዕንት ፡ ወቆቃውሕ ፡ ወማ ይ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወዕቤሃ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዕበ ፡ እወፍር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዙዕንት ፡ ይጸራዕ ፡ ግብር የ ፡ ወጸሎትየ ፡ ወአልብየ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ዘያዓቱ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ አነ ፡ ወዘይቤ ፡ ኢትብላፅ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ቢጽክ ፡ በሐሜት ፡ ወያአምር ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ ከመ ፡ አ ልብየ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ሲሳይ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ እላንቱ ፤ ወእምይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አኃዘ ፡ ያሥግሮን ። ወይሠገራ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በበ፳ ፡ ወያበስሎ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሐምለ ፡ ወይሴሰይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይሰቲ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ማይ ። ፦ ወያአዙኩት ፡ ወይስእል ፡ ወይጴሊ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወይተግህ ፡ ዙሎ ፡
3. - From there he left for the desert, a ten days' journey. He arrived at a mountain on which there were only roots, partridges, and much water. Immediately he had a thought and said: "When I go to the fields to pull up roots, my work and my prayer will be interrupted. I have no disciple to bring them to me, for I am a solitary. My God who said: 'Do not eat flesh: is not flesh your companion in outrage?' knows that I have no other food than these partridges." From that day on he began to catch them. They were caught for him one by one. He would cook each partridge and it would become for him a vegetable. He himself fed on what the Lord gave him. He drank water. 4. - He gave thanks, implored, and prayed to the Lord. He kept vigil all the


[p. 754] {#po.15.p754}
ሌሊተ ። ወበዘከመዝ ፡ ግብር ፡ ነበረ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ወኢይሰምዕ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወኢይሬኢ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወ” ኢይትናገር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ኢሐመየ ፣፤ ወኢጸረፈ፣ ኢመነሂ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኢይመጽእ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰብእ ። ፦፦ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡መጽአ ፡ 8መነኮስ ፡ እምቀ“ስጥንጥንያ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ይነብር፣ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃሪ ፡ እንዘ ፣ የኃሥሥ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ለአባ ፡ መቃሪ ፡ እን ዘ ፡ ያሠግሮን ፣ ለእማንቱ ፡ አዕዋፍ ፡ ኢተዓዝ ፡ አላ ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ ጐጉዓ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅት ል ፡ እጉሁ ፡ በሐሜት ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ቀኑስጥንጥንያ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዜኑ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘርእየ ። ወይቤ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ከመ ፡ እኅሥሥ ፡ በዓታተ ፡ ወበህየ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ መነኮሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፤ ወያሠግሮን ፡ ለአዕዋፍ ፡፣ ወይበ ልፅ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርስሖመ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘይገብር ።
nights. He persevered in such work for many years. He did not hear the speech of men; he did not see the face of men; * he did not converse with men. He did not insult or outrage anyone. He said: "Satan will only come with men." 5. - Then came a monk from Constantinople (Questentenya) to where Saint Abba Macarius lived: he was exploring the desert. When he saw Abba Macarius catching birds, he did not restrain himself, but on the contrary he hastened to kill his brother with insult. He arrived in Constantinople and entered the patriarch's house to tell him everything he had seen. He said: "For my part, I went to the desert, in order to visit the caves. There I saw a monk living as a solitary, catching birds and eating flesh, to the point of scandalizing people who might see what he was doing."
-፦ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ዘንተ ፣ ፈነዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ላእክ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያአምር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ወይጠይቅ ፡ ለአመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እሙነ ፡ ዘነገሮ ፡ መነ ኮስ ፡ ዘሖረ ፤ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይብጽሐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ሖረ ፡ አባ፣
6. - When the patriarch had learned this, he sent a monk with a servant, to know everything that was happening and to know with certainty if what the monk who had gone to find him had told him was true. Before


[p. 755] {#po.15.p755}
መቃሪ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያሥግር ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ. ፣ ወተሠግራ ፡ ፻አፅዋፍ ፣ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ መሥ“ርት ። ወሐለየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኪያየኑ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ያሜክር ፡ ወሀበኒ ፡ የአዕዋፈ ፡ ወሚመ ፡፣ ኢጸግበትኑ ፣ ከርሥየ ፤ እምቅድመዝ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወእመኒ ፡ ለካልኣን ፡ ኮነ ፡ አ ልቦ ፡ አመ ርኢኩ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ውስተዝ ፡ ገዳም ።
the envoys arrived, while they were on the way, Abba Macarius went, according to his custom, to catch birds. Three birds were caught in the same trap. He thought and said: "Has the Lord given me these three birds to test me, or has my stomach not been satisfied before this day? If it is for others, I confess I have never seen people in this desert."
-፦- ወዘንተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሄሊ ፡ በጽሑ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ”መነኮሳት ፡ አለ ፡ ተፈነዉ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ዕበ ፣ ርእዮሙ ፣ ወይቤ አአኩተከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ዘ ወሀብከኒ ፡ ሲሳዮሙ ፡ ለአግብርቲከ ፡ አእሚረከ ፡ ንዴትየ ፣ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ይትቃጸብዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያቄርብ ፣ ሎሙ ፡ ማፅደ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ንሥኡ ፡ ኦአኃውየ ፡ ወባርኩ ፡ ወውእ ቱሰ ፡ አኃዘ ፡ ንዋዮ ፣ ይብላዕ ፣ በጽሚት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ነገር ። ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ዓበይዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕነሰ፣ ኢንበልፅ ፣ ሥጋ ፡ እስመ ፣ መነኮሳት፣ ንሕነ ፣ ወብነ ፡ ትሕርምት ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢንብላፅ ፡ ሥጋ ። ወውእቱሰ ፦ ኀደጎሙ ፣ ወኢያገበሮሙ ፡ ወአኃዞን ፣ ለእማን
7. - While he was having this thought, * the monks who had been sent by the patriarch arrived. When he saw them, he rejoiced and said: "I give thanks to you, O Lord, knowing my poverty, for having given me the food of your servants." They themselves mocked him, while he was setting the table for them. He said to them: "Take, O my brothers, and bless." As for him, he took his bowl to eat apart, without speaking. But they refused him and said to him: "As for us, we do not eat meat, for we are monks; we practice abstinence, so that we do not eat meat." He himself left them and did not force them. He took


[p. 756] {#po.15.p756}
ቱ ፡ አዕዋፍ ፡ ዘአብሰሎን ፡ ወነፍሐ ፡ ላዕሌሆን ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ ወሰረራ ፡ ወገብኣ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካናቲሆን ፡ ከመ ፣ ዘኢተሠግራ ።
the birds that he had cooked and blew on them three times. They flew away and returned to their places, as if they had not been caught.
፦ ወዕበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወይ ቤልዎ ፡ስረይ ፡ ለነ ፡ ኦቅዱሱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አበስነ ፡ ወጌገይነ ፡ ላዕሌከ ። ወይቤሎ ሙ ፡ ይስረይ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡'አበሳክሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኃጥእ ። ወዘንተ ፡ › ርእዮሙ ፡ አንከሩ ፣ ወገብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡፣ ወዜነዉ ፡ ዙሎ ፣ ተአምራቲሁ። ፦ ወሰሚዖ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያነክር ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ተረክበ ፣ በመዋዕሊከ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ንሖር ፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንን ሣእ ፡ በረከቶ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፣ ወቀሳውስት ፡ ወዲያቆናት ። ወሶበ ፡ ቀርቡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ መልአ ፤ ክ ፡ በደመና ፡ ወጾሮ ፡ ”ከመ ፡ ያብጽሖ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ሕያዋን ።
8. - When they had seen this miracle, they themselves were astonished. They prostrated themselves before him and said to him: "Forgive us, O saint of the Lord: we have sinned and faulted against you." He said to them: "May the Lord forgive you your sin, for I too am a sinner." Having seen this, they were astonished. They returned to their country and told of all his miracles. 9. - Having learned this, the patriarch, astonished, went to find the king and said to him: "Behold, in your days there is a righteous monk. Come, let us go to find him, in order to receive his blessing." The king rose with his troops and with the patriarch, the bishops, the priests, and the deacons. When they were near him, an angel took him on a cloud and transported him, * in order to bring him to the land of the living.


[p. 757] {#po.15.p757}
ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡እንዝዘ ፡-የዓርግ ፣ ይቤልዎ ፡ ባርከነ ፡ ኦቅዱሱ ፡ ለእግዚአብ ሔር ፡ወንግረነ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ቃለ ። ወይቤሎሙ ይጹም ፡ ልሳንክሙ ፡-እምነገረ ፣፡ ውዴት። ካህን ፡ እመ ፡ ኢተምህረ ፡ ብዙኃ ፤ መጻሕፍተ ፡ እምኢያምጽኦ ፡ ትዕቢተ ፣ ወትዝሀር ተ። ወመነኮስ ፡እመ፣ ኢያብዝኃ ፡ ለትምህርት ፡ እምኢያምጽኦ ፡ ትምክሕተ ። ተፋቀሩ ፡ በበይናቲክሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፣፤ ኩልክሙ ።፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ዓርገ፣ እምቅድሜሆሙ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ሰላም ፡ ለመቃርዮስ ፡ ዘተመስጠ ፡ መልዕልተ ። በቅድመ ፡ ጉቡኣን ፡ ይንብብ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ቃላተ ። እምኢያእመሩ ፡ ትዕቢተ ፣ ወዘይመስሎ ፡ ትምክህተ ። እምኢያፈድፈደ ፡ ካህን ፡ ትምህርተ ። ወእምኢያብገኃ ፡ ክፅበ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡፡
10. - When they saw him ascending, they said to him: "Bless us, O saint of the Lord, and speak a word to us." He said to them: "Let your tongue fast from slanderous words. The priest, if he did not study many books, would not bring pride and boasting upon himself. The monk, if he did not develop science, would not bring pride upon himself. Love one another. May the Lord be with you all." Having said this, he ascended before them. May his holy blessing be with his dear Za-Manfas-Qedous for centuries of centuries. Salutation to Macarius, who was caught up into the high places And who said these words before people gathered having asked him to speak: "If the priest did not increase science And if the monk, likewise, did not develop science, They would not know pride, nor the boasting that resembles it."


[p. 758] {#po.15.p758}
'- ወካዕበ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ተዝካረ ፡ አብራኒቆስ ፣ ሰማዕት ፣ ወሐርሶፎን ፣ ወቄርኔሌ ዎስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወወርቅጥኦፅስ ፡ ወእርፋዴ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡=
10. Memory of 'Abränigos, martyr, of Harsofon, of Cornelius, magistrate, of Wargetsos and of 'Erfâdé. On this day also is the memory of 'Abrâniqos, martyr, of Harsofon, of Cornelius (Qérnëléwos), magistrate, of Wargetsos and of 'Erfâdé. May their prayer and their holy blessing be with their dear King 'Iyâsou for centuries of centuries.

### THE 14TH OF TAHSCHASCH


[p. 759] {#po.15.p759}
አመ ፤ ፲፪8ለታኅሣሥ ። ፲866630610ጪ ጅ፲1:010176. በስመ ፡ አብ ፣ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ፡=፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ መኑፍ ፡ እምደበብ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በመዋዕሊሆሙ ፡ ለተንባላት ፡፡ ጸሎቱ ፣ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፣፤ ቅዱሳን ፣ ሰማዕታት ፡ አባ ፣ ብሑር ፡ ወአባ ፡ ሚና ስ፣አረጋዊ ፡= ጸሎቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡=፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ኮነ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ መርምህናም ፣ወእኅቱ ፡ ሳራ ፡ሰማዕት ፡=፡
|. - Primitive recension. 1. Martyrdom of Saint Simon of Manouf. * In the name of the Father, of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day is Saint Simon (Sem'on), martyr, of the city of Manouf, in the north of Egypt (Gebs), in the days of the Muslims. May his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen. 2. Martyrdom of Abba Behour and Abba Minäs. On this day also are the holy martyrs Abba Behour and Abba Minäs, the elder. May their prayer be with us. Amen. 3. Martyrdom of Saint Marmehnäm and his sister Sara. 1. - On this day also are Saint Marmehnäm and his sister Sara (Sarä), martyrs.


[p. 760] {#po.15.p760}
-፦ እስመ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይወየት ፡ ደዌ ፣ ዘቀ“ስል ።፡ ወደክሙ፣0፣ አበዊሃ ፡ ወኢረከቡ ፡ መነሂ ፡ እምጠቢባን ፡ ዘይፌውሳ ፡፡
2. - The sister of this saint was afflicted with a disease of ulcers. Her parents were tired of finding no one, among the doctors, who could heal her.
፦ ወኮነ ፡ ፳መነኮስ ፡ ጽሙድ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ጐየ ፡ አእመንግሥቱ ፡ ለሉል ያኖስ ፡ ዘክህደ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምልኩ ፡ ለከዋክብት፣፡ወይስግዱ.፡ሎሙ ፡=፡ ወኃደገ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለሉልያኖስ ፡ ወኃደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ውእቱ፣ ደብር ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ለአቡሁ ፡ ለቅዳዱስ፣መርምህሀናም ።፡፡ እስመ ፡አሙ ንቱ ፡ ኢኮኑ ፣ ያመነድቡ ፡ ኢመነሂ ፡ እምሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በውስተ ፡ መንግሥቶ ሙ ወሶበ ፡ ጐየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ጐዩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እምክርስቲያን ፡ ወ ኃደሩ ፣ በቅሩብ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡፡ ወኮነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ጸጋ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይፈውስ ፡ ለድውያን ።፡ ኮኑ ፣ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ድውያን ፣ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዝየኅድር ፡ ውስቴቱ ፣፤ ወይጴሊ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፣ ወይፌውሶሙ ፣ እግዚ
3. - There was a devout monk, called Matthew (Mâtéwos). He had fled the kingdom of Julian (Loulyânos) who had apostatized and had ordered men to worship the stars and to prostrate themselves before them. This saint had abandoned the land of the kingdom of Julian. He lived on the mountain that is near the capital of the kingdom of the father of Saint Marmehnâm. Indeed, no one of the Christian people was persecuted in this kingdom. When Saint Matthew fled, many Christians fled with him and lived near him. The Lord had given Saint Matthew the grace to heal the sick. Many sick people came to find him on the mountain where he lived. He prayed over them and the Lord healed them of their diseases.
አብሔር ፡ አምደዌሆሙ ፡=፡ ፦ ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መርምሀናም ፡ ወፅአ ፡አሐተ፣ ፅለተ ፡ ከመ፡ይንዓው ፡ው ስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዘየኅድር ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፣ ወርእየ ፡ ውስተ ፤፡ ው እቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዓርዌ ፡ ዓቢ?የ ፡ በፍጥረቱ ፡ ወተለዎ ፡ እስከ ሌሊት ፡ ወኢተክህሎ ፡የ አኀ። ፡=፡ ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ምሴተ ፡ ኅደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ፣ም
4. - Saint Marmehnäm went out one day, in order to hunt on the mountain where Saint Matthew lived. He saw on this mountain a beast of a * great corpulence. He pursued it until night, but it was not possible for him to catch it. When it was evening, he stayed on the mountain, he and many men with him. While he himself was sleeping, during the night, there appeared to him


[p. 761] {#po.15.p761}
ስሌሁ ፡፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይነውም ፡ በሌሊት ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ገጽ ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ወአ ንቅሆ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ዘየኀድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸሊ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ እኅቱ ፡ ወይፈውሳ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምደዌሃ ።፡
a luminous personage. This one awakened him and ordered him to go find Saint Matthew who lived on this mountain, so that he might pray over his sister and that the Lord might heal her of her disease.
፦፦ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ የኃትት ፣፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ደብር ፡እስክ ፡ አመ ፡ ረከበ ፡ በዓቶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ራእ የ ፣ በእንቲአሁ ፡= ወገሠጾ ፤ ቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፣ ወመሀሮ ፡ ፍኖተ ፣ ሕይወቱ ። ወሖረ ፡
5. - Immediately he arose. He explored the mountain, until he found the cave of Saint Matthew. He prostrated himself before him and he explained to him how he had had a vision concerning him. Saint Matthew exhorted him and taught him the way of life. He went with him to the city, in secret.
ምስሌሁ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ህገር ፣ በህቡእ ፡፡ -፦ ወቦአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መርምህናም ፡ ኅበ ፡ አሙ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ወነገራ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እም ኔሁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ራእይ ፡ ዘርአየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ያእምር ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ወፅ አ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አፍኣ ፡ሀገር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸሊ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሳራ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ መር ምሀናም ፡ከመ ፡ ይፈውሳ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምደዌሃ ፡፡ ወተፈሥሐት ፡ እሙ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መርምሀናም ፡ ምስለ ፡ እኅቱ ፣ ወሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ወጸ ለየ ፣ ላዕለ ፣ እኅቱ ፣ ወፈወሳ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ በጸሎቱ ።፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ወዓንቅ አ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ነቅዓ ፣ ማይ ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፣ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈ ስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፡ አምሥጢር ፡ መለኮታዊት ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀ
6. - Saint Marmehnâm entered his mother's house, in secret. He explained to her what had happened to him regarding the vision he had had, without his father knowing. As for Saint Matthew, he went out, in secret, outside the city, to pray over Sara, sister of Saint Marmehnâm, so that the Lord might heal her of her disease. His mother rejoiced at this. Saint Marmehnäm went out with his sister. They went to find Saint Matthew. He prayed over the sister of Saint Marmehnâm and the Lord healed her by his prayer. Then he prayed and the Lord made a spring of water gush forth. He baptized them in the name of the Father, of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. He gave them the divine mystery and he went away to his dwelling.

7. - When the king, father of Saint Marmehnäm, knew that his daughter Sara was healed of her disease, he rejoiced with a great joy. He believed that it was by his impure gods. He inquired and said: "How has my daughter * been

healed?" They said to him: "It is Our Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the Lord, who healed her of her disease by the prayer of Saint Abba Matthew. It is not the created stars that one worships." When he had heard this from them, he became angry with them and threatened to torture them with a great torture, if they did not turn away from their opinion. 8. - When it was night, Saint Marmehnâm as well as his sister Sara resolved to go find Saint Abba Matthew on the mountain, in order to receive from him good counsel. Saint Marmehnâm and his sister Sara mounted horses and went away, escorted by many of their companions, in secret, without their father knowing. 9. - When the king learned this, he thought that they themselves had fled to another place, in order to revolt against him. Immediately he ordered many of his soldiers to mount horses, to pursue them, and, when they had found them, to kill them. When they had gone in search of them, they found them outside the city, in the vicinity. They took and killed by the sword, for the name of Our Lord Jesus Christ, Saint Marmehnâm, his sister Sara and all their companions who had remained with them. They received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of the heavens.

10. - When the soldiers of the king had killed them and had returned to him, the spirit of Satan (Saytän) seized the king, father of Marmehnâm and of Sara, overturned him and made him epileptic. The mother of Saint Marmehnâm sent to Saint Abba Matthew and had him brought to her, so that he might pray over the king and that * the Lord might heal him. Saint Abba Matthew came, prayed over the king and the Lord healed him by his prayer. Immediately the king and his wife believed. They became Christians having faith in Our Lord Christ, as well as all the people of the kingdom.

11. - They built a church over the bodies of the saints Marmehnäm and Sara, his sister, their children. There was a great monastery at the place where the saints were killed. Saint Abba Matthew lived there. The Lord manifested through the bodies of the saints miracles and great healings evident to all the people. May their intercession and their blessing be with us and with all the baptized. Amen. Amen.

4. Death of Christodoulos, patriarch of Alexandria. 1. - On this day also died the holy Father Christodoulos (Gabra-Krestos), patriarch of the city of Alexandria ('Eskendryä). He is of the number of the patriarchs the sixty-sixth. 2. - There were in the days of this Father numerous miracles and wonders. Among them is the following: He himself learned about a church, which had been built under the invocation of Our Lady Saint Mary (Märyâm) in the land of 'Endoulès, that a dry olive tree was found near the door of this church, that on the day of the feast of Our Lady Saint Mary this tree budded and gave fruit and that, when the feast was finished, this tree returned to its first state and became dry, as it was before. 3. - This is why this holy patriarch Christodoulos asked the Lord


[p. 765] {#po.15.p765}

to manifest this miracle to him. The very night, the angel of the Lord caught him up and brought him to this church. He saw the dry tree budding, giving * fruit and returning immediately to its previous state. He explained to the people what had happened. They doubted about this.

4. - A few days later, arrived a magistrate of the land of 'Endoulès, called John (Yohannès). The Father patriarch Christodoulos questioned him before all the priests and the people. He said to him: "Is it true, as I have learned, that a dry olive tree, being near the door of the church of Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary in the land of 'Endoulès, has budded and has given fruit on the day of the feast of Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary?"

Mary. I saw with my eyes this tree, while it was dry. When the sun rose, on the day of the feast of Our Lady Mary, the tree budded. All the people saw it flower and give fruit. Its fruits multiplied until noon. There were produced on this tree many fruits, indeed even innumerable. Immediately the steward of the church went out, took some of these fruits, made olive oil from them and lit with this oil the lamp of the church. They prayed, they celebrated the sacrifice of the mass, they received the holy mysteries and they went away each to his country. Then the steward and the priests went out of the church. They gathered all the olives that were on this tree, after the people had taken some as blessed relics. The steward and the priests used the olives that were on this tree for the lamps of the church

6. - When the people had learned of this miracle, they were extremely astonished. They glorified the Lord and gave thanks to Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary. 7. - In the days of this Father, a man, called Cyril (Qoril), went to the land of Ethiopia ('Ityopyä) and made himself a false metropolitan. He sent much money to the king of Egypt (Gebs). He said to him: "Order the patriarch Christodoulos to send a deputation and to institute me metropolitan, while I am in the land of Ethiopia. For me, each year, I will send you, as gifts, gold." The king of Egypt called the patriarch Christodoulos

The patriarch said to him: "May your will be done."

3. - This saint Marmehnâm asked his father to go to the desert, in order to hunt beasts. When he had permitted him, he departed with his forty * servants, horsemen. Having arrived at his mother's, he said to her: "I am going to hunt beasts." His mother said to him: "May the Lord, God of the heavens and of the earth, bless you." Having gone away, he arrived at Mount Magsoub. He remained there.

rise and go up to this mountain. You will find a holy man, called Matthew (Mâtéwos). He himself will speak words of life to you." The next day, when he had climbed the mountain, he found Abba Matthew clothed in hair like a sheep. Having seen him, Marmehnäm was afraid of him. Saint Matthew said to him: "Approach me, O my son, for I too am a man like you, a creature of the Lord." Marmehnäm said to him: "Is there a God other than the gods of my father?" Saint Matthew taught him the entire faith in the Lord up to the birth of Christ, his death, his resurrection, the reward of the righteous, and the punishment of sinners.
ወይቤሎ ፡ መርምህሀናም ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ እኅትየ ፡ ዘአኃዛ ፤ ሕማመ ፡ ለም ጽ ፡ እምርአጳ ፡ እስከ ፡ እግራ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ፈወስካ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ወበስሙ ፡ ለአምላክክ ፡ አነ ፡ አአምን ፡ ቦቱ ፡ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ማቴዎስ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ንረድ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወአነ፤ አእፌውሳ። -=- ወወሪዶ ፡ እምነ ፡ ደብር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መርምሀናም ፡ ኃደጎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡
5. - Marmehnâm said to him: "O Abba, there is my sister whom the evil of leprosy has seized from head to foot. If you heal her by your prayer and by the name of your God, I myself will believe in him." Abba Matthew said to him: "Come, let us go down together. I myself will heal her." 6. - Having descended from the mountain, saint Marmehnäm left Abba

Matthew on the way. Having arrived at his mother's, he explained everything to her. Then he took his sister in secret and led her to Abba Matthew. When he saw them, Abba Matthew made a long prayer to the Lord and struck the earth, * while making the sign of the cross. Water gushed out like the water of a lake. He made them descend into the water. He placed his hand on their heads and baptized them in the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, as well as the forty servants who were with Marmehnâm. He gave them the holy mysteries. Immediately the sister of Marmehnâm was purified of her leprosy. Then he exhorted them and strengthened them in the faith in Our Lord Jesus Christ, so that they might receive every suffering and every blow that would come upon them because of the name of Christ. Then he prayed over them, blessed them, and dismissed them in peace.

7. - Saint Marmehnâm, having arrived in his country, entered his mother's house. He did not go to find his father and did not enter the temple of his gods. Having learned this, his father, the king, became angry. He ordered him to be struck. Saint Marmehnâm departed with his sister and his soldiers. He climbed to the top of the mountain called Qesr and remained there. 8. - Having learned this, his father sent magistrates to him with the

garments of pomp and the royal crown, in order to say to him: "Take my kingdom." Marmehnäm looked at them and grew angry with them. He said to them: "As for me, I seek the kingdom of my Lord Jesus Christ, which does not grow old and does not fade away." Having learned this, his father became very angry. He ordered the servants of Marmehnâm to be killed first, so that his heart would be afraid and change its sentiment. But as his heart was not afraid and did not change its sentiment, he ordered him to be killed, him also with his sister. 9. - Having learned this, saint Marmehnâm prayed and asked the Lord to grant him all his vows. A word came saying: "* I have heard your prayer and I have granted you the desire of your heart." Immediately the soldiers cut off the heads of the saints with the sword. They threw them into a pit. They brought much wood, in order to burn the bodies of the saints with fire. But they did not find them, for the Lord had hidden them. When the soldiers saw that the sun was darkening and that the earth was shaking, they were afraid and fled.

10. - Satan (Saytän) entered into the heart of King 'Ator, who cried out like a pig. 11. - Having learned that her husband had become mad, the mother of saint Marmehnäm sent for Abba Matthew, had him come, and explained the king's affair to him. Immediately Abba Matthew prayed over some oil and anointed 'Ator. Satan went out of him in the form of a pig. The king believed in Our Lord Jesus Christ. Abba Matthew baptized the king, his wife, all the people of his house, and all the people of his royal city.

He did as he had ordered him. 13. - Then the mother of saint Marmehnâm made forty reliquaries of precious stones. She gathered the bones of these martyr soldiers and


[p. 776] {#po.15.p776}
ወወደየቶሙ ፦ለለጴ58 ፡ ወገብረት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ፪ሣፁናተ ፡ ዘማኅው ፡ ለደቂቃ ፡ ወወደየቶሙ፣ ውስቴቶሙ ።፡ ወአንበረቶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘሐነጸት ። "ወአስተርአየ ፡ እአምሥጋሆሙ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ዐበይት ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡-ልቀ፥ ። በረከቶሙ፣ቅድስት፣0ተሀሉ፣ምስለ፣ፍቁሮሙ ፡ዘመንፈስ፣ቅዱስ፣ለዓለመ፣ዓለም። ሰላም ፡ ለመርምህናም ፡ እንተ ፡ ዔለ ፡ በድወ ። ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ይኅሥሥ ፡ አምሳለ ፣ ይፈቅድ ፡ ንዒወ ። ነጺሮሙ ፡ደሞ ፡በእንተ፡፣ሃይማኖት ፡ ተክዕወ። ከመ፡፣ሐራውያ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ነቀወ። ወፀሓይኒ ፡ ብርሃኖ ፡ ሐሰወ ፡=፡
put them in one by one. She also made two glass reliquaries for her children and put them in them. She placed them in the church that she had built. * Miracles and great and innumerable wonders were manifested through their bodies. May their holy blessing be with their dear Za-Manfas-Qedous for ages of ages. Hail to Marmehnäm who traveled through the desert, As if he wanted to hunt, in order to seek the faith in Christ. Seeing that the blood of Marmehnâm was shed for the faith, The king grunted like a pig, And the sun also hid its light. 8. Martyrdom of the seventeen thousand soldiers of King 'Ator, father of Marmehnâm.


[p. 777] {#po.15.p777}
-፦ ወካዕበ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለ፲ወ፪፳ሰማዕታት ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡፤ ንጉ ሥ ፡ አቶር ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለመርምህናም ።
1. - On this day also is the memory of the seventeen thousand martyrs, soldiers of King 'Ator, father of Marmehnâm.
ወዕበ ፡ ነግሠ ፣ ክልእ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ እምድኅረ ፡ ሰናክሬም ፡ ዘአጥመቆ ፡ አባ፣፣ ማቴዎስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ኢያአምሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ከለዳ ውያን፣ሰምዓ ፣ከመ ፡ሞተ ፡ንጉሥ ፡ አቶር፡ ወከመ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ወልዱ፡፣ህየንቴሁ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ ዓቂበ ፡ መንግሥት ። ወኮኑ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መገብት፡ አለ 0፣። ኮኑ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ የዓቅቡ ፣ መንግሥቶ ። ወውእቱ ኮነ ፡ ሕፃነ፥፤ ዘኢያአምር ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ትኤዝዝ ፡ እሙ ፡ ሀየንቴሁ ፡ ወመገብት ሂ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይትቀነዩ ፡ ላቲ።
2. - When another king reigned after Sennacherib (Sanâkerêm) whom Abba Matthew (Mâtéwos) had baptized, he did not know the Lord. He himself, while he was in the land of the Chaldeans (Kaladäwyân), learned that King 'Ator was dead and that his son was reigning in his place. 3. - The latter could not keep the kingdom. He had the prefects who were in the days of his father; they themselves kept his kingdom. He himself was a child who did not know royal power. His mother commanded in his place and the prefects were subject to her.
ወበእንተዝ ፡ አስተጋብአ ፡ ሠራዊቶ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ከመ ፡ይንግሥ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ሀገ ረ ፡ አቶር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ከመ ፡ መጽአ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ይን ግሥ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘይሰግድ ፣ ለጣዖት ፣፡ ወአስተጋብኡ ፣ ሠራዊቶሙ ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ ለተቃት
4. - That is why the king gathered his troops and departed, in order to reign over the city of 'Ator. Then the people of the kingdom learned that a king who worshipped idols was coming to reign over them. They gathered their troops and


[p. 778] {#po.15.p778}
ሎቱ ። ወአግብርተ ፡ ቤቱኒ ፣ ለሰናክሬም ፡ ወአግብርተ ፡ መርምህሀ"ናም ፡ እለ ፡ የዓቅ ቡ፣መቃብርሮ ፡ወፅኡ ፡ አንተ ፡ ባሕቲቶሙ ፡ከመ ፡ ይትቃተሉ። ፦ ወሶበ ፡ ተቃተሉ ፡ ሞኦሙ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ከለዳውያን ፡ ወጐዩ ፡ አእምቅድሜሁ፣ ወአርኃዋ ፡ ለሀገር ፡ ወአኃዞ ፣ ለሕፃን ፡ ወቀተሎ ፡ ወለእሙኒ ፣ ቀተላ ። ወእምዝ ፡ገረሩ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ መንግሥት ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ።
departed to fight against him. The servants of the palace of Sennacherib also and the servants * who guarded the tomb of Marmehnâm departed separately to fight. 5. - When they fought, the king of the Chaldeans defeated them. They fled from before him. He opened the city, took the child, killed him, and killed his mother also. Then the people of the kingdom all submitted to him and he reigned over them.
፦፦ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፣ አስተጋብአሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፣ ሀገረ ፡ አቶር ፣ ወተስአሎሙ ፡፣ በእንተ ፣ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፡፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕነ ፡-ክርስቲያን ። ወአዘዞሙ;፣ ይስግዱ ፡ ለጣዖት ፡ ወዙሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡፣ ሀገር ፣ ዐለዉ ፣ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፡ ወሰገዱ፣
6. - A few days later, he gathered the people of the city of 'Ator and he questioned them about their faith. They said to him: "We are Christians." He ordered them to worship idols. All the people of the city apostatized from their faith and worshipped the idols.
ለጣዖት። -፦- ወመጽኡ ፡ አግብርተ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መርምህናም ፡ ወአግብርተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወይቤ ልዎ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ገሀደ ፣ አግብርቲሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፣ ወዓንቀብተ ፡ መቃብሪሁ ፡
7. - The servants of saint Marmehnâm and the servants of his father came. They said to him: "We are Christians openly, we the servants of Christ and the guardians of the tomb of saint Marmehnâm. Do what you


[p. 779] {#po.15.p779}
ለቅዱስ ፡ መርምህናም ፡ ግበር ፡ ዘትፈቅድ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፣ ኢንክህድ ፣ ሃይማኖተነ ፣ ዘመሀረ ነ ፣ እግዚእነ ። ወተምዕዓ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይቅትልዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ሀ ገር ፣ ወሎሙኒ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣ ቀተልዎሙ ፡፣ ወኮነ ፡ ጐልቆሙ ፡ ፲ወ፲፳ ፣ ወእሙንቱኒ ፡ ተሰምዩ ፡ ማኅበራኒሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ መርምህናም ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡=፡ ሰላም ፡ ሰላም ፡ ዕደወ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ሐራ። ዘጐልቆሙ ፡ ካዕበተ ፡ ፳ራ ። በእንተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘጾሩ ፡ ለክበደ ፡ ዕለት ፣ የዕራ ። ዘመርምህናም ፡ እግዚኦሙ ፡ ዕበ ፣ ነጸሩ መከራ ።
will against us: we will not deny our faith that our master taught us." He became angry with them. He ordered all the people of the city to be killed. Them too, he killed with the sword. Their number was seventeen thousand. They themselves were called the companions of saint Marmehnäm. May their prayer and their blessing be with our king Iyâsou for ages of ages. Hail, hail to the soldiers, men of courage, Whose number is twice twenty, And who for Christ have endured the torment of the burden of the day, When they had looked upon the trial of Marmehnâm, their master, And of Sara who suffered with him.
ወዘምስሌሁ ፡አንተ ፡ ሐመት ፡ ሳራ ። ሰላም ፡ ለክሙ ፡ አዕላፈ ፡ መርምሀናም ፡ ብፁዕ ። ዐሥሩ ፡ ወስብፅ ። ዘተጋደልክሙ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓውደ ፡ ስምዕ ።
Hail to you, seventeen thousand Companions of the blessed Marmehnâm, Who sustained the combat in the arena of martyrdom.


[p. 780] {#po.15.p780}
አዕጥቁኒ ፡ ኃይላቲክሙ ፡ ወአቅንቱሄ ፡ ለፀብዕ ። እስመ ፡ ለፅቡስ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ መድኅኑ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡=፡ ሰላም ፡ ለገብረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በቅብአ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዘተሐትመ ። አበ ፡ ድክቱማን ፣ ይኩን ፡ ወለመዓስባት ፣ ቃውመ ። ከመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በቤተ ፡ ማርያም ፡ መድምመ ። ይቡስ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ዘይት ፡ እስከ ፣ ይፈሪ ፡ ጥፁመ ። ፡ ፪ ዘለለ ፡ በዓላ ፡ ሠሪዖ ፡ ይየብስ ፡ ዳግመ ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ አባ ፡ አሞኔዎስ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆ ይስ ፣ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስና ። ሽ
Gird me with your strength and gird me for war, For the strong is called the savior of the weak. 9. Salâm to Christodulus, patriarch of Alexandria. Hail to Christodulus who was signed with the oil of grace, In order to become the father of orphans and the protector of widows. He says that he had seen a wonder in the church of Mary: A withered olive tree capable of giving sweet fruits, And, after having budded at each feast of Mary, of becoming dry again. 10. Martyrdom of Abba Ammonius, bishop of 'Esnâ. 1. - On this day also is the martyrdom of Abba Ammonius (‘Amonëwos), illustrious bishop of the city of 'Esnâ. 2. - When Arianus ('Aryânos) arrived at the city of 'Esnâ, he found all
፦ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስና ፡ ረከቦሙ ፣ ለዙሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ተጋቢኦሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አባ ፡ አሞኔዎስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትሜሀሩ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ሃይማኖት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ቀተሎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ።
the people of the city gathered near Abba Ammonius, in the process of learning the exposition of the faith. Immediately he killed them all.


[p. 781] {#po.15.p781}
ወለአባ ፡ አሞኔዎስ ፡ አኃዞ ፡ ወአሠሮ ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እንጽናዕ ፡ ወአገ በሮ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕጥን ፣ ለአማልክት ፡ ወይስግድ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፣ አሞኔዎስ ፡ እስመ ፣ ጽሑፍ ፤ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክከ ፡ ትሰግድ ፡ ወኪያሁ ፡ ባሕቲቶ ፡ ታመል ክ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይሠምር ፣ በተዛውዖትክሙ ፡፣ ከንቱ ። ወኢይሠምዕ ፡ነገረክሙ ፡ በከ፣ ወኢይሬሏኢ ፡ አምልኮተክሙ ፡ ርኩስተ ። ወኢያንሶሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ጣዖትክሙ ፡ ምዝቡር ። ወኢይእኅዝ ፡ ዕጣነክሙ ፡ ጽዩዓ ፣ ወ"”መሥዋዕተክሙ ፡ ፍጉገ ። ወዕበ ፡ ር እየ ፡ አርያኖስ ፤ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ኢይፈልስ ፡ ወኢይት
3. - He arrested Abba Ammonius and chained him. He led him to the city of ‘Ensenä’. He forced him strongly to offer incense to the gods and to worship them. Abba Ammonius said to him: "It is written: You shall worship the Lord, your God, and him alone shall you serve. As for me, I will not approve your vain tales; I will not listen to your empty words; I will not see your impure worship; I will not advance into the temple of your ruined idols; I will not take your fetid incense and * your stinking sacrifice." When the judge Arianus saw that the resolution of this saint would not pass, would not be modified, and would not change, he ordered him to be burned alive in the fire.
መየጥ ፡ ወኢይትዌለጥ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ያውዕይዎ ፡ በእሳት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ውእቱ ። ፦ ወሰአሎሙ ፡፣ አባ ፡ አሞኔዎስ ፡ ለሐራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጽንሕዎ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ እስ

he had prayed. He stood upright, while his hands and his feet were chained. He recited the prayer of the Gospel. He blessed his city, his people, and all his people believing in Christ. He prayed for the judge Arianus. He prophesied concerning him that he would become a martyr. Furthermore, he unchained himself.
ወዕበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፣ ጸሎቶ ፣ ወገርዎ ውስተ ፡ አፈ ፡ እሳት ፣ወፈጸመ፣ በድሮ፣ ሠናየ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ጠፍአ ፡ እሳት ፡ ረከቡ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልቦ ፡ዘ ለከፎ ። ወገነዝዎ ፡ በአልባስ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምዕራቢሃ ፡ ለሀገረ ፡ እንጽ ና ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅፈድ ፡ ዘሐነጸት ፡ አክላኡበጥራ ፡ ንግሥት ፣ ወለተ ፡ በጥሊሞስ ፡ ንጉ ሥ። ወገብረ ፡ ኃይላተ ፣ ወተኣምራተ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ኀልቅ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡፡ ሰላም ፣ ለአሞኔዎስ ፣ ለአድያመ ፡ እስና ፡ ዘዖዳ ።
5. - When he had finished his prayer, they threw him into the opening of the fire. He finished his good race. Then when the fire was extinguished, his body was found intact, while nothing had touched it. They shrouded him in beautiful garments. They buried him to the west of the city of 'Ensenâ, in the tower that Queen Cleopatra ('Aklä'oubaträ), daughter of King Ptolemy (Batlimos), had built. He performed innumerable wonders and miracles. May his holy blessing be with our king Iyâsou for ages of ages. Hail to Ammonius who traveled through the territory of 'Esnâ

With the words of good teaching in the midst of his many children. At the moment when he had finished his martyrdom in the flames of the furnace of fire, After the waves of the fire had been silenced and after its floods had calmed, His body was found white as snow.

11. Death of Nasâhit, daughter of the emperor of Constantinople. 1. - * On this day died the daughter of the king of Rome (Romê), called Nasâhit. 2. - Here is what Abba James (Yä'qob), bishop of Wesim, has explained concerning her, saying: "One day when I was sitting in the church of Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary (Märyâm), Mother of God, I heard a monk knocking at the door of the monastery. I rose, in order to see her. I saw on him an old cloak. His body was withered and his face was veiled. I

said to him: "Where do you come from and where are you going?" He said to me: "I come from the convents of Abba Macarius (Magäryos) and I want to spend this night in this monastery, in order to receive the Eucharist." It was then the day of Saturday. I said to him: "I will not open to you, if you do not show me your face." He said to me: "Do not seek the sight of my face, for my face is black like my sins. Indeed, to whoever sees my face all my evil works will appear." When I had refused him, he wanted to return to the desert. I thought in my heart that he was hungry or thirsty. That is why I opened the door for him and I made him enter my dwelling. I brought him food. He refused me and said to me: "I will not eat until tomorrow after the Eucharist."

we approached him, in order to ask him to pray for us. He said to us: "How would I pray for you, since I am black of face and my sins are many?" 4. - When we had offered the Eucharist, he stood upright on his feet. He began to recite the book of the Epistles of Paul (Päwlos), the Epistles of the Apostles, and the Acts. At the fourth hour he stood upright before the altar. He began to recite the Gospel of John (Yohannès). He removed his garment from his face. Indeed, it was not possible to see him because of the greatness of the light that was upon him.

6. There was an old man of Roman family. He was a monk and a virtuous man. He said to me: "O my Father Jacques, this woman is one of the royal daughters. That is why she hides her face from men, so that one may not

ኀቤሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኣምሀላ ፡ በስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትኅብአኒ ፡ ግብራ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ኃ ጣእክዋ ፣ ወኢረከብዋ ። ወእምድ"ኅረዝ ፣ በ፳አውራኅ ፣ መጽኡ ፡ ብዙኃን ፣ ሰብእ ፡ አለ ፡ ተ ፈነዉ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እንዘ ፣ የኃሥ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ዜነዉነ ፡ግ ብራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወፅአት ፡ በሌሊት ፡ እምድረ ፡ሮም ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ኮኖሙ ፡ ፲ወ፪ዓመ ት ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኃሥሥዋ ፣ ወኢረከብዋ ። ወእምዝ ፣ በፈቃደ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዜነዉነ ፡ ፈላስያን ፡ ከመ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀሮ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡፡፡ ሰላም ፡ ለወለተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ነሳሒት ፡፣ ስማ። ከዊና ፣ ግኅሥተ ፡ እምነ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወእማ ። እንበለ ፡ ታጠይቅ ፡ ገጻ ፡ በመካነ ፡ ጽልመት ፣ ቀዊማ ።
recognize her." Then I went to find her, in order to adjure her by the name of Christ not to hide her work from me. Then I did not find her and she was not discovered. 7. Then, at the end of five months, many people who were sent by the king, her father, came to look for her. They themselves recounted her work: she had departed, during the night, from the territory of Rome. Behold, they had been looking for her for twelve years, without finding her. Then, by the will of the Lord, pilgrims announced to us that she had died that day." May the Lord, through her prayer, have mercy on our king 'Iyasu for ever and ever. Hail to the king's daughter, called Nasahit. Having remained far from her father and her mother, without showing her face, while standing in a dark place,

12. The Christ-Light. On this day also Our Savior manifested the splendor of his light, he who came into the world, in order to deliver the prisoners and to push aside the darkness of error by his power. Glory to him. May his holy blessing be with his dear Se'ela-Syon for ever and ever. Hail to the Light that was brilliant in the land of darkness. In order to deliver the prisoners and to trample Sheol underfoot While the Lord, his Father, was preparing the Word, At the beginning of his sentence, as he said expressly, The Light of the divinity came and clothed itself in a body.
ብርሃነ ፡መለኮት ፡ መጽአ ፡ ወለብሰ ፡ አባለ ፡=፡ በላም ፡ለብርሃን ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ዓለም ፣ ዘተዓውቀ ፡=፡ ምስለ ፡ ቃል ፡ ነባቢ ፡ እመ ፡ አምሀላዌሁ ፡ ሠረቀ ።፡
Hail to the Light that appeared before the world With the spiritual Word, since it proceeds from his essence.

On the mountain it shone to the point that Moses (Muse) was terrified and fell. When they looked at its strongly sparkling splendor, The Israelites ('Esra'el) were afraid and stood far off. 13. Death of Arianus, priest, and of his brother Archelides. On this day also died Arianus ('Aryanos), priest, and Archelides ('Arkides), his brother, fearing the Lord, who donned the monastic habits. They were spiritual sons of Gregory (Gorgoryos). Not a single stain was known in their virtue. Arianus was instituted metropolitan. Having repudiated the world, his companion emigrated to the desert.
ሰላም ፡፣ እብል ፡አርያኖስሃ ፡ ቅሲሰ ። ወአርከሌድስ ፡ እሁ ፡ ፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አልባሰ ፡፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ጎርጎርዮስ ፡ እሉ ፡ ዘኢያእመሩ ፡ ደነሰ ፡፡ 8፳በጊሩቱ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ጳጳሰ = ወክልኡ ፡ መኒኖ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡=፡
I say hail to Arianus, priest, And to Archelides ('Arkalides), his brother, having the fear of the Lord, who donned the monastic habits. They were spiritual sons of Gregory. Not a single stain was known In their virtue. Arianus was instituted metropolitan. Having repudiated the world, his companion emigrated to the desert.

(Dertades), king of Armenia, tortured him with a great torture because of his disobedience toward him in the worship of idols. After he had tortured him, he threw him into a dry pit where he remained for fifteen years. The Lord was with him. An old woman brought him his food, every day. No one knew that he was alive.

3. - When the king had killed the virgins Ripsime ('Arsima) and her companions, because he himself wanted to marry Ripsime, the bodies of the virgins were thrown onto the mountains. Seven days after their death, he himself was sad about what he had done against them and especially about Saint Ripsime. That is why his people prayed to him and said to him: "Mount your horse and go hunting * in the countryside, so that the sadness which is in your heart may go away."

4. - When he had mounted his horse and had gone to the countryside with all his soldiers, immediately Satan (Saytan) seized him, made him fall from his horse, bit him in his flesh and bit his people also. The Lord changed his appearance. He became like a wild boar. He prowled in the countryside and bit whomever he found. Likewise, the demons seized many people of the royal palace. There was great terror and cries in the royal palace. All this took place because of the virgin Ripsime and her companions.

illness." One was terrified about this. Indeed, one believed that he himself had been dead for a long time. 6. - Then one went to the pit and threw ropes into the pit, in order to know if he was alive. One shouted to him to hang onto the ropes. One brought him back up, one washed him, one clothed him in new clothes, one made him mount a horse and one made him arrive at the royal palace. He questioned them about the bodies of the holy virgins. One led him and made him arrive at the place where the virgins had been thrown. He found them intact from the beasts of the desert and the birds of the sky. Saint Gregory ordered to make for them a beautiful sanctuary that suited them. He deposited them there in great pomp.

7. - Then the people asked him * to heal the king and to expel from him Satan who had seized him. The saint made the king come and said to him: "Will you return to your evil works?" The king made a sign with his head, as if he were saying: "No." The saint prayed over him and expelled Satan from him. Reason returned to the king and his appearance was as it was before. But the Lord made a little of the nature of the wild boar remain in him, so that he would become humble: these were the nails of his hands and his feet.

8. - Then Saint Gregory healed all the people of the royal palace and expelled the demons from them. Then he ordered that all the people of the land of Armenia gather. He gave them a penance and he ordered them to fast for sixty days. They did as he had ordered them.

10. - They asked Saint Gregory to baptize them with Christian baptism. He said to them: "As for me, I must not do that, because I am not a priest." * They sent messengers to the land of Lunya. They wrote a letter to the emperor Honorius ('Anoryos), to inform him that they themselves had converted to the Lord. They asked him to institute Gregory as their patriarch. Saint Leontius (Lawendeyos) was patriarch in the city of Rome (Rome) in those days. Then they sent many gifts with their messengers. Saint Gregory was with them. When their messengers had reached the emperor and the patriarch and they had read the


[p. 795] {#po.15.p795}
ጉሥ ፡ ወኅበ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ወአንበቡ ፣፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ መልእክቶሙ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ዓቢ የ ፣ ፍሥሓ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ተመይጦቶሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ አርማንያ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡= ወሜመ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ጎርጎርዮስሃ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቂሳርያ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ሎ ሙ. በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ድርጣድስ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ አርማንያ ፡፡
text of their letter, the emperor and the patriarch rejoiced with great joy because of the conversion of the inhabitants of Armenia to the Lord. The patriarch consecrated Gregory as patriarch for them in the city of Caesarea (Qisarya) and sent him to them, in great pomp, to the capital of Tiridates, king of Armenia.

11. - When Saint Gregory arrived in the land of Armenia, the Armenians rejoiced with great joy because of his coming to them. Then he built a beautiful church under the invocation of the holy virgins. All the institutions of their church and their faith were established rightly. Then Saint Gregory finished his course excellently. He died in peace and went toward Christ who loved him. * May his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen.

2. Death of Saint Luke, stylite. On this day also died Saint Luke (Lugas), stylite. May his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen. 3. Martyrdom of Abba Yemsah. On this day also is Saint Abba Yemsah, martyr. May his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen. Amen. II. - Revision or Vulgate. 4. Salam to Saint Gregory, patriarch of Armenia. Hail to Gregory who endured the torment: The night, the cold and the day, the heat, For fifteen years, when he had been thrown into a pit.

Hail to Luke who underwent the pain. He fought and struggled, in a garment of iron, * with the demon (mastema). Regulating the abstinence for his empty stomach, Until the days of his fast which lasted six days were finished, On the day of Sunday he ate only a small bread. 6. Salam to Abba Yemsah. Hail to you, Abba Yemsah, Treasure of blessing and purity. Lead me into the palace of joy by the excellence of your luminous holiness,
እምቃለ ፡ ኃጥኣን ፡ ወአምእለ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ሐተታ ፡ ወፍትሕ ፡፡ ከመ ፡ማየ ፡ አፍላግ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ይደምፅ ፡ ላህ ፡፡ ፲፪. ጅጀዐህጀፀ. ፪ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ዕድወተ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ አቡነ ፣ ኤዎስጣቴዎስ ፡ በአጽፈ ፡ ንዴ ቱ ፡ ክቡር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተራምም ፡ ባሕረ ፡፣ እምተሀውኮ ፡፣ ወያስተናሥእ ፡ አድባረ እምአዕ"ርፎ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለፍቁሩ ፡ ነገደ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡፡ ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ዓዕጻፈከ ፡ ክቡረ ፡=፡ እንተ ፡ ኮነከ ፡ ሐመረ ።፡ ኤዎስጣቴዎስ ፡ ንግደከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብር ፡ መንክረ ፡=፡ እምነ ፡ ተሀውኮ ፡ አስተራመመ ፡ባሕረ ፡=፡ ሽ -ዝ0 ወእምአዕርፎ ፡ አንሥአ ፡ አድባረ ።፡
Far from the words of sinners and of those whose, on the day of examination and judgment, The lamentations will make noise like the numerous water of the rivers. III. - Proper. 7. Crossing of a river by Abba 'Ewostatewos. On this day also is the crossing of a river by our Father 'Ewostatewos on the glorious mantle of his poverty, by calming the river of agitation and by making the mountains come out of * rest. May the Lord have mercy through his prayer on his dear Nagada-Iyasus for ever and ever. I say hail to your glorious mantle. Which became for you a ship, 'Ewostatewos, while it made your journey prodigious. It calmed the river of agitation And it pulled the mountains out of rest.
